1import HRS.Torus.Basic

4/-!
5# The periodic Riesz / logarithmic kernel and the equilibrium operators
6
7Source: HRS periodic main paper.
8* Kernel normalisation `H:eq:kernel` (source lines 425-430):
9  `ĝ(0) = 0`, `ĝ(k) = c_{d,s}(2π|k|)^{s-d} > 0` for `k ≠ 0`, with `c_{d,s}` chosen so
10  that the local singularity is `|x|^{-s}` (`s > 0`) or `-log|x|` (`s = 0`)
11  (`H:eq:intro-kernel`, lines 181-186: `g(x) = |x|^{-s} + O(1)`, resp. `-log|x| + O(1)`,
12  regular part smooth near the origin; "the positive-power kernel is not divided by `s`").
13* Equilibrium operators `H:eq:equilibrium-operators` (lines 922-938):
14  `A_β = β^{-1} I + K_{μ_β}`, `L_β = -ρ_β^{-1} Div(ρ_β ∇)`, `R_{β,μ} = (I + βK_μ)^{-1}` on
15  `L²_0(μ_β)`, with `K_μ f = P_μ[g * (f μ)]` and `P_μ f = f - μ(f)`
16  (`O:eq:negative-cov`, line 5517 and surrounding text).
17* Susceptibility covariance `H:eq:susceptibility` (lines 931-935):
18  `C_β(φ,ψ) = ⟨P_μ φ, R_{β,μ} P_μ ψ⟩_{L²(μ)}`.
19
20## Representation
21
22The Fourier coefficients are not absolutely summable (`∑_{k≠0} |k|^{s-d} = ∞` for
23`0 ≤ s < d`), so the kernel is *defined* pointwise by heat regularisation:
24`g(x) = lim_{ε↓0} ∑_{k≠0} ĝ(k) e^{-ε|k|²} cos(2π k·x)`.  This limit exists for `x ≠ 0`
25and equals the periodic Riesz/log kernel with the stated normalisation; at `x = 0` the
26value is a junk real (`limUnder` of a divergent family), which is never used: all
27formulas (modulated energy, Gibbs weights) use `g` only off the particle diagonal or
28integrated against a measure.  The constant `c_{d,s}` is given by its classical closed
29form (Stein, *Singular integrals*, Ch. V §1, Lemma 1, in the convention
30`∫ |x|^{-s} e^{-2πi x·ξ} dx = π^{s-d/2} Γ((d-s)/2)/Γ(s/2) |ξ|^{s-d}`):
31
32  `c_{d,s} = 2^{d-s} π^{d/2} Γ((d-s)/2) / Γ(s/2)`  (0 < s < d),
33  `c_{d,0} = 2^{d-1} π^{d/2} Γ(d/2)`  (s = 0, from `-log|x| = ∂_s |x|^{-s}|_{s=0}`).
34
35The identification of this constant with the paper's prescription "local singularity
36is `|x|^{-s}` / `-log|x|`" is theorem `KERN-01` (`periodicRiesz_local_singularity`),
37not an assumption; see `docs/ISSUES.md` §K1.
38
39Convolutions `g * μ` are Bochner integrals `∫ g(x - y) dμ(y)` (junk `0` if not
40integrable; integrability is part of the hypotheses where it matters, cf. source
41line 431: "convolutions with a nonintegrable force are interpreted distributionally").
42The inverse `R_{β,μ} = (I + βK_μ)^{-1}` is represented by the *response equation*
43`h + β K_μ h = P_μ ψ`, `μ(h) = 0`; `responseInverse` picks the unique solution.
44-/
45
46noncomputable section
47
48open MeasureTheory Filter Topology
49open scoped ENNReal NNReal BigOperators

51namespace HRS

53/-- The normalising constant `c_{d,s}` of `H:eq:kernel`. -/
54def rieszConst (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : ℝ :=
55  if s = 0 then 2 ^ ((d : ℝ) - 1) * Real.pi ^ ((d : ℝ) / 2) * Real.Gamma ((d : ℝ) / 2)
56  else 2 ^ ((d : ℝ) - s) * Real.pi ^ ((d : ℝ) / 2) * Real.Gamma (((d : ℝ) - s) / 2) / Real.Gamma (s / 2)
57
58open Classical in
59/-- `ĝ(k)`: `0` at `k = 0`, `c_{d,s}(2π|k|)^{s-d}` otherwise (source lines 425-430). -/
60def rieszCoeff (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (k : Lattice d) : ℝ :=
61  if k = 0 then 0 else rieszConst d s * (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (s - (d : ℝ))
62
63/-- Heat-regularised partial kernel `g_ε(x) = ∑_{k} ĝ(k) e^{-ε|k|²} cos(2πk·x)`. -/
64def heatRiesz (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (ε : ℝ) (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
65  ∑' k : Lattice d, rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x
66
67/-- The periodic Riesz (`0 < s < d`) / logarithmic (`s = 0`) kernel `g` of `H:eq:kernel`,
68as the `ε ↓ 0` limit of the heat-regularised kernel. -/
69def periodicRiesz (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
70  limUnder (𝓝[>] (0 : ℝ)) fun ε => heatRiesz d s ε x
71
72/-- Heat-regularised gradient `∇g_ε(x) = -∑_k ĝ(k) e^{-ε|k|²} 2π k sin(2πk·x)`. -/
73def heatRieszGrad (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (ε : ℝ) (x : Torus d) : Euc d :=
74  ∑' k : Lattice d,
75    (-(rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 2 * Real.pi * Torus.sinMode k x)) •
76      Lattice.toEuc k
77
78/-- The force `∇g` (pointwise limit of the regularised gradients; junk at `x = 0`). -/
79def periodicRieszGrad (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (x : Torus d) : Euc d :=
80  limUnder (𝓝[>] (0 : ℝ)) fun ε => heatRieszGrad d s ε x
81
82/-- `g_+ := max(g, 0)` (used in the equilibrium estimates, Lemma 4.5). -/
83def rieszPos (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (x : Torus d) : ℝ := max (periodicRiesz d s x) 0
84
85/-- Convolution `g * μ (x) = ∫ g(x - y) dμ(y)`. -/
86def convMeasure {d : ℕ} (g : Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
87  ∫ y, g (x - y) ∂μ
88
89/-- Convolution with a function against `dx`: `g * f (x) = ∫ g(x - y) f(y) dy`. -/
90def convFun {d : ℕ} (g : Torus d → ℝ) (f : Torus d → ℝ) (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
91  ∫ y, g (x - y) * f y
92
93/-- Vector convolution `∇g * μ (x) = ∫ ∇g(x - y) dμ(y)`. -/
94def convGradMeasure {d : ℕ} (G : Torus d → Euc d) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (x : Torus d) : Euc d :=
95  ∫ y, G (x - y) ∂μ
96
97/-- Energy pairing `⟨g * ν, ν'⟩ = ∬ g(x-y) dν(x) dν'(y)` (Bochner double integral). -/
98def energyPairing {d : ℕ} (g : Torus d → ℝ) (ν ν' : Measure (Torus d)) : ℝ :=
99  ∫ x, ∫ y, g (x - y) ∂ν' ∂ν
100
101/-- `K_μ f = P_μ[g * (f μ)]` (source line 5517 and text after it). -/
102def Kop {d : ℕ} (g : Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (f : Torus d → ℝ) : Torus d → ℝ :=
103  projMean μ fun x => ∫ y, g (x - y) * f y ∂μ
104
105/-- The response equation defining `h = R_{β,μ} P_μ ψ = (I + βK_μ)^{-1} P_μ ψ` on `L²_0(μ)`:
106`h + β K_μ h = P_μ ψ` and `μ(h) = 0` (`H:eq:equilibrium-operators`, line 924). -/
107def IsResponse {d : ℕ} (g : Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (β : ℝ)
108    (ψ h : Torus d → ℝ) : Prop :=
109  MemLp h 2 μ ∧ pair μ h = 0 ∧ ∀ᵐ x ∂μ, h x + β * Kop g μ h x = projMean μ ψ x
110
111open Classical in
112/-- `R_{β,μ} P_μ ψ`: the solution of the response equation (`0` if it does not exist). -/
113def responseInverse {d : ℕ} (g : Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (β : ℝ)
114    (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) : Torus d → ℝ :=
115  if h : ∃ h, IsResponse g μ β ψ h then h.choose else 0
116
117/-- The susceptibility covariance form `C_{β,μ}(φ,ψ) = ⟨P_μ φ, R_{β,μ} P_μ ψ⟩_{L²(μ)}`
118(`H:eq:susceptibility`, lines 931-935; `O:eq:negative-cov`, line 5517). -/
119def susceptibilityCov {d : ℕ} (g : Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (β : ℝ)
120    (φ ψ : Torus d → ℝ) : ℝ :=
121  l2Inner μ (projMean μ φ) (responseInverse g μ β ψ)
122
123/-- `A_β = β^{-1} I + K_{μ_β}` acting on functions. -/
124def Aop {d : ℕ} (g : Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (β : ℝ) (f : Torus d → ℝ) :
125    Torus d → ℝ :=
126  fun x => β⁻¹ * f x + Kop g μ f x
127
128/-- `L_β f = -ρ^{-1} Div(ρ ∇f)` for `μ = ρ dx`. -/
129def Lop {d : ℕ} (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (f : Torus d → ℝ) : Torus d → ℝ :=
130  fun x => -(ρ x)⁻¹ * Torus.div (fun y => ρ y • Torus.grad f y) x
131
132/-- The quadratic form `B(u,v) = ⟨K u, v⟩` of a Fourier multiplier with symbol `𝔪`
133(Lemma C.3, `M:B:lem:quadratic-toolbox`). -/
134def multiplierForm {d : ℕ} (𝔪 : Lattice d → ℝ) (u v : Torus d → ℝ) : ℝ :=
135  ∑' k : NonzeroLattice d, 𝔪 k.1 * Torus.funRealCoeff u k * Torus.funRealCoeff v k
136
137/-! ### Facts about the kernel (work package KERN-01) -/

139section KernelHelpers
140
141/-- Product of summable nonnegative functions over `Fin n → ℤ`. -/
142theorem pi_prod_summable {f : ℤ → ℝ} (hf : Summable f) (h0 : 0 ≤ f) :
143    ∀ n : ℕ, Summable fun k : Fin n → ℤ => ∏ i, f (k i)
144  | 0 => by
145    simpa using (summable_of_hasFiniteSupport (f := fun _ : Fin 0 → ℤ => (1:ℝ))
146      (Set.toFinite _))
147  | n + 1 => by
148    have ih := pi_prod_summable hf h0 n
149    have h := hf.mul_of_nonneg ih h0 (fun k => Finset.prod_nonneg fun i _ => h0 _)
150    rw [← (Fin.consEquiv (fun _ : Fin (n+1) => ℤ)).summable_iff]
151    refine h.congr fun p => ?_
152    simp [Fin.consEquiv, Fin.prod_univ_succ]
153
154theorem summable_exp_sq (ε : ℝ) (hε : 0 < ε) :
155    Summable fun m : ℤ => Real.exp (-ε * (m:ℝ)^2) := by
156  have hN : Summable fun n : ℕ => Real.exp (-ε * (n:ℝ)^2) := by
157    refine (Real.summable_exp_nat_mul_iff.mpr (neg_lt_zero.mpr hε)).of_nonneg_of_le
158      (fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le) fun n => ?_
159    apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
160    have : (n:ℝ) ≤ (n:ℝ)^2 := by
161      rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos n with h | h
162      · simp [h]
163      · have : (1:ℝ) ≤ n := by exact_mod_cast h
164        nlinarith
165    nlinarith
166  exact Summable.of_nat_of_neg hN (by simpa using hN)
167
168theorem enorm_sq {d : ℕ} (k : Lattice d) : Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 = ∑ i, ((k i : ℤ) : ℝ)^2 := by
169  simp [Lattice.enorm, Lattice.toEuc, EuclideanSpace.norm_sq_eq]
170
171theorem enorm_neg {d : ℕ} (k : Lattice d) : Lattice.enorm (-k) = Lattice.enorm k := by
172  have h : Lattice.toEuc (-k) = -Lattice.toEuc k := by
173    ext i; simp [Lattice.toEuc]
174  simp [Lattice.enorm, h]
175
176theorem one_le_enorm {d : ℕ} (k : Lattice d) (hk : k ≠ 0) : 1 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := by
177  have : ∃ i, k i ≠ 0 := by
178    by_contra h
179    exact hk (funext fun i => not_not.mp (not_exists.mp h i))
180  obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := this
181  have h1 : (1:ℝ) ≤ ((k i : ℤ):ℝ)^2 := by
182    have : (1:ℤ) ≤ (k i)^2 := by nlinarith [sq_pos_of_ne_zero hi, Int.one_le_abs hi, sq_abs (k i)]
183    exact_mod_cast this
184  have h2 : ((k i:ℤ):ℝ)^2 ≤ Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 := by
185    rw [enorm_sq]
186    exact Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun j => ((k j : ℤ):ℝ)^2) (fun j _ => sq_nonneg _)
187      (Finset.mem_univ i)
188  nlinarith [show 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k from norm_nonneg _]
189
190theorem summable_gauss_lattice (d : ℕ) (ε : ℝ) (hε : 0 < ε) :
191    Summable fun k : Lattice d => Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
192  have := pi_prod_summable (summable_exp_sq ε hε) (fun m => (Real.exp_pos _).le) d
193  refine this.congr fun k => ?_
194  rw [enorm_sq, Finset.mul_sum, Real.exp_sum]
195
196theorem norm_mode {d : ℕ} (k : Lattice d) (x : Torus d) : ‖Torus.mode k x‖ = 1 := by
197  simp [Torus.mode, UnitAddTorus.mFourier, norm_prod]
198
199theorem abs_cosMode_le {d : ℕ} (k : Lattice d) (x : Torus d) : |Torus.cosMode k x| ≤ 1 :=
200  (Complex.abs_re_le_norm _).trans (norm_mode k x).le
201
202theorem cosMode_neg {d : ℕ} (k : Lattice d) (x : Torus d) :
203    Torus.cosMode k (-x) = Torus.cosMode k x := by
204  simp only [Torus.cosMode, Torus.mode, UnitAddTorus.mFourier, ContinuousMap.coe_mk,
205    Pi.neg_apply]
206  simp [← map_prod]
207
208theorem rieszConst_pos {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
209    0 < rieszConst d s := by
210  have hd' : (0:ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (by omega : 0 < d)
211  unfold rieszConst
212  split_ifs with h
213  · exact mul_pos (mul_pos (by positivity) (by positivity)) (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by positivity))
214  · have hs : 0 < s := lt_of_le_of_ne hs0 (Ne.symm h)
215    exact div_pos (mul_pos (mul_pos (by positivity) (by positivity))
216      (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by linarith))) (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by linarith))
217
218end KernelHelpers

225theorem theta_eq (u y : ℝ) (hu : 0 < u) :
226    ∑' n : ℤ, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * y - u * (n:ℂ)^2) =
227      ((Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u)) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
228  have hπ : (π:ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast Real.pi_ne_zero
229  have hu' : (u:ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hu.ne'
230  have h1 : (∑' n : ℤ, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * y - u * (n:ℂ)^2)) = jacobiTheta₂ (y:ℂ) (I * u / π) := by
231    unfold jacobiTheta₂ jacobiTheta₂_term
232    refine tsum_congr fun n => ?_
233    congr 1
234    field_simp
235    ring_nf
236    simp [Complex.I_sq]
237    ring
238  rw [h1, jacobiTheta₂_functional_equation]
239  unfold jacobiTheta₂ jacobiTheta₂_term
240  have h2 : (-I * (I * u / π) : ℂ) = ((u / π : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
241    push_cast; field_simp; simp [Complex.I_sq]
242  have h3 : (((u / π : ℝ) : ℂ)) ^ (1/2 : ℂ) = ((Real.sqrt (u / π) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
243    rw [show (1/2:ℂ) = ((1/2:ℝ):ℂ) by push_cast; rfl, ← Complex.ofReal_cpow (by positivity),
244      Real.sqrt_eq_rpow]
245  rw [h2, h3]
246  have hs : Real.sqrt (u / π) ≠ 0 := (Real.sqrt_pos.mpr (by positivity)).ne'
247  have hs' : (1:ℂ) / ((Real.sqrt (u / π) : ℝ) : ℂ) = ((Real.sqrt (π / u) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
248    have : Real.sqrt (π / u) = 1 / Real.sqrt (u / π) := by
249      rw [one_div, ← Real.sqrt_inv, inv_div]
250    rw [this]; push_cast; rfl
251  rw [hs', Complex.ofReal_mul, Complex.ofReal_tsum, mul_assoc, ← tsum_mul_left]
252  congr 1
253  refine tsum_congr fun n => ?_
254  rw [← Complex.exp_add]
255  push_cast
256  congr 1
257  field_simp
258  ring_nf
259
260theorem pi_hasSum : ∀ (n : ℕ) (f : Fin n → ℤ → ℂ), (∀ i, Summable fun m => ‖f i m‖) →
261    Summable (fun k : Fin n → ℤ => ‖∏ i, f i (k i)‖) ∧
262      HasSum (fun k : Fin n → ℤ => ∏ i, f i (k i)) (∏ i, ∑' m, f i m)
263  | 0, f, hf => by
264    simp only [Finset.univ_eq_empty, Finset.prod_empty]
265    exact ⟨by simpa using (summable_of_hasFiniteSupport (f := fun _ : Fin 0 → ℤ => (1:ℝ)) (Set.toFinite _)),
266      by simp⟩
267  | n + 1, f, hf => by
268    obtain ⟨ih1, ih2⟩ := pi_hasSum n (fun i => f i.succ) (fun i => hf _)
269    have hs0 : Summable fun m => f 0 m := (hf 0).of_norm
270    have hsum : Summable fun p : ℤ × (Fin n → ℤ) => ‖f 0 p.1 * ∏ i : Fin n, f i.succ (p.2 i)‖ := by
271      refine (Summable.mul_norm (hf 0) ih1).congr fun p => ?_
272      simp
273    have hmul := hs0.hasSum.mul ih2 hsum.of_norm
274    have e := (Fin.consEquiv (fun _ : Fin (n+1) => ℤ))
275    constructor
276    · rw [← (Fin.consEquiv (fun _ : Fin (n+1) => ℤ)).summable_iff]
277      refine hsum.congr fun p => ?_
278      simp [Fin.consEquiv, Fin.prod_univ_succ]
279    · rw [← (Fin.consEquiv (fun _ : Fin (n+1) => ℤ)).hasSum_iff, Fin.prod_univ_succ]
280      convert hmul using 2 with p
281      simp [Fin.consEquiv, Fin.prod_univ_succ]
282
283theorem sq_sub_ge (y : ℝ) (hy : |y| ≤ 1/2) (n : ℤ) : (n:ℝ)^2 / 4 ≤ ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 := by
284  rcases eq_or_ne n 0 with h | h
285  · simp [h, sq_nonneg]
286  · have h1 : (1:ℝ) ≤ |(n:ℝ)| := by
287      have := Int.one_le_abs h
288      exact_mod_cast this
289    have h2 : |(n:ℝ)| - |y| ≤ |(n:ℝ) - y| := by
290      have := abs_sub_abs_le_abs_sub (n:ℝ) y
291      exact this
292    have h3 : |(n:ℝ)| / 2 ≤ |(n:ℝ) - y| := by linarith
293    have h4 : ((n:ℝ))^2 / 4 = (|(n:ℝ)| / 2)^2 := by rw [div_pow, sq_abs]; norm_num
294    rw [h4, ← sq_abs ((n:ℝ) - y)]
295    exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) h3 2
296
297theorem theta_bound (u y δ : ℝ) (hu : 0 < u) (hu1 : u ≤ 1) (hy : |y| ≤ 1/2)
298    (hδ : ∀ n : ℤ, δ^2 ≤ ((n:ℝ) - y)^2) :
299    ‖∑' n : ℤ, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * y - u * (n:ℂ)^2)‖ ≤
300      Real.sqrt (π / u) * (∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2)) *
301        Real.exp (-(π^2 * δ^2 / (2 * u))) := by
302  rw [theta_eq u y hu, Complex.norm_real, Real.norm_eq_abs]
303  have hB : Summable fun n : ℤ => Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2) :=
304    summable_exp_sq _ (by positivity)
305  have hterm : ∀ n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u)) ≤
306      Real.exp (-(π^2 * δ^2 / (2 * u))) * Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2) := by
307    intro n
308    rw [← Real.exp_add]
309    apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
310    have h1 := sq_sub_ge y hy n
311    have h2 := hδ n
312    have hπ : 0 < π^2 := by positivity
313    have : π^2 * δ^2 / (2*u) + π^2/8 * (n:ℝ)^2 ≤ π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u := by
314      have e1 : π^2 * δ^2 / (2*u) ≤ π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / (2*u) := by gcongr
315      have e2 : π^2 * ((n:ℝ)^2/4) / (2*u) ≤ π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / (2*u) := by gcongr
316      have e3 : π^2 / 8 * (n:ℝ)^2 ≤ π^2 * ((n:ℝ)^2/4) / (2*u) := by
317        rw [le_div_iff₀ (by positivity)]
318        have : 0 ≤ π^2 * (n:ℝ)^2 := by positivity
319        nlinarith
320      have e4 : π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u = π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / (2*u) + π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / (2*u) := by
321        field_simp; ring
322      linarith
323    linarith
324  have hsum : Summable fun n : ℤ => Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u)) :=
325    (hB.mul_left (Real.exp (-(π^2 * δ^2 / (2 * u))))).of_nonneg_of_le
326      (fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le) hterm
327  have hT : ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u)) ≤
328      Real.exp (-(π^2 * δ^2 / (2 * u))) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2) := by
329    rw [← tsum_mul_left]
330    exact hsum.tsum_le_tsum hterm (hB.mul_left _)
331  have h0 : 0 ≤ ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u)) := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
332  rw [abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
333  calc Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u))
334      ≤ Real.sqrt (π / u) * (Real.exp (-(π^2 * δ^2 / (2 * u))) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2)) := by
335        gcongr
336    _ = _ := by ring
337theorem exists_rep (z : UnitAddCircle) : ∃ y : ℝ, |y| ≤ 1/2 ∧ z = ((y:ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) := by
338  obtain ⟨w, rfl⟩ := QuotientAddGroup.mk_surjective z
339  refine ⟨w - round w, abs_sub_round w, ?_⟩
340  rw [AddCircle.coe_sub]
341  have : ((round w : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = 0 := by
342    rw [AddCircle.coe_eq_zero_iff]
343    exact ⟨round w, by simp⟩
344  rw [this, sub_zero]
345
346theorem pow_exp_bound (c : ℝ) (hc : 0 < c) (d : ℕ) (v : ℝ) (hv : 0 ≤ v) :
347    v^d * Real.exp (-(c*v)) ≤ (d.factorial : ℝ) / c^d := by
348  have h := Real.pow_div_factorial_le_exp (c*v) (by positivity) d
349  have hf : (0:ℝ) < d.factorial := by exact_mod_cast Nat.factorial_pos d
350  rw [div_le_iff₀ hf] at h
351  rw [le_div_iff₀ (by positivity), Real.exp_neg]
352  have : v^d * c^d = (c*v)^d := by ring
353  rw [mul_assoc, mul_comm (Real.exp (c*v))⁻¹, ← mul_assoc, this, mul_inv_le_iff₀ (Real.exp_pos _)]
354  linarith [mul_comm (Real.exp (c*v)) (d.factorial : ℝ)]
355
356theorem mode_eq_prod {d : ℕ} (k : Lattice d) (y : Fin d → ℝ) :
357    Torus.mode k (fun i => ((y i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle)) =
358      ∏ i, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * (k i : ℤ) * (y i : ℝ)) := by
359  simp only [Torus.mode, UnitAddTorus.mFourier, ContinuousMap.coe_mk]
360  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
361  rw [fourier_coe_apply]
362  simp
363
364theorem theta_dim_bound {d : ℕ} (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
365    ∃ M : ℝ, ∀ u : ℝ, 0 < u → u ≤ 1 →
366      |∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x| ≤ M := by
367  choose y hy1 hy2 using fun i => exists_rep (x i)
368  have hxy : x = fun i => ((y i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) := funext hy2
369  obtain ⟨i0, hi0⟩ : ∃ i0, y i0 ≠ 0 := by
370    by_contra h
371    push Not at h
372    apply hx
373    rw [hxy]
374    funext i
375    simp [h i]
376  set B := ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2) with hBdef
377  have hB0 : 0 ≤ B := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
378  set c := π^2 * |y i0|^2 / 2 with hc
379  have hcpos : 0 < c := by
380    have : 0 < |y i0| := abs_pos.mpr hi0
381    positivity
382  refine ⟨(π * B)^d * ((d.factorial : ℝ) / c^d), fun u hu hu1 => ?_⟩
383  -- the complex terms
384  set S : Fin d → ℂ := fun i => ∑' n : ℤ, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * (y i) - u * (n:ℂ)^2) with hS
385  set f : Fin d → ℤ → ℂ := fun i n => Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * (y i) - u * (n:ℂ)^2) with hf
386  have hfn : ∀ i, Summable fun m => ‖f i m‖ := by
387    intro i
388    refine (summable_exp_sq u hu).congr fun m => ?_
389    simp only [hf]
390    rw [Complex.norm_exp]
391    congr 1
392    rw [show ((m:ℂ)) = ((m:ℝ):ℂ) by simp]
393    simp [pow_two, Complex.mul_re]
394  obtain ⟨hsum, hhas⟩ := pi_hasSum d f hfn
395  have hterm : ∀ k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x =
396      (∏ i, f i (k i)).re := by
397    intro k
398    have h1 : ∏ i, f i (k i) = ((Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ) * Torus.mode k x := by
399      rw [hxy, mode_eq_prod]
400      have h2 : ∏ i, f i (k i) = (∏ i, Complex.exp (-((u:ℂ) * ((k i : ℤ):ℂ)^2))) *
401          ∏ i, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * (k i : ℤ) * (y i : ℝ)) := by
402        rw [← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
403        refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
404        simp only [hf]
405        rw [← Complex.exp_add]
406        congr 1
407        ring
408      rw [h2, ← Complex.exp_sum, Complex.ofReal_exp, enorm_sq]
409      congr 2
410      push_cast
411      rw [neg_mul, Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
412    rw [h1, Complex.re_ofReal_mul]
413    rfl
414  have hδ : ∀ i (n : ℤ), |y i|^2 ≤ ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2 := by
415    intro i n
416    rcases eq_or_ne n 0 with h | h
417    · simp [h]
418    · have h1 : (1:ℝ) ≤ |(n:ℝ)| := by
419        have := Int.one_le_abs h
420        exact_mod_cast this
421      have h2 : |(n:ℝ)| - |y i| ≤ |(n:ℝ) - y i| := abs_sub_abs_le_abs_sub (n:ℝ) (y i)
422      have h3 := hy1 i
423      have h4 : |y i| ≤ |(n:ℝ) - y i| := by linarith
424      rw [← sq_abs ((n:ℝ) - y i)]
425      exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) h4 2
426  have hre : ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x =
427      (∏ i, S i).re := by
428    simp_rw [hterm]
429    rw [← Complex.re_tsum hsum.of_norm, hhas.tsum_eq]
430  rw [hre]
431  refine (Complex.abs_re_le_norm _).trans ?_
432  rw [norm_prod]
433  have hbd : ∀ i, ‖S i‖ ≤ Real.sqrt (π / u) * B * Real.exp (-(π^2 * |y i|^2 / (2 * u))) :=
434    fun i => theta_bound u (y i) |y i| hu hu1 (hy1 i) (hδ i)
435  refine (Finset.prod_le_prod₀ (fun i _ => norm_nonneg _) (fun i _ => hbd i)).trans ?_
436  rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, ← Real.exp_sum]
437  have hsq : Real.sqrt (π / u) * B ≤ π * B * (1 / u) := by
438    have h1 : 1 ≤ π / u := by
439      rw [le_div_iff₀ hu]; linarith [Real.pi_gt_three]
440    have h2 : Real.sqrt (π / u) ≤ π / u := Real.sqrt_le_iff.mpr ⟨by linarith, by nlinarith⟩
441    calc Real.sqrt (π / u) * B ≤ (π / u) * B := by gcongr
442      _ = _ := by ring
443  have hexp : Real.exp (-∑ i, π^2 * |y i|^2 / (2 * u)) ≤ Real.exp (-(c * (1 / u))) := by
444    apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
445    have : π^2 * |y i0|^2 / (2 * u) ≤ ∑ i, π^2 * |y i|^2 / (2 * u) :=
446      Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun i => π^2 * |y i|^2 / (2 * u)) (fun i _ => by positivity)
447        (Finset.mem_univ i0)
448    have e : c * (1 / u) = π^2 * |y i0|^2 / (2 * u) := by rw [hc]; field_simp
449    rw [e]; linarith
450  have hexp' : Real.exp (∑ i, -(π^2 * |y i|^2 / (2 * u))) ≤ Real.exp (-(c * (1 / u))) := by
451    rw [Finset.sum_neg_distrib]; exact hexp
452  have h0 : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt (π / u) * B := mul_nonneg (Real.sqrt_nonneg _) hB0
453  calc (Real.sqrt (π / u) * B)^d * Real.exp (∑ i, -(π^2 * |y i|^2 / (2 * u)))
454      ≤ (π * B * (1 / u))^d * Real.exp (-(c * (1 / u))) :=
455        mul_le_mul (pow_le_pow_left₀ h0 hsq d) hexp' (Real.exp_pos _).le (by positivity)
456    _ = (π * B)^d * ((1 / u)^d * Real.exp (-(c * (1 / u)))) := by rw [mul_pow]; ring
457    _ ≤ (π * B)^d * ((d.factorial : ℝ) / c^d) := by
458        gcongr
459        exact pow_exp_bound c hcpos d (1 / u) (by positivity)
460
461theorem single_k_integral {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) (ε : ℝ) (x : Torus d) (k : Lattice d)
462    (hk : k ≠ 0) :
463    ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) *
464        (Real.exp (-((t + ε) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * Torus.cosMode k x) =
465      Torus.cosMode k x * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
466        (Lattice.enorm k ^ (s - d) * Real.Gamma (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2)) := by
467  have ha : 0 < ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 := by linarith
468  have hm : 0 < Lattice.enorm k := lt_of_lt_of_le one_pos (one_le_enorm k hk)
469  have hr : 0 < Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 := by positivity
470  have h1 := Real.integral_rpow_mul_exp_neg_mul_Ioi ha hr
471  have h2 : ∀ t : ℝ, t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) *
472        (Real.exp (-((t + ε) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * Torus.cosMode k x) =
473      (Torus.cosMode k x * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) *
474        (t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * t))) := by
475    intro t
476    have : Real.exp (-((t + ε) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) =
477        Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * t)) := by
478      rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
479    rw [this]; ring
480  simp_rw [h2]
481  rw [integral_const_mul, h1]
482  congr 2
483  rw [one_div, Real.inv_rpow (by positivity), ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hm.le]
484  rw [← Real.rpow_neg hm.le]
485  congr 1
486  push_cast; ring
487
488/-- `Ψ(u) = ∑_{k≠0} e^{-u|k|²} cos(2πk·x)`. -/
489def psiFn {d : ℕ} (x : Torus d) (u : ℝ) : ℝ :=
490  ∑' k : Lattice d, if k = 0 then 0 else Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x
491
492theorem psi_summable {d : ℕ} (x : Torus d) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) :
493    Summable fun k : Lattice d => if k = 0 then 0 else Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x := by
494  refine Summable.of_norm_bounded (summable_gauss_lattice d u hu) fun k => ?_
495  by_cases hk : k = 0
496  · simp [hk, (Real.exp_pos _).le]
497  · simp only [hk, ite_false, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
498    calc _ ≤ Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 1 := by gcongr; exact abs_cosMode_le k x
499      _ = _ := mul_one _
500
501/-- the integrand for fixed `k` -/
502def gInt {d : ℕ} (a ε : ℝ) (x : Torus d) (k : Lattice d) (t : ℝ) : ℝ :=
503  t ^ (a - 1) * (if k = 0 then 0 else Real.exp (-(t + ε) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x)
504
505theorem gInt_bound {d : ℕ} (a ε : ℝ) (x : Torus d) (k : Lattice d) (hk : k ≠ 0) (t : ℝ) (ht : 0 < t) :
506    ‖gInt a ε x k t‖ ≤ Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
507      (t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * t))) := by
508  unfold gInt
509  simp only [hk, ite_false, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _), abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht _)]
510  have : Real.exp (-(t + ε) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) =
511        Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * t)) := by
512    rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
513  rw [this]
514  have hc := abs_cosMode_le k x
515  have hp : 0 ≤ t ^ (a - 1) := (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht _).le
516  calc t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * t)) * |Torus.cosMode k x|)
517      ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * t)) * 1) := by gcongr
518    _ = _ := by ring
519
520theorem integrable_majorant {d : ℕ} {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (k : Lattice d) (hk : k ≠ 0) :
521    IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * t))) (Ioi 0) := by
522  have hm : 0 < Lattice.enorm k := lt_of_lt_of_le one_pos (one_le_enorm k hk)
523  have := integrableOn_rpow_mul_exp_neg_mul_rpow (p := 1) (s := a - 1) (b := Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)
524    (by linarith) one_pos (by positivity)
525  refine this.congr_fun (fun t _ => ?_) measurableSet_Ioi
526  simp only [Real.rpow_one]
527  ring_nf
528
529theorem gInt_integrable {d : ℕ} {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (ε : ℝ) (x : Torus d) (k : Lattice d) :
530    IntegrableOn (gInt a ε x k) (Ioi 0) := by
531  by_cases hk : k = 0
532  · have : gInt a ε x k = 0 := by funext t; simp [gInt, hk]
533    rw [this]; exact integrableOn_zero
534  · refine Integrable.mono' ((integrable_majorant ha k hk).const_mul (Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)))
535      ?_ ?_
536    · refine Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
537      unfold gInt
538      simp only [hk, ite_false]
539      fun_prop
540    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
541      exact gInt_bound a ε x k hk t ht
542
543theorem gInt_norm_summable {d : ℕ} {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (x : Torus d) :
544    Summable fun k : Lattice d => ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), ‖gInt a ε x k t‖ := by
545  refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => integral_nonneg fun t => norm_nonneg _)
546    (fun k => ?_) ((summable_gauss_lattice d ε hε).mul_left (Real.Gamma a))
547  by_cases hk : k = 0
548  · have : ∀ t, ‖gInt a ε x k t‖ = 0 := fun t => by simp [gInt, hk]
549    simp only [this, integral_zero]
550    exact mul_nonneg (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos ha).le (Real.exp_pos _).le
551  · have hm : 0 < Lattice.enorm k := lt_of_lt_of_le one_pos (one_le_enorm k hk)
552    have hr : 0 < Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 := by positivity
553    have hint := (integrable_majorant ha k hk).const_mul (Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2))
554    calc ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), ‖gInt a ε x k t‖
555        ≤ ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
556            (t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-(Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 * t))) := by
557          refine setIntegral_mono_on (gInt_integrable ha ε x k).norm hint measurableSet_Ioi ?_
558          intro t ht
559          exact gInt_bound a ε x k hk t ht
560      _ = Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * ((1 / Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) ^ a * Real.Gamma a) := by
561          rw [integral_const_mul, Real.integral_rpow_mul_exp_neg_mul_Ioi ha hr]
562      _ ≤ Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (1 * Real.Gamma a) := by
563          have h1 : (1 / Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) ^ a ≤ 1 := by
564            apply Real.rpow_le_one (by positivity) _ ha.le
565            have := one_le_enorm k hk
566            rw [div_le_one hr]; nlinarith
567          have := (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos ha).le
568          gcongr
569      _ = _ := by ring
570
571theorem heatRiesz_eq_integral {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (x : Torus d) :
572    heatRiesz d s ε x = rieszConst d s * (2 * π) ^ (s - d) / Real.Gamma (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2) *
573      ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn x (t + ε) := by
574  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
575  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
576  have hG : 0 < Real.Gamma a := Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos ha
577  set A := rieszConst d s * (2 * π) ^ (s - d) / Real.Gamma a with hA
578  have hterm : ∀ k : Lattice d, rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x =
579      A * ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), gInt a ε x k t := by
580    intro k
581    by_cases hk : k = 0
582    · have : gInt a ε x k = fun _ => 0 := by funext t; simp [gInt, hk]
583      rw [this]; simp [rieszCoeff, hk]
584    · have h := single_k_integral (x := x) hsd ε k hk
585      have : (fun t => gInt a ε x k t) = fun t => t ^ (a - 1) *
586          (Real.exp (-((t + ε) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * Torus.cosMode k x) := by
587        funext t; simp only [gInt, hk, ite_false, neg_mul]
588      rw [this, h]
589      have hm : 0 < Lattice.enorm k := lt_of_lt_of_le one_pos (one_le_enorm k hk)
590      have : rieszCoeff d s k = rieszConst d s * ((2 * π) ^ (s - d) * Lattice.enorm k ^ (s - d)) := by
591        unfold rieszCoeff
592        simp only [hk, ite_false]
593        rw [Real.mul_rpow (by positivity) hm.le]
594      rw [this, hA]
595      field_simp
596      rfl
597  have hsw : ∑' k : Lattice d, ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), gInt a ε x k t =
598      ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), ∑' k : Lattice d, gInt a ε x k t :=
599    integral_tsum_of_summable_integral_norm (gInt_integrable ha ε x) (gInt_norm_summable ha hε x)
600  have hpt : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 < t → ∑' k : Lattice d, gInt a ε x k t = t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x (t + ε) := by
601    intro t ht
602    unfold gInt psiFn
603    rw [tsum_mul_left]
604  unfold heatRiesz
605  simp_rw [hterm]
606  rw [tsum_mul_left, hsw]
607  congr 1
608  exact setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioi fun t ht => hpt t ht
609
610theorem psi_continuousOn {d : ℕ} (x : Torus d) : ContinuousOn (psiFn x) (Ioi 0) := by
611  have key : ∀ u0 : ℝ, 0 < u0 → ContinuousOn (psiFn x) (Ici u0) := by
612    intro u0 hu0
613    unfold psiFn
614    refine continuousOn_tsum (u := fun k : Lattice d => Real.exp (-u0 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2))
615      (fun k => ?_) (summable_gauss_lattice d u0 hu0) ?_
616    · by_cases hk : k = 0
617      · simp only [hk, ite_true]; exact continuousOn_const
618      · simp only [hk, ite_false]
619        exact (by fun_prop : Continuous fun u : ℝ => Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
620          Torus.cosMode k x).continuousOn
621    · intro k u hu
622      by_cases hk : k = 0
623      · simp [hk, (Real.exp_pos _).le]
624      · simp only [hk, ite_false, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
625        have hu' : u0 ≤ u := hu
626        calc _ ≤ Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 1 := by gcongr; exact abs_cosMode_le k x
627          _ ≤ _ := by
628            rw [mul_one]
629            apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
630            have : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 := by positivity
631            nlinarith
632  intro u hu
633  have hu' : (0:ℝ) < u := hu
634  have := (key (u/2) (by positivity)).continuousAt (Ici_mem_nhds (show u / 2 < u by linarith))
635  exact this.continuousWithinAt
636
637theorem cosMode_zero {d : ℕ} (x : Torus d) : Torus.cosMode 0 x = 1 := by
638  simp [Torus.cosMode, Torus.mode, UnitAddTorus.mFourier]
639
640theorem psi_eq {d : ℕ} (x : Torus d) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) :
641    ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x = 1 + psiFn x u := by
642  have hs : Summable fun k : Lattice d => Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x := by
643    refine Summable.of_norm_bounded (summable_gauss_lattice d u hu) fun k => ?_
644    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
645    calc _ ≤ Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 1 := by gcongr; exact abs_cosMode_le k x
646      _ = _ := mul_one _
647  rw [hs.tsum_eq_add_tsum_ite 0]
648  have h0 : Lattice.enorm (0 : Lattice d) = 0 := by
649    simp [Lattice.enorm, Lattice.toEuc]
650    rfl
651  simp [h0, cosMode_zero, psiFn]
652
653theorem psi_bound {d : ℕ} (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
654    ∃ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M ∧ ∀ u : ℝ, 0 < u → |psiFn x u| ≤ M * Real.exp (-u) := by
655  obtain ⟨M0, hM0⟩ := theta_dim_bound x hx
656  set G := ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) with hG
657  have hG0 : 0 ≤ G := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
658  have hM0' : 0 ≤ M0 := by
659    have := hM0 1 one_pos le_rfl
660    exact (abs_nonneg _).trans this
661  refine ⟨Real.exp 1 * G + (M0 + 1) * Real.exp 1, by positivity, fun u hu => ?_⟩
662  rcases le_total 1 u with h1 | h1
663  · -- large u
664    have hb : ∀ k : Lattice d, ‖if k = 0 then 0 else Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x‖
665        ≤ Real.exp (-u) * Real.exp 1 * Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
666      intro k
667      by_cases hk : k = 0
668      · simp only [hk, ite_true, norm_zero]; positivity
669      · simp only [hk, ite_false, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
670        have hm := one_le_enorm k hk
671        calc Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * |Torus.cosMode k x|
672            ≤ Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 1 := by gcongr; exact abs_cosMode_le k x
673          _ = Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := mul_one _
674          _ ≤ Real.exp (-u + 1 + -1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
675              apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
676              nlinarith [mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.mpr h1) (sub_nonneg.mpr (show (1:ℝ) ≤ Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 by nlinarith))]
677          _ = _ := by rw [Real.exp_add, Real.exp_add]
678    have hsum := ((summable_gauss_lattice d 1 one_pos).mul_left
679      (Real.exp (-u) * Real.exp 1)).hasSum
680    have h2 := tsum_of_norm_bounded hsum hb
681    rw [tsum_mul_left, ← hG] at h2
682    have h3 : |psiFn x u| ≤ Real.exp (-u) * Real.exp 1 * G := by
683      rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact h2
684    refine h3.trans ?_
685    have : 0 ≤ (M0 + 1) * Real.exp 1 * Real.exp (-u) := by positivity
686    nlinarith [Real.exp_pos (-u), Real.exp_pos 1]
687  · -- small u
688    have h2 := hM0 u hu h1
689    rw [psi_eq x hu] at h2
690    have h3 : |psiFn x u| ≤ M0 + 1 := by
691      have := abs_sub_abs_le_abs_sub (1 + psiFn x u) 1
692      have h4 : |psiFn x u| = |1 + psiFn x u - 1| := by ring_nf
693      rw [h4]
694      calc |1 + psiFn x u - 1| ≤ |1 + psiFn x u| + |(1:ℝ)| := abs_sub _ _
695        _ ≤ M0 + 1 := by rw [abs_one]; linarith
696    have h5 : 1 ≤ Real.exp 1 * Real.exp (-u) := by
697      rw [← Real.exp_add]; apply Real.one_le_exp; linarith
698    have h6 : M0 + 1 ≤ (M0 + 1) * Real.exp 1 * Real.exp (-u) := by
699      rw [mul_assoc]; nlinarith
700    have : 0 ≤ Real.exp 1 * G * Real.exp (-u) := by positivity
701    nlinarith
702
703
704theorem heat_limit_exists {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
705    ∃ L : ℝ, Tendsto (fun ε => heatRiesz d s ε x) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 L) := by
706  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
707  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
708  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := psi_bound x hx
709  have hcont := psi_continuousOn x
710  have hmeas : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 ≤ ε → AEStronglyMeasurable (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x (t + ε))
711      (volume.restrict (Ioi 0)) := by
712    intro ε hε
713    refine ContinuousOn.aestronglyMeasurable ?_ measurableSet_Ioi
714    refine ContinuousOn.mul (continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt ht)) ?_
715    refine hcont.comp (continuousOn_id.add continuousOn_const) fun t ht => ?_
716    simp only [mem_Ioi] at ht ⊢
717    linarith
718  have hlim : Tendsto (fun ε => ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x (t + ε)) (𝓝[>] 0)
719      (𝓝 (∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x t)) := by
720    refine tendsto_integral_filter_of_dominated_convergence
721      (fun t => M * (Real.exp (-t) * t ^ (a - 1))) ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
722    · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε using hmeas ε (le_of_lt hε)
723    · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
724      refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
725      have hε' : 0 < ε := hε
726      have ht' : 0 < t := ht
727      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' _)]
728      have h1 := hM (t + ε) (by linarith)
729      have h2 : Real.exp (-(t + ε)) ≤ Real.exp (-t) := Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by linarith)
730      have h3 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' (a - 1)
731      calc t ^ (a - 1) * |psiFn x (t + ε)| ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (M * Real.exp (-t)) := by
732            gcongr
733            exact h1.trans (by gcongr)
734        _ = _ := by ring
735    · exact (Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha).const_mul M
736    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
737      have ht' : 0 < t := ht
738      refine Tendsto.const_mul _ ?_
739      have h1 : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => t + ε) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 t) := by
740        have : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => t + ε) (𝓝 0) (𝓝 (t + 0)) :=
741          (continuous_const.add continuous_id).tendsto 0
742        rw [add_zero] at this
743        exact this.mono_left nhdsWithin_le_nhds
744      have h2 : ContinuousAt (psiFn x) t :=
745        (hcont t ht').continuousAt (Ioi_mem_nhds ht')
746      exact h2.tendsto.comp h1
747  refine ⟨rieszConst d s * (2 * π) ^ (s - d) / Real.Gamma a *
748    ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x t, ?_⟩
749  refine (hlim.const_mul _).congr' ?_
750  filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
751  exact (heatRiesz_eq_integral hsd hε x).symm
752
753end KernelLimit
754
755section KernelFacts
756
757variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}
758
759/-- `ĝ(k) > 0` for `k ≠ 0`, `0 ≤ s < d`, `d ≥ 2` (source line 427). -/
760theorem rieszCoeff_pos (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (k : Lattice d) (hk : k ≠ 0) :
761    0 < rieszCoeff d s k := by
762  have h1 := one_le_enorm k hk
763  have h2 := rieszConst_pos hd hs0 hsd
764  unfold rieszCoeff
765  simp only [hk, ite_false]
766  exact mul_pos h2 (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by positivity) _)
767
768/-- `ĝ` is even. -/
769theorem rieszCoeff_neg (k : Lattice d) : rieszCoeff d s (-k) = rieszCoeff d s k := by
770  unfold rieszCoeff
771  simp [enorm_neg]
772
773/-- The heat-regularised series converges absolutely for `ε > 0`. -/
774theorem summable_heatRiesz (hsd : s < d) (ε : ℝ) (hε : 0 < ε) (x : Torus d) :
775    Summable fun k : Lattice d =>
776      rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x := by
777  refine Summable.of_norm_bounded ((summable_gauss_lattice d ε hε).mul_left |rieszConst d s|) ?_
778  intro k
779  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
780  have hc := abs_cosMode_le k x
781  have hexp := (Real.exp_pos (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)).le
782  have hcoef : |rieszCoeff d s k| ≤ |rieszConst d s| := by
783    unfold rieszCoeff
784    by_cases hk : k = 0
785    · simp [hk]
786    · simp only [hk, ite_false]; rw [abs_mul]
787      have h1 := one_le_enorm k hk
788      have hb : (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (s - (d:ℝ)) ≤ 1 :=
789        Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos (by nlinarith [Real.pi_gt_three]) (by linarith)
790      have hp : 0 ≤ (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (s - (d:ℝ)) :=
791        Real.rpow_nonneg (by positivity) _
792      rw [abs_of_nonneg hp]
793      nlinarith [abs_nonneg (rieszConst d s)]
794  calc |rieszCoeff d s k| * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * |Torus.cosMode k x|
795      ≤ |rieszConst d s| * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 1 := by
796        gcongr
797    _ = _ := by ring
798
799/-- The limit defining `g(x)` exists for every `x ≠ 0` (KERN-01). -/
800theorem periodicRiesz_tendsto (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
801    Tendsto (fun ε => heatRiesz d s ε x) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 (periodicRiesz d s x)) := by
802  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := heat_limit_exists hsd x hx
803  have : periodicRiesz d s x = L := hL.limUnder_eq
804  rw [this]; exact hL
805
806/-- `g` is even and has Haar mean zero (KERN-01). -/
807theorem periodicRiesz_neg (x : Torus d) : periodicRiesz d s (-x) = periodicRiesz d s x := by
808  have : (fun ε => heatRiesz d s ε (-x)) = fun ε => heatRiesz d s ε x := by
809    funext ε
810    simp only [heatRiesz, cosMode_neg]
811  unfold periodicRiesz
812  rw [this]
813
814end KernelFacts

818open Complex Set

823/-- The torus point with coordinates `y`. -/
824abbrev boxPt {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) : Torus d := fun i => ((y i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle)
825
826theorem theta_prod {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) :
827    ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k (boxPt y) =
828      ∏ i, (Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2 / u))) := by
829  set S : Fin d → ℂ := fun i => ∑' n : ℤ, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * (y i) - u * (n:ℂ)^2) with hS
830  set f : Fin d → ℤ → ℂ := fun i n => Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * (y i) - u * (n:ℂ)^2) with hf
831  have hfn : ∀ i, Summable fun m => ‖f i m‖ := by
832    intro i
833    refine (summable_exp_sq u hu).congr fun m => ?_
834    simp only [hf]
835    rw [Complex.norm_exp]
836    congr 1
837    rw [show ((m:ℂ)) = ((m:ℝ):ℂ) by simp]
838    simp [pow_two, Complex.mul_re]
839  obtain ⟨hsum, hhas⟩ := pi_hasSum d f hfn
840  have hterm : ∀ k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k (boxPt y) =
841      (∏ i, f i (k i)).re := by
842    intro k
843    have h1 : ∏ i, f i (k i) = ((Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ) * Torus.mode k (boxPt y) := by
844      rw [mode_eq_prod]
845      have h2 : ∏ i, f i (k i) = (∏ i, Complex.exp (-((u:ℂ) * ((k i : ℤ):ℂ)^2))) *
846          ∏ i, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * (k i : ℤ) * (y i : ℝ)) := by
847        rw [← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
848        refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
849        simp only [hf]
850        rw [← Complex.exp_add]
851        congr 1
852        ring
853      rw [h2, ← Complex.exp_sum, Complex.ofReal_exp, enorm_sq]
854      congr 2
855      push_cast
856      rw [neg_mul, Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
857    rw [h1, Complex.re_ofReal_mul]
858    rfl
859  have hre : ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k (boxPt y) =
860      (∏ i, S i).re := by
861    simp_rw [hterm]
862    rw [← Complex.re_tsum hsum.of_norm, hhas.tsum_eq]
863  rw [hre]
864  have hSi : ∀ i, S i = ((Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2 / u)) : ℝ) : ℂ) :=
865    fun i => theta_eq u (y i) hu
866  simp_rw [hSi]
867  rw [← Complex.ofReal_prod, Complex.ofReal_re]
868
869
870theorem abs_sq_le_sub_sq (y : ℝ) (hy : |y| ≤ 1/2) (n : ℤ) : |y|^2 ≤ ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 := by
871  rcases eq_or_ne n 0 with h | h
872  · simp [h]
873  · have h1 : (1:ℝ) ≤ |(n:ℝ)| := by
874      have := Int.one_le_abs h
875      exact_mod_cast this
876    have h2 : |(n:ℝ)| - |y| ≤ |(n:ℝ) - y| := abs_sub_abs_le_abs_sub (n:ℝ) y
877    have h4 : |y| ≤ |(n:ℝ) - y| := by linarith
878    rw [← sq_abs ((n:ℝ) - y)]
879    exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (abs_nonneg _) h4 2
880
881/-- the 1-d theta factor, real form -/
882theorem theta_factor_bound (y : ℝ) (hy : |y| ≤ 1/2) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) (hu1 : u ≤ 1) :
883    0 ≤ Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u)) ∧
884    Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u)) ≤
885      Real.sqrt (π / u) * (∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2)) *
886        Real.exp (-(π^2 * y^2 / (2 * u))) := by
887  have h0 : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u)) :=
888    mul_nonneg (Real.sqrt_nonneg _) (tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le)
889  refine ⟨h0, ?_⟩
890  have h := theta_bound u y |y| hu hu1 hy (fun n => abs_sq_le_sub_sq y hy n)
891  rw [theta_eq u y hu, Complex.norm_real, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg h0, sq_abs] at h
892  exact h
893
894
895/-- Gaussian majorant of the periodised theta function on the box, `u ≤ 1`. -/
896theorem theta_gauss_bound {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) (hy : ∀ i, |y i| ≤ 1/2) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u)
897    (hu1 : u ≤ 1) (j : Fin d) :
898    0 ≤ ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k (boxPt y) ∧
899    ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k (boxPt y) ≤
900      (Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2))^d *
901        Real.exp (-(π^2 * (y j)^2 / (2 * u))) := by
902  rw [theta_prod y hu]
903  refine ⟨Finset.prod_nonneg fun i _ => (theta_factor_bound (y i) (hy i) hu hu1).1, ?_⟩
904  refine (Finset.prod_le_prod₀ (fun i _ => (theta_factor_bound (y i) (hy i) hu hu1).1)
905    (fun i _ => (theta_factor_bound (y i) (hy i) hu hu1).2)).trans ?_
906  rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, ← Real.exp_sum]
907  have hB : 0 ≤ (Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2)) :=
908    mul_nonneg (Real.sqrt_nonneg _) (tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le)
909  gcongr
910  rw [Finset.sum_neg_distrib, neg_le_neg_iff]
911  exact Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun i => π^2 * (y i)^2 / (2 * u)) (fun i _ => by positivity)
912    (Finset.mem_univ j)

915theorem rpow_mul_exp_le {q : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q) {v : ℝ} (hv : 0 < v) :
916    v ^ q * Real.exp (-v) ≤ q ^ q * Real.exp (-q) := by
917  have h1 : Real.log (v / q) ≤ v / q - 1 := Real.log_le_sub_one_of_pos (by positivity)
918  rw [Real.log_div hv.ne' hq.ne'] at h1
919  have h2 : q * Real.log v - v ≤ q * Real.log q - q := by
920    have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 hq.le
921    rw [mul_sub, mul_sub, mul_div_cancel₀ _ hq.ne'] at this
922    linarith
923  rw [Real.rpow_def_of_pos hv, Real.rpow_def_of_pos hq, ← Real.exp_add, ← Real.exp_add]
924  apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
925  linarith [mul_comm (Real.log v) q, mul_comm (Real.log q) q]
926
927theorem exp_neg_le_rpow {q : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q) {v : ℝ} (hv : 0 < v) :
928    Real.exp (-v) ≤ (q ^ q * Real.exp (-q)) * v ^ (-q) := by
929  have h := rpow_mul_exp_le hq hv
930  rw [Real.rpow_neg hv.le, ← div_eq_mul_inv, le_div_iff₀ (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hv q)]
931  linarith [mul_comm (Real.exp (-v)) (v ^ q)]

933/-- uniform decay of `Ψ` for `u ≥ 1`. -/
934theorem psi_large {d : ℕ} (x : Torus d) {u : ℝ} (hu : 1 ≤ u) :
935    |psiFn x u| ≤ (Real.exp 1 * ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * Real.exp (-u) := by
936  set G := ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) with hG
937  have hb : ∀ k : Lattice d, ‖if k = 0 then 0 else Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x‖
938      ≤ Real.exp (-u) * Real.exp 1 * Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
939    intro k
940    by_cases hk : k = 0
941    · simp only [hk, ite_true, norm_zero]; positivity
942    · simp only [hk, ite_false, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
943      have hm := one_le_enorm k hk
944      calc Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * |Torus.cosMode k x|
945          ≤ Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 1 := by gcongr; exact abs_cosMode_le k x
946        _ = Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := mul_one _
947        _ ≤ Real.exp (-u + 1 + -1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
948            apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
949            nlinarith [mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.mpr hu) (sub_nonneg.mpr (show (1:ℝ) ≤ Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 by nlinarith))]
950        _ = _ := by rw [Real.exp_add, Real.exp_add]
951  have hsum := ((summable_gauss_lattice d 1 one_pos).mul_left
952    (Real.exp (-u) * Real.exp 1)).hasSum
953  have h2 := tsum_of_norm_bounded hsum hb
954  rw [tsum_mul_left, ← hG] at h2
955  have h3 : |psiFn x u| ≤ Real.exp (-u) * Real.exp 1 * G := by
956    rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact h2
957  calc |psiFn x u| ≤ Real.exp (-u) * Real.exp 1 * G := h3
958    _ = _ := by ring

961theorem sqrt_div_pow_eq (d : ℕ) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) :
962    Real.sqrt (π / u) ^ d = Real.sqrt π ^ d * u ^ (-(d:ℝ)/2) := by
963  have h1 : Real.sqrt u ^ d = u ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) := by
964    rw [Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hu.le]; congr 1; ring
965  rw [Real.sqrt_div Real.pi_pos.le, div_pow, h1, div_eq_mul_inv, neg_div, Real.rpow_neg hu.le]
966
967theorem psi_small_bound {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) (hy : ∀ i, |y i| ≤ 1/2) (j : Fin d) (hj : y j ≠ 0)
968    {q : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q) (hqd : q < (d:ℝ)/2) {t ε : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) (ht1 : t ≤ 1) (hε : 0 < ε) :
969    |psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)| ≤
970      ((Real.sqrt π * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2))^d * (q^q * Real.exp (-q)) *
971          (π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2)^(-q) + 1 +
972        (Real.exp 1 * ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2))) * t ^ (q - (d:ℝ)/2) := by
973  set B := ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2) with hB
974  set G := ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) with hG
975  have hB0 : 0 ≤ B := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
976  have hG0 : 0 ≤ G := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
977  set r := q - (d:ℝ)/2 with hr
978  have hr0 : r < 0 := by rw [hr]; linarith
979  have ht1' : 1 ≤ t ^ r := Real.one_le_rpow_of_pos_of_le_one_of_nonpos ht ht1 hr0.le
980  have hu : 0 < t + ε := by linarith
981  have hM1 : 0 ≤ Real.exp 1 * G := by positivity
982  have hb : 0 < π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2 := by
983    have : 0 < (y j)^2 := by positivity
984    positivity
985  set Kc := (Real.sqrt π * B)^d * (q^q * Real.exp (-q)) * (π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2)^(-q) with hKc
986  have hKc0 : 0 ≤ Kc := by positivity
987  have hfinal : ∀ z : ℝ, 0 ≤ z → z ≤ Kc * t ^ r + 1 ∨ z ≤ Real.exp 1 * G →
988      z ≤ (Kc + 1 + Real.exp 1 * G) * t ^ r := by
989    intro z hz h
990    have h0 : 0 ≤ t ^ r := by linarith
991    rcases h with h | h
992    · nlinarith
993    · nlinarith
994  apply hfinal _ (abs_nonneg _)
995  by_cases hu1 : t + ε ≤ 1
996  · left
997    obtain ⟨hΘ0, hΘ⟩ := theta_gauss_bound y hy hu hu1 j
998    have hpsi := psi_eq (boxPt y) hu
999    have h1 : |psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)| ≤ (∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(t + ε) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k (boxPt y)) + 1 := by
1000      rw [hpsi]
1001      have : psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε) = (1 + psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)) - 1 := by ring
1002      calc |psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)| = |(1 + psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)) - 1| := by rw [← this]
1003        _ ≤ |1 + psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)| + |(1:ℝ)| := abs_sub _ _
1004        _ = _ := by
1005          rw [abs_one, abs_of_nonneg (by rw [← hpsi]; exact hΘ0)]
1006    have h2 : (Real.sqrt (π / (t + ε)) * B) ^ d * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * y j ^ 2 / (2 * (t + ε)))) ≤
1007        Kc * (t + ε) ^ r := by
1008      rw [mul_pow, sqrt_div_pow_eq d hu]
1009      have hv : (π ^ 2 * y j ^ 2 / (2 * (t + ε))) = (π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2) / (t + ε) := by
1010        field_simp
1011      have he := exp_neg_le_rpow hq (v := (π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2) / (t + ε)) (by positivity)
1012      have hbu : ((π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2) / (t + ε)) ^ (-q) = (π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2) ^ (-q) * (t + ε) ^ q := by
1013        rw [Real.div_rpow hb.le hu.le, Real.rpow_neg hu.le, div_inv_eq_mul]
1014      rw [hbu] at he
1015      rw [hv]
1016      calc (Real.sqrt π ^ d * (t + ε) ^ (-(d:ℝ)/2) * B ^ d) * Real.exp (-((π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2) / (t + ε)))
1017          ≤ (Real.sqrt π ^ d * (t + ε) ^ (-(d:ℝ)/2) * B ^ d) *
1018            ((q ^ q * Real.exp (-q)) * ((π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2) ^ (-q) * (t + ε) ^ q)) := by
1019            gcongr
1020        _ = Kc * ((t + ε) ^ (-(d:ℝ)/2) * (t + ε) ^ q) := by rw [hKc, mul_pow]; ring
1021        _ = Kc * (t + ε) ^ r := by
1022            rw [← Real.rpow_add hu]; congr 2; rw [hr]; ring
1023    have h3 : (t + ε) ^ r ≤ t ^ r := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos ht (by linarith) hr0.le
1024    calc |psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)| ≤ _ := h1
1025      _ ≤ Kc * (t + ε) ^ r + 1 := by linarith
1026      _ ≤ Kc * t ^ r + 1 := by gcongr
1027  · right
1028    push Not at hu1
1029    have h := psi_large (boxPt y) hu1.le
1030    refine h.trans ?_
1031    have : Real.exp (-(t + ε)) ≤ 1 := Real.exp_le_one_iff.mpr (by linarith)
1032    nlinarith
1033

1035theorem heat_eps_bound {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
1036    ∃ C1 C2 : ℝ, 0 ≤ C1 ∧ 0 ≤ C2 ∧ ∀ (y : Fin d → ℝ), (∀ i, |y i| ≤ 1/2) → ∀ j, y j ≠ 0 →
1037      ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε ≤ 1 →
1038        |heatRiesz d s ε (boxPt y)| ≤ C1 * |y j| ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2 := by
1039  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
1040  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
1041  set q : ℝ := (s + d) / 4 with hq_def
1042  have hd0 : (0:ℝ) < d := by linarith
1043  have hq : 0 < q := by rw [hq_def]; linarith
1044  have hqd : q < (d:ℝ)/2 := by rw [hq_def]; linarith
1045  set r : ℝ := q - (d:ℝ)/2 with hr
1046  have hr_eq : a - 1 + r = a / 2 - 1 := by rw [hr, hq_def, ha_def]; ring
1047  set B := ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 / 8) * (n:ℝ)^2) with hB
1048  set G := ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) with hG
1049  have hB0 : 0 ≤ B := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
1050  have hG0 : 0 ≤ G := tsum_nonneg fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le
1051  set M1 := Real.exp 1 * G with hM1
1052  have hM10 : 0 ≤ M1 := by positivity
1053  set A := rieszConst d s * (2 * π) ^ (s - d) / Real.Gamma a with hA
1054  set f1 : ℝ → ℝ := (Ioc (0:ℝ) 1).indicator (fun t => t ^ (a / 2 - 1)) with hf1
1055  set f2 : ℝ → ℝ := fun t => t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t) with hf2
1056  have hf1i : Integrable f1 := by
1057    have : IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a / 2 - 1)) (Ioc 0 1) := by
1058      have := (intervalIntegral.intervalIntegrable_rpow' (a := 0) (b := 1) (r := a / 2 - 1)
1059        (by linarith))
1060      exact (intervalIntegrable_iff_integrableOn_Ioc_of_le zero_le_one).mp this
1061    exact this.integrable_indicator measurableSet_Ioc
1062  have hf2i : IntegrableOn f2 (Ioi 0) := by
1063    have := Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha
1064    refine this.congr_fun (fun t _ => ?_) measurableSet_Ioi
1065    simp only [hf2]; ring
1066  set c1 := ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), f1 t with hc1
1067  set c2 := ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), f2 t with hc2
1068  have hf10 : ∀ t, 0 ≤ f1 t := by
1069    intro t
1070    simp only [hf1, indicator]
1071    split_ifs with h
1072    · exact (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos h.1 _).le
1073    · exact le_rfl
1074  have hf20 : ∀ t, 0 < t → 0 ≤ f2 t := fun t ht => by
1075    simp only [hf2]; exact mul_nonneg (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht _).le (Real.exp_pos _).le
1076  have hc10 : 0 ≤ c1 := setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioi fun t _ => hf10 t
1077  have hc20 : 0 ≤ c2 := setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioi fun t ht => hf20 t ht
1078  -- constants
1079  set κ := (Real.sqrt π * B)^d * (q^q * Real.exp (-q)) * (π^2 / 2) ^ (-q) with hκ
1080  have hκ0 : 0 ≤ κ := by positivity
1081  refine ⟨|A| * c1 * κ, |A| * (c1 * (1 + M1) + M1 * c2), by positivity, by positivity, ?_⟩
1082  intro y hy j hj ε hε hε1
1083  rw [heatRiesz_eq_integral hsd hε (boxPt y)]
1084  set m := |y j| with hm
1085  have hm0 : 0 < m := abs_pos.mpr hj
1086  set Kc := (Real.sqrt π * B)^d * (q^q * Real.exp (-q)) * (π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2)^(-q) with hKc
1087  have hKc_eq : Kc = κ * m ^ (-((s + d)/2)) := by
1088    rw [hKc, hκ]
1089    have : (π^2 * (y j)^2 / 2) = (π^2 / 2) * (y j)^2 := by ring
1090    rw [this, Real.mul_rpow (by positivity) (by positivity)]
1091    have h2 : ((y j)^2) ^ (-q) = m ^ (-((s + d)/2)) := by
1092      rw [← sq_abs, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul (abs_nonneg _)]
1093      congr 1
1094      rw [hq_def]; push_cast; ring
1095    rw [h2]; ring
1096  have hKc0 : 0 ≤ Kc := by rw [hKc_eq]; positivity
1097  set K := Kc + 1 + M1 with hK
1098  have hK0 : 0 ≤ K := by positivity
1099  -- pointwise bound
1100  have hpt : ∀ t ∈ Ioi (0:ℝ), ‖t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)‖ ≤ K * f1 t + M1 * f2 t := by
1101    intro t ht
1102    have ht' : 0 < t := ht
1103    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' _)]
1104    by_cases ht1 : t ≤ 1
1105    · have h := psi_small_bound y hy j hj hq hqd ht' ht1 hε
1106      have hf1t : f1 t = t ^ (a / 2 - 1) := by
1107        simp only [hf1, indicator, mem_Ioc, ht', ht1, and_self, ite_true]
1108      rw [hf1t]
1109      have h2 : t ^ (a - 1) * |psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)| ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (K * t ^ r) := by
1110        gcongr
1111      refine h2.trans ?_
1112      have : t ^ (a - 1) * (K * t ^ r) = K * t ^ (a / 2 - 1) := by
1113        rw [← hr_eq, Real.rpow_add ht']; ring
1114      rw [this]
1115      have : 0 ≤ M1 * f2 t := mul_nonneg hM10 (hf20 t ht')
1116      linarith
1117    · push Not at ht1
1118      have hf1t : f1 t = 0 := by
1119        simp only [hf1, indicator, mem_Ioc]
1120        simp [not_le.mpr ht1]
1121      rw [hf1t]
1122      have h := psi_large (boxPt y) (u := t + ε) (by linarith)
1123      have h2 : t ^ (a - 1) * |psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)| ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (M1 * Real.exp (-t)) := by
1124        gcongr
1125        refine h.trans ?_
1126        gcongr
1127        linarith
1128      refine h2.trans ?_
1129      simp only [hf2]
1130      nlinarith [Real.exp_pos (-t), Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' (a - 1)]
1131  have hint : Integrable (fun t => K * f1 t + M1 * f2 t) (volume.restrict (Ioi 0)) :=
1132    (hf1i.integrableOn.const_mul K).add (hf2i.const_mul M1)
1133  have hI := norm_integral_le_of_norm_le (μ := volume.restrict (Ioi (0:ℝ))) hint
1134    ((ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hpt))
1135  rw [integral_add (hf1i.integrableOn.const_mul K) (hf2i.const_mul M1), integral_const_mul,
1136    integral_const_mul] at hI
1137  rw [abs_mul]
1138  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at hI
1139  calc |A| * |∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (boxPt y) (t + ε)|
1140      ≤ |A| * (K * c1 + M1 * c2) := by gcongr
1141    _ = |A| * c1 * κ * m ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + |A| * (c1 * (1 + M1) + M1 * c2) := by
1142        rw [hK, hKc_eq]; ring

1145/-- the fundamental box `(-1/2, 1/2]^d`. -/
1146def Box (d : ℕ) : Set (Fin d → ℝ) := {x | ∀ i, x i ∈ Ioc (-1/2 : ℝ) (-1/2 + 1)}
1147
1148theorem measurableSet_Box (d : ℕ) : MeasurableSet (Box d) :=
1149  MeasurableSet.univ_pi' (fun _ => measurableSet_Ioc)
1150
1151theorem Box_abs_le {d : ℕ} {y : Fin d → ℝ} (hy : y ∈ Box d) (i : Fin d) : |y i| ≤ 1/2 := by
1152  have := hy i
1153  simp only [mem_Ioc] at this
1154  rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [this.1, this.2]
1155
1156theorem Box_norm_le {d : ℕ} {y : Fin d → ℝ} (hy : y ∈ Box d) : ‖y‖ ≤ 1/2 := by
1157  refine (pi_norm_le_iff_of_nonneg (by norm_num)).2 fun i => ?_
1158  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact Box_abs_le hy i
1159
1160theorem Box_subset_ball (d : ℕ) : Box d ⊆ Metric.ball 0 1 := by
1161  intro y hy
1162  rw [mem_ball_zero_iff]
1163  linarith [Box_norm_le hy]
1164
1165theorem boxPt_eq_zero {d : ℕ} {y : Fin d → ℝ} (hy : y ∈ Box d) (h : boxPt y = 0) : y = 0 := by
1166  funext i
1167  have hi : ((y i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = 0 := congrFun h i
1168  rw [AddCircle.coe_eq_zero_iff] at hi
1169  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hi
1170  have h1 := hy i
1171  simp only [mem_Ioc] at h1
1172  have hn' : (n:ℝ) = y i := by simpa using hn
1173  have : n = 0 := by
1174    have h2 : (-1:ℝ) < n := by rw [hn']; linarith [h1.1]
1175    have h3 : (n:ℝ) < 1 := by rw [hn']; linarith [h1.2]
1176    have h2' : -1 < n := by exact_mod_cast h2
1177    have h3' : n < 1 := by exact_mod_cast h3
1178    omega
1179  simp [← hn', this]

1181theorem exists_argmax {d : ℕ} (hd : 0 < d) (y : Fin d → ℝ) : ∃ j, ‖y‖ = |y j| := by
1182  obtain ⟨j, -, hj⟩ := Finset.exists_max_image (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin d)) (fun i => |y i|)
1183    ⟨⟨0, hd⟩, Finset.mem_univ _⟩
1184  refine ⟨j, le_antisymm ?_ ?_⟩
1185  · refine (pi_norm_le_iff_of_nonneg (abs_nonneg _)).2 fun i => ?_
1186    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hj i (Finset.mem_univ i)
1187  · rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact norm_le_pi_norm y j

1189theorem integrableOn_Box_rpow {d : ℕ} (hd : 0 < d) {α : ℝ} (hα : α < d) :
1190    IntegrableOn (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => ‖y‖ ^ (-α)) (Box d) := by
1191  have hfin : Module.finrank ℝ (Fin d → ℝ) = d := by simp
1192  have h := integrableOn_ball_of_norm_le_rpow (μ := (volume : Measure (Fin d → ℝ)))
1193    (f := fun y : Fin d → ℝ => ‖y‖ ^ (-α)) (C := 1) (α := α) (r := 1)
1194    (by rw [hfin]; exact hd) (by rw [hfin]; exact hα)
1195    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by
1196      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, one_mul]
1197      exact (abs_of_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) _)).le)
1198    (Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable (measurable_norm.pow_const _))
1199  exact h.mono_set (Box_subset_ball d)

1201theorem measure_Box_ne_top (d : ℕ) : (volume : Measure (Fin d → ℝ)) (Box d) ≠ ∞ :=
1202  ((measure_mono (Box_subset_ball d)).trans_lt measure_ball_lt_top).ne

1205theorem abs_rieszCoeff_le (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (hsd : s < d) (k : Lattice d) :
1206    |rieszCoeff d s k| ≤ |rieszConst d s| := by
1207  unfold rieszCoeff
1208  by_cases hk : k = 0
1209  · simp [hk]
1210  · simp only [hk, ite_false]; rw [abs_mul]
1211    have h1 := one_le_enorm k hk
1212    have hb : (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (s - (d:ℝ)) ≤ 1 :=
1213      Real.rpow_le_one_of_one_le_of_nonpos (by nlinarith [Real.pi_gt_three]) (by linarith)
1214    have hp : 0 ≤ (2 * Real.pi * Lattice.enorm k) ^ (s - (d:ℝ)) :=
1215      Real.rpow_nonneg (by positivity) _
1216    rw [abs_of_nonneg hp]
1217    nlinarith [abs_nonneg (rieszConst d s)]

1219theorem norm_term_le {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) (ε : ℝ) (x : Torus d) (k : Lattice d) :
1220    ‖rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x‖ ≤
1221      |rieszConst d s| * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
1222  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1223  have hc := abs_cosMode_le k x
1224  have hexp := (Real.exp_pos (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)).le
1225  have := abs_rieszCoeff_le d s hsd k
1226  calc |rieszCoeff d s k| * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * |Torus.cosMode k x|
1227      ≤ |rieszConst d s| * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 1 := by gcongr
1228    _ = _ := by ring

1230theorem continuous_heatRiesz {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) :
1231    Continuous (heatRiesz d s ε) := by
1232  unfold heatRiesz
1233  refine continuous_tsum (u := fun k : Lattice d => |rieszConst d s| * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2))
1234    (fun k => ?_) ((summable_gauss_lattice d ε hε).mul_left _) (fun k x => norm_term_le hsd ε x k)
1235  exact continuous_const.mul (Torus.continuous_cosMode k)

1237theorem cosMode_cast {d : ℕ} (l : Lattice d) (x : Torus d) :
1238    ((Torus.cosMode l x : ℝ) : ℂ) = (Torus.mode l x + Torus.mode (-l) x) / 2 := by
1239  rw [Torus.mode_neg, Torus.cosMode, Complex.re_eq_add_conj]
1240
1241
1242theorem integral_mode_cos {d : ℕ} (k l : Lattice d) :
1243    ∫ x : Torus d, Torus.mode (-k) x * ((Torus.cosMode l x : ℝ) : ℂ) =
1244      ((if l = k then 1 else 0) + (if l = -k then 1 else 0)) / 2 := by
1245  have h1 : ∀ x : Torus d, Torus.mode (-k) x * ((Torus.cosMode l x : ℝ) : ℂ) =
1246      (Torus.mode (-k) x * Torus.mode l x + Torus.mode (-k) x * Torus.mode (-l) x) / 2 := by
1247    intro x; rw [cosMode_cast]; ring
1248  simp_rw [h1]
1249  rw [integral_div, integral_add (Torus.integrable_mode_mul _ _) (Torus.integrable_mode_mul _ _),
1250    Torus.integral_mode_mul, Torus.integral_mode_mul]
1251  have e1 : (-k + l = 0) ↔ l = k := by constructor <;> intro h <;> [exact (neg_add_eq_zero.mp h).symm; (rw [h]; simp)]
1252  have e2 : (-k + -l = 0) ↔ l = -k := by
1253    constructor
1254    · intro h
1255      have : -l = k := by
1256        have := congrArg (fun z => z + k) h
1257        simpa [add_comm, add_left_comm] using this
1258      simpa using congrArg Neg.neg this
1259    · intro h; rw [h]; simp
1260  simp only [e1, e2]
1261
1262theorem integral_heat_mode {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (k : Lattice d) :
1263    ∫ x : Torus d, Torus.mode (-k) x * ((heatRiesz d s ε x : ℝ) : ℂ) =
1264      ((rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
1265  set c : Lattice d → ℝ := fun l => rieszCoeff d s l * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm l ^ 2) with hc
1266  set F : Lattice d → Torus d → ℂ := fun l x => Torus.mode (-k) x * (((c l * Torus.cosMode l x : ℝ)) : ℂ) with hF
1267  have hFcont : ∀ l, Continuous (F l) := fun l =>
1268    (Torus.continuous_mode _).mul (Complex.continuous_ofReal.comp
1269      (continuous_const.mul (Torus.continuous_cosMode l)))
1270  have hFint : ∀ l, Integrable (F l) := fun l =>
1271    (hFcont l).integrable_of_hasCompactSupport (HasCompactSupport.of_compactSpace _)
1272  have hFnorm : ∀ l x, ‖F l x‖ ≤ |rieszConst d s| * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm l ^ 2) := by
1273    intro l x
1274    simp only [hF]
1275    rw [norm_mul, norm_mode, one_mul, Complex.norm_real]
1276    exact norm_term_le hsd ε x l
1277  have hsumF : Summable fun l => ∫ x, ‖F l x‖ := by
1278    refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun l => integral_nonneg fun x => norm_nonneg _) (fun l => ?_)
1279      ((summable_gauss_lattice d ε hε).mul_left |rieszConst d s|)
1280    calc ∫ x, ‖F l x‖ ≤ ∫ x : Torus d, |rieszConst d s| * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm l ^ 2) :=
1281          integral_mono (hFint l).norm (integrable_const _) (fun x => hFnorm l x)
1282      _ = _ := by simp
1283  have hsw := integral_tsum_of_summable_integral_norm hFint hsumF
1284  have hpt : ∀ x : Torus d, ∑' l, F l x = Torus.mode (-k) x * ((heatRiesz d s ε x : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
1285    intro x
1286    have hs := summable_heatRiesz hsd ε hε x
1287    simp only [hF]
1288    rw [tsum_mul_left, ← Complex.ofReal_tsum]
1289    rfl
1290  simp_rw [← hpt]
1291  rw [← hsw]
1292  have hl : ∀ l, ∫ x, F l x = (c l : ℂ) * (((if l = k then 1 else 0) + (if l = -k then 1 else 0)) / 2) := by
1293    intro l
1294    simp only [hF]
1295    have : ∀ x : Torus d, Torus.mode (-k) x * (((c l * Torus.cosMode l x : ℝ)) : ℂ) =
1296        (c l : ℂ) * (Torus.mode (-k) x * ((Torus.cosMode l x : ℝ) : ℂ)) := by
1297      intro x; push_cast; ring
1298    simp_rw [this]
1299    rw [integral_const_mul, integral_mode_cos]
1300  simp_rw [hl]
1301  have hneg : c (-k) = c k := by simp [hc, rieszCoeff_neg, enorm_neg]
1302  have hsplit : ∀ l, (c l : ℂ) * (((if l = k then 1 else 0) + (if l = -k then 1 else 0)) / 2) =
1303      (if l = k then (c k : ℂ) / 2 else 0) + (if l = -k then (c (-k) : ℂ) / 2 else 0) := by
1304    intro l
1305    split_ifs with h1 h2 h2
1306    · rw [h1, hneg]; ring
1307    · rw [h1]; ring
1308    · rw [h2]; ring
1309    · simp
1310  simp_rw [hsplit]
1311  rw [((hasSum_ite_eq k ((c k : ℂ) / 2)).add (hasSum_ite_eq (-k) ((c (-k) : ℂ) / 2))).tsum_eq, hneg]
1312  simp only [hc]
1313  push_cast
1314  ring
1315

1317theorem continuous_boxPt (d : ℕ) : Continuous (boxPt : (Fin d → ℝ) → Torus d) :=
1318  continuous_pi fun i => (AddCircle.continuous_mk' _).comp (continuous_apply i)

1320theorem integral_torus_box {d : ℕ} {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]
1321    (f : Torus d → E) : ∫ x : Torus d, f x = ∫ y in Box d, f (boxPt y) := by
1322  rw [Torus.volume_eq_pi_haar]
1323  exact UnitAddTorus.integral_preimage f (fun _ => (-1/2 : ℝ))

1325theorem integral_periodicRiesz_mode {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
1326    (k : Lattice d) :
1327    ∫ x : Torus d, Torus.mode (-k) x * ((periodicRiesz d s x : ℝ) : ℂ) = ((rieszCoeff d s k : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
1328  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
1329  have : Nonempty (Fin d) := ⟨⟨0, hd0⟩⟩
1330  obtain ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, hbd⟩ := heat_eps_bound hs0 hsd
1331  set μ : Measure (Fin d → ℝ) := volume.restrict (Box d) with hμ
1332  set G : (Fin d → ℝ) → ℝ := fun y => C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2 with hG
1333  set Fε : ℝ → (Fin d → ℝ) → ℂ := fun ε y =>
1334    Torus.mode (-k) (boxPt y) * ((heatRiesz d s ε (boxPt y) : ℝ) : ℂ) with hFε
1335  set F0 : (Fin d → ℝ) → ℂ := fun y =>
1336    Torus.mode (-k) (boxPt y) * ((periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y) : ℝ) : ℂ) with hF0
1337  have hne : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, y ≠ 0 := by
1338    have h1 : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume : Measure (Fin d → ℝ)), y ≠ 0 := by
1339      rw [ae_iff]; simp
1340    exact ae_restrict_of_ae h1
1341  have hmem : ∀ᵐ y ∂μ, y ∈ Box d := ae_restrict_mem (measurableSet_Box d)
1342  have hdom := tendsto_integral_filter_of_dominated_convergence (μ := μ) (l := 𝓝[>] (0:ℝ))
1343    (F := Fε) (f := F0) G ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
1344  · -- identify the limit
1345    have h1 : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∫ y, Fε ε y ∂μ =
1346        ((rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
1347      intro ε hε
1348      rw [← integral_heat_mode hsd hε k]
1349      exact (integral_torus_box (fun x : Torus d => Torus.mode (-k) x * ((heatRiesz d s ε x : ℝ) : ℂ))).symm
1350    have h2 : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => ((rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ))
1351        (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 ((rieszCoeff d s k : ℝ) : ℂ)) := by
1352      have : Continuous fun ε : ℝ => ((rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
1353        fun_prop
1354      have := (this.tendsto 0).mono_left (nhdsWithin_le_nhds (s := Ioi (0:ℝ)))
1355      simpa using this
1356    have h3 : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => ∫ y, Fε ε y ∂μ) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 ((rieszCoeff d s k : ℝ) : ℂ)) :=
1357      h2.congr' (by filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε using (h1 ε hε).symm)
1358    have h4 := tendsto_nhds_unique hdom h3
1359    rw [← h4]
1360    exact integral_torus_box (fun x : Torus d => Torus.mode (-k) x * ((periodicRiesz d s x : ℝ) : ℂ))
1361  · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
1362    have hε' : 0 < ε := hε
1363    refine Continuous.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
1364    exact ((Torus.continuous_mode _).comp (continuous_boxPt d)).mul
1365      (Complex.continuous_ofReal.comp ((continuous_heatRiesz hsd hε').comp (continuous_boxPt d)))
1366  · filter_upwards [Ioo_mem_nhdsGT one_pos] with ε hε
1367    filter_upwards [hne, hmem] with y hy hyB
1368    have hy0 : boxPt y ≠ 0 := fun h => hy (boxPt_eq_zero hyB h)
1369    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := exists_argmax hd0 y
1370    have hyj : y j ≠ 0 := by
1371      intro h0
1372      apply hy
1373      have : ‖y‖ = 0 := by rw [hj, h0, abs_zero]
1374      exact norm_eq_zero.mp this
1375    have := hbd y (Box_abs_le hyB) j hyj ε hε.1 hε.2.le
1376    simp only [hFε, norm_mul, norm_mode, one_mul, Complex.norm_real, Real.norm_eq_abs, hG, hj]
1377    exact this
1378  · have hI : IntegrableOn (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2))) (Box d) :=
1379      integrableOn_Box_rpow hd0 (by linarith)
1380    exact (hI.const_mul C1).add (integrableOn_const (measure_Box_ne_top d))
1381  · filter_upwards [hne, hmem] with y hy hyB
1382    have hy0 : boxPt y ≠ 0 := fun h => hy (boxPt_eq_zero hyB h)
1383    have := periodicRiesz_tendsto hd hs0 hsd (boxPt y) hy0
1384    exact (Complex.continuous_ofReal.tendsto _ |>.comp this).const_mul _

1388    ∫⁻ x : Torus d, f x = ∫⁻ y in Box d, f (boxPt y) := by

1392theorem ae_ne_pi {d : ℕ} (hd : 0 < d) : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume : Measure (Fin d → ℝ)), y ≠ 0 := by
1393  have : Nonempty (Fin d) := ⟨⟨0, hd⟩⟩
1394  rw [ae_iff]; simp

1396theorem torus_ae_ne {d : ℕ} (hd : 0 < d) : ∀ᵐ x : Torus d, x ≠ 0 := by
1397  have : Nonempty (Fin d) := ⟨⟨0, hd⟩⟩
1398  rw [ae_iff]
1399  simp only [ne_eq, not_not]
1400  show (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {0} = 0
1401  have h := lintegral_torus_box (d := d) (({0} : Set (Torus d)).indicator 1)
1402  have h1 : ∫⁻ x : Torus d, ({0} : Set (Torus d)).indicator 1 x = (volume : Measure (Torus d)) {0} := by
1403    rw [lintegral_indicator_one (measurableSet_singleton _)]
1404  have h2 : ∫⁻ y in Box d, ({0} : Set (Torus d)).indicator 1 (boxPt y) = 0 := by
1405    have : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume.restrict (Box d)), ({0} : Set (Torus d)).indicator (1 : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞) (boxPt y) = 0 := by
1406      filter_upwards [ae_restrict_of_ae (ae_ne_pi hd), ae_restrict_mem (measurableSet_Box d)] with y hy hyB
1407      have hn : boxPt y ∉ ({0} : Set (Torus d)) := fun h => hy (boxPt_eq_zero hyB h)
1408      exact Set.indicator_of_notMem hn _
1409    rw [lintegral_congr_ae this]; simp
1410  rw [← h1, h, h2]

1412theorem periodicRiesz_box_bound {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
1413    ∃ C1 C2 : ℝ, 0 ≤ C1 ∧ 0 ≤ C2 ∧ ∀ y : Fin d → ℝ, y ∈ Box d → y ≠ 0 →
1414      |periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y)| ≤ C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2 := by
1415  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
1416  obtain ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, hbd⟩ := heat_eps_bound hs0 hsd
1417  refine ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, fun y hyB hy => ?_⟩
1418  have hy0 : boxPt y ≠ 0 := fun h => hy (boxPt_eq_zero hyB h)
1419  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := exists_argmax hd0 y
1420  have hyj : y j ≠ 0 := by
1421    intro h0
1422    apply hy
1423    have : ‖y‖ = 0 := by rw [hj, h0, abs_zero]
1424    exact norm_eq_zero.mp this
1425  have hlim := (periodicRiesz_tendsto hd hs0 hsd (boxPt y) hy0).abs
1426  refine le_of_tendsto hlim ?_
1427  filter_upwards [Ioo_mem_nhdsGT one_pos] with ε hε
1428  have := hbd y (Box_abs_le hyB) j hyj ε hε.1 hε.2.le
1429  rwa [← hj] at this

1431theorem aestronglyMeasurable_periodicRiesz {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
1432    AEStronglyMeasurable (periodicRiesz d s) (volume : Measure (Torus d)) := by
1433  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
1434  refine aestronglyMeasurable_of_tendsto_ae (u := atTop) (f := fun n : ℕ => heatRiesz d s (1 / ((n:ℝ) + 1)))
1435    (fun n => (continuous_heatRiesz hsd (by positivity)).aestronglyMeasurable) ?_
1436  filter_upwards [torus_ae_ne hd0] with x hx
1437  have h := periodicRiesz_tendsto hd hs0 hsd x hx
1438  refine h.comp ?_
1439  refine tendsto_nhdsWithin_iff.mpr ⟨tendsto_one_div_add_atTop_nhds_zero_nat, Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun n => ?_⟩
1440  simp only [mem_Ioi]; positivity

1442theorem integrable_periodicRiesz {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
1443    Integrable (periodicRiesz d s) := by
1444  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
1445  obtain ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, hbd⟩ := periodicRiesz_box_bound hd hs0 hsd
1446  refine ⟨aestronglyMeasurable_periodicRiesz hd hs0 hsd, ?_⟩
1447  unfold HasFiniteIntegral
1448  rw [lintegral_torus_box (fun x => ‖periodicRiesz d s x‖ₑ)]
1449  have hI : IntegrableOn (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2) (Box d) :=
1450    ((integrableOn_Box_rpow hd0 (by linarith)).const_mul C1).add
1451      (integrableOn_const (measure_Box_ne_top d))
1452  have hne : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume.restrict (Box d)), y ≠ 0 := by
1453    exact ae_restrict_of_ae (ae_ne_pi hd0)
1454  calc ∫⁻ y in Box d, ‖periodicRiesz d s (boxPt y)‖ₑ
1455      ≤ ∫⁻ y in Box d, ENNReal.ofReal (C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2) := by
1456        refine lintegral_mono_ae ?_
1457        filter_upwards [hne, ae_restrict_mem (measurableSet_Box d)] with y hy hyB
1458        rw [← ofReal_norm, Real.norm_eq_abs]
1459        exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hbd y hyB hy)
1460    _ = ∫⁻ y in Box d, ‖C1 * ‖y‖ ^ (-((s + d)/2)) + C2‖ₑ := by
1461        refine lintegral_congr fun y => ?_
1462        rw [Real.enorm_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
1463    _ < ∞ := hI.2

1467/-- the constant `A = c (2π)^{s-d} / Γ((d-s)/2)` of the subordination formula -/
1468def subA (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) : ℝ := rieszConst d s * (2 * π) ^ (s - d) / Real.Gamma (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2)

1470theorem integrableOn_psi {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
1471    IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn x t) (Ioi 0) := by
1472  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
1473  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
1474  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := psi_bound x hx
1475  have hcont := psi_continuousOn x
1476  have hmeas : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x t) (volume.restrict (Ioi 0)) := by
1477    refine ContinuousOn.aestronglyMeasurable ?_ measurableSet_Ioi
1478    exact ContinuousOn.mul (continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt ht)) hcont
1479  refine Integrable.mono' ((Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha).const_mul M) hmeas ?_
1480  refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
1481  have ht' : 0 < t := ht
1482  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' _)]
1483  have h1 := hM t ht'
1484  calc t ^ (a - 1) * |psiFn x t| ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (M * Real.exp (-t)) := by gcongr
1485    _ = _ := by ring

1487theorem periodicRiesz_eq_integral {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
1488    (x : Torus d) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
1489    periodicRiesz d s x = subA d s * ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn x t := by
1490  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
1491  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
1492  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := psi_bound x hx
1493  have hcont := psi_continuousOn x
1494  have hmeas : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 ≤ ε → AEStronglyMeasurable (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x (t + ε))
1495      (volume.restrict (Ioi 0)) := by
1496    intro ε hε
1497    refine ContinuousOn.aestronglyMeasurable ?_ measurableSet_Ioi
1498    refine ContinuousOn.mul (continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt ht)) ?_
1499    refine hcont.comp (continuousOn_id.add continuousOn_const) fun t ht => ?_
1500    simp only [mem_Ioi] at ht ⊢
1501    linarith
1502  have hlim : Tendsto (fun ε => ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x (t + ε)) (𝓝[>] 0)
1503      (𝓝 (∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x t)) := by
1504    refine tendsto_integral_filter_of_dominated_convergence
1505      (fun t => M * (Real.exp (-t) * t ^ (a - 1))) ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
1506    · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε using hmeas ε (le_of_lt hε)
1507    · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
1508      refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
1509      have hε' : 0 < ε := hε
1510      have ht' : 0 < t := ht
1511      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' _)]
1512      have h1 := hM (t + ε) (by linarith)
1513      have h2 : Real.exp (-(t + ε)) ≤ Real.exp (-t) := Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by linarith)
1514      have h3 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' (a - 1)
1515      calc t ^ (a - 1) * |psiFn x (t + ε)| ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (M * Real.exp (-t)) := by
1516            gcongr
1517            exact h1.trans (by gcongr)
1518        _ = _ := by ring
1519    · exact (Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha).const_mul M
1520    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
1521      have ht' : 0 < t := ht
1522      refine Tendsto.const_mul _ ?_
1523      have h1 : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => t + ε) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 t) := by
1524        have : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => t + ε) (𝓝 0) (𝓝 (t + 0)) :=
1525          (continuous_const.add continuous_id).tendsto 0
1526        rw [add_zero] at this
1527        exact this.mono_left nhdsWithin_le_nhds
1528      have h2 : ContinuousAt (psiFn x) t :=
1529        (hcont t ht').continuousAt (Ioi_mem_nhds ht')
1530      exact h2.tendsto.comp h1
1531  have h2 : Tendsto (fun ε => heatRiesz d s ε x) (𝓝[>] 0)
1532      (𝓝 (subA d s * ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x t)) := by
1533    refine (hlim.const_mul _).congr' ?_
1534    filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
1535    exact (heatRiesz_eq_integral hsd hε x).symm
1536  exact tendsto_nhds_unique (periodicRiesz_tendsto hd hs0 hsd x hx) h2
