1539theorem pi_hasSum_real (n : ℕ) (f : Fin n → ℤ → ℝ) (hf : ∀ i, Summable fun m => |f i m|) :
1540    HasSum (fun k : Fin n → ℤ => ∏ i, f i (k i)) (∏ i, ∑' m, f i m) := by
1541  have h := (pi_hasSum n (fun i m => (f i m : ℂ)) (fun i => by simpa using hf i)).2
1542  have e1 : (fun k : Fin n → ℤ => ∏ i, ((f i (k i) : ℝ) : ℂ)) = fun k => (((∏ i, f i (k i)) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
1543    funext k; push_cast; rfl
1544  have e2 : (∏ i, ∑' m, ((f i m : ℝ) : ℂ)) = (((∏ i, ∑' m, f i m) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
1545    push_cast; simp
1546  rw [e1, e2] at h
1547  exact Complex.hasSum_ofReal.mp h

1549theorem summable_shift_gauss {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) (y : ℝ) :
1550    Summable fun n : ℤ => Real.exp (-c * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2) := by
1551  refine ((summable_exp_sq (c/2) (by positivity)).mul_left (Real.exp (c * y^2))).of_nonneg_of_le
1552    (fun _ => (Real.exp_pos _).le) fun n => ?_
1553  rw [← Real.exp_add]
1554  apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
1555  nlinarith [sq_nonneg ((n:ℝ) / 2 - y), sq_nonneg ((n:ℝ) - 2 * y)]

1557theorem theta_images {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) :
1558    ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k (boxPt y) =
1559      Real.sqrt (π / u) ^ d *
1560        ∑' m : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ∑ i, (((m i : ℤ) : ℝ) - y i)^2 / u)) := by
1561  rw [theta_prod y hu, Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ,
1562    Fintype.card_fin]
1563  congr 1
1564  have hs : ∀ i, Summable fun n : ℤ => |Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2 / u))| := by
1565    intro i
1566    simp_rw [abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1567    -- Gaussian summable
1568    have : Summable fun n : ℤ => Real.exp (-(π^2 / u) * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2) :=
1569      summable_shift_gauss (by positivity) _
1570    refine this.congr fun n => ?_
1571    congr 1; ring
1572  have h := (pi_hasSum_real d (fun i n => Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2 / u))) hs).tsum_eq
1573  rw [← h]
1574  refine tsum_congr fun m => ?_
1575  rw [← Real.exp_sum, Finset.sum_neg_distrib, ← Finset.sum_div, ← Finset.mul_sum, mul_div_assoc, Finset.sum_div]

1578/-! ### The function `W_s(w) = ∫_0^1 t^{-s/2-1} e^{-w/t} dt` -/
1579
1580def Wfun (s w : ℝ) : ℝ := ∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, t ^ (-s/2 - 1) * Real.exp (-w / t)
1581
1582def Wmeas (s : ℝ) : Measure ℝ :=
1583  (volume.restrict (Ioc (0:ℝ) 1)).withDensity (fun t => ENNReal.ofReal (t ^ (-s/2 - 1)))

1585theorem t_pow_exp_bound {N θ : ℝ} (hN : 0 < N) (hθ : 0 < θ) {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 < t) :
1586    t ^ (-N) * Real.exp (-θ / t) ≤ (N ^ N * Real.exp (-N)) * θ ^ (-N) := by
1587  have h := exp_neg_le_rpow hN (v := θ / t) (by positivity)
1588  rw [Real.div_rpow hθ.le ht.le, Real.rpow_neg hθ.le, Real.rpow_neg ht.le, div_inv_eq_mul,
1589    ← neg_div] at h
1590  rw [Real.rpow_neg ht.le]
1591  calc (t ^ N)⁻¹ * Real.exp (-θ / t) ≤ (t ^ N)⁻¹ * ((N ^ N * Real.exp (-N)) * ((θ ^ N)⁻¹ * t ^ N)) := by
1592        gcongr
1593    _ = _ := by
1594        have : t ^ N ≠ 0 := (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht _).ne'
1595        rw [Real.rpow_neg hθ.le]
1596        field_simp

1598theorem integrableOn_W_integrand (s : ℝ) (hs : 0 ≤ s) {w : ℝ} (hw : 0 < w) :
1599    IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (-s/2 - 1) * Real.exp (-w / t)) (Ioc 0 1) := by
1600  have hN : 0 < s/2 + 1 := by linarith
1601  have hb := fun t (ht : 0 < t) => t_pow_exp_bound hN hw ht
1602  refine Measure.integrableOn_of_bounded (M := (( s/2+1) ^ (s/2+1) * Real.exp (-(s/2+1))) * w ^ (-(s/2+1)))
1603    (by simp) ?_ ?_
1604  · refine Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
1605    fun_prop
1606  · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioc).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
1607    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by have := ht.1; positivity)]
1608    have := hb t ht.1
1609    have e : -s/2 - 1 = -(s/2+1) := by ring
1610    rw [e]; exact this

1612theorem Wfun_eq_mgf (s w : ℝ) :
1613    Wfun s w = ProbabilityTheory.mgf (fun t : ℝ => -1/t) (Wmeas s) w := by
1614  unfold Wfun ProbabilityTheory.mgf Wmeas
1615  rw [integral_withDensity_eq_integral_toReal_smul (by fun_prop) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top)]
1616  refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc fun t ht => ?_
1617  rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht.1 _).le, smul_eq_mul]
1618  congr 2
1619  ring
1620
1621
1622theorem Wfun_eq_mgf' (s : ℝ) : Wfun s = ProbabilityTheory.mgf (fun t : ℝ => -1/t) (Wmeas s) :=
1623  funext (Wfun_eq_mgf s)
1624
1625theorem Ioi_subset_integrableExpSet (s : ℝ) (hs : 0 ≤ s) :
1626    Ioi (0:ℝ) ⊆ interior (ProbabilityTheory.integrableExpSet (fun t : ℝ => -1/t) (Wmeas s)) := by
1627  refine isOpen_Ioi.subset_interior_iff.mpr fun θ hθ => ?_
1628  have hθ' : 0 < θ := hθ
1629  show Integrable (fun t : ℝ => Real.exp (θ * (-1/t))) (Wmeas s)
1630  unfold Wmeas
1631  rw [integrable_withDensity_iff_integrable_smul' (by fun_prop)
1632    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top)]
1633  refine (integrableOn_W_integrand s hs hθ').congr_fun_ae ?_
1634  refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioc).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
1635  simp only
1636  rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht.1 _).le, smul_eq_mul]
1637  congr 2; ring
1638
1639theorem contDiffOn_Wfun (s : ℝ) (hs : 0 ≤ s) : ContDiffOn ℝ ∞' (Wfun s) (Ioi 0) := by
1640  rw [Wfun_eq_mgf']
1641  have := (ProbabilityTheory.analyticOn_mgf (X := fun t : ℝ => -1/t) (μ := Wmeas s)).mono
1642    (Ioi_subset_integrableExpSet s hs)
1643  exact (this.contDiffOn isOpen_Ioi.uniqueDiffOn).of_le (by exact_mod_cast le_top)
1644
1645theorem iteratedDeriv_Wfun (s : ℝ) (hs : 0 ≤ s) (i : ℕ) {w : ℝ} (hw : 0 < w) :
1646    iteratedDeriv i (Wfun s) w =
1647      ∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, t ^ (-s/2 - 1) * ((-1/t) ^ i * Real.exp (-w / t)) := by
1648  rw [Wfun_eq_mgf', ProbabilityTheory.iteratedDeriv_mgf (Ioi_subset_integrableExpSet s hs hw)]
1649  unfold Wmeas
1650  rw [integral_withDensity_eq_integral_toReal_smul (by fun_prop) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top)]
1651  refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc fun t ht => ?_
1652  rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht.1 _).le, smul_eq_mul]
1653  congr 3; ring
1654
1655
1656theorem W_deriv_bound (s : ℝ) (hs : 0 ≤ s) (i : ℕ) :
1657    ∃ B : ℝ, 0 ≤ B ∧ ∀ w : ℝ, 1 ≤ w → |iteratedDeriv i (Wfun s) w| ≤ B * Real.exp (-w / 2) := by
1658  set g : ℝ → ℝ := fun t => t ^ (-s/2 - 1) * ((1 / t) ^ i * Real.exp (-(1/2) / t)) with hg
1659  have hN : 0 < s/2 + 1 + i := by positivity
1660  have hgeq : ∀ t, 0 < t → g t = t ^ (-(s/2 + 1 + i)) * Real.exp (-(1/2) / t) := by
1661    intro t ht
1662    simp only [hg]
1663    rw [one_div, inv_pow, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_neg ht.le, ← mul_assoc,
1664      ← Real.rpow_add ht]
1665    congr 2; ring
1666  have hgi : IntegrableOn g (Ioc 0 1) := by
1667    refine Measure.integrableOn_of_bounded (M := ((s/2+1+i) ^ (s/2+1+i) * Real.exp (-(s/2+1+i))) * (1/2) ^ (-(s/2+1+i)))
1668      (by simp) ?_ ?_
1669    · refine Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
1670      simp only [hg]; fun_prop
1671    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioc).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
1672      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, hgeq t ht.1, abs_of_nonneg (by have := ht.1; positivity)]
1673      exact t_pow_exp_bound hN (by norm_num) ht.1
1674  refine ⟨∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, g t, setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioc fun t ht => ?_, fun w hw => ?_⟩
1675  · have := ht.1; simp only [hg]; positivity
1676  · have hw0 : 0 < w := by linarith
1677    rw [iteratedDeriv_Wfun s hs i hw0, ← Real.norm_eq_abs, mul_comm, ← integral_const_mul]
1678    refine (norm_integral_le_of_norm_le (g := fun t => Real.exp (-w / 2) * g t) (hgi.const_mul _) ?_).trans_eq ?_
1679    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioc).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
1680      have ht0 := ht.1
1681      have ht1 := ht.2
1682      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht0 _), abs_pow,
1683        abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1684      have h1 : |(-1/t)| = 1 / t := by
1685        rw [abs_div, abs_neg, abs_one, abs_of_pos ht0]
1686      rw [h1]
1687      simp only [hg]
1688      have h2 : Real.exp (-w / t) ≤ Real.exp (-w / 2) * Real.exp (-(1/2) / t) := by
1689        rw [← Real.exp_add]
1690        apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
1691        have h3 : w / 2 / t ≥ w / 2 := by
1692          rw [ge_iff_le, le_div_iff₀ ht0]; nlinarith
1693        have h4 : w / 2 / t ≥ (1/2) / t := by
1694          apply div_le_div_of_nonneg_right (by linarith) ht0.le
1695        have e1 : -w / t = -(w/2/t) + -(w/2/t) := by ring
1696        have e2 : -(1/2) / t = -((1/2)/t) := by ring
1697        rw [e1, e2]; linarith
1698      have hp : 0 ≤ t ^ (-s/2 - 1) * (1 / t) ^ i := by positivity
1699      calc t ^ (-s/2 - 1) * ((1 / t) ^ i * Real.exp (-w / t))
1700          ≤ t ^ (-s/2 - 1) * ((1 / t) ^ i * (Real.exp (-w / 2) * Real.exp (-(1/2) / t))) := by gcongr
1701        _ = Real.exp (-w / 2) * (t ^ (-s/2 - 1) * ((1 / t) ^ i * Real.exp (-(1/2) / t))) := by ring
1702    · rw [integral_const_mul]
1703
1704
1705/-- squared distance `|y - m|²` from the lattice point `m` -/
1706def wsq {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) (m : Lattice d) : ℝ := ∑ i, (((m i : ℤ) : ℝ) - y i)^2
1707
1708theorem wsq_nonneg {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) (m : Lattice d) : 0 ≤ wsq y m :=
1709  Finset.sum_nonneg fun _ _ => sq_nonneg _
1710
1711theorem eventually_one_le_wsq {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) :
1712    ∀ᶠ m in (Filter.cofinite : Filter (Lattice d)), 1 ≤ π^2 * wsq y m := by
1713  rw [Filter.eventually_cofinite]
1714  refine Set.Finite.subset (Set.finite_Icc (fun i => ⌊y i⌋ - 1) (fun i => ⌈y i⌉ + 1)) ?_
1715  intro m hm
1716  simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq, not_le] at hm
1717  have hlt : wsq y m < 1 := by
1718    have : π^2 > 1 := by nlinarith [Real.pi_gt_three]
1719    by_contra h
1720    push Not at h
1721    nlinarith [wsq_nonneg y m]
1722  have hi : ∀ i, |((m i : ℤ) : ℝ) - y i| < 1 := by
1723    intro i
1724    have h1 : (((m i : ℤ) : ℝ) - y i)^2 ≤ wsq y m :=
1725      Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun i => (((m i : ℤ) : ℝ) - y i)^2) (fun _ _ => sq_nonneg _)
1726        (Finset.mem_univ i)
1727    have : (((m i : ℤ) : ℝ) - y i)^2 < 1 := h1.trans_lt hlt
1728    exact abs_lt_of_sq_lt_sq' (by simpa using this) zero_le_one |> fun h => abs_lt.mpr h
1729  constructor
1730  · intro i
1731    have := abs_lt.mp (hi i)
1732    have h2 : ((⌊y i⌋ - 1 : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ ((m i : ℤ) : ℝ) := by
1733      push_cast
1734      have := Int.floor_le (y i)
1735      have := Int.lt_floor_add_one (y i)
1736      linarith [(abs_lt.mp (hi i)).1]
1737    exact_mod_cast h2
1738  · intro i
1739    have h2 : ((m i : ℤ) : ℝ) ≤ ((⌈y i⌉ + 1 : ℤ) : ℝ) := by
1740      push_cast
1741      have := Int.le_ceil (y i)
1742      have := Int.ceil_lt_add_one (y i)
1743      linarith [(abs_lt.mp (hi i)).2]
1744    exact_mod_cast h2
1745
1746
1747theorem Wfun_nonneg (s w : ℝ) : 0 ≤ Wfun s w :=
1748  setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioc fun t ht => by
1749    have := ht.1; positivity
1750
1751theorem summable_gauss_wsq {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) :
1752    Summable fun m : Lattice d => Real.exp (-(c * wsq y m)) := by
1753  have hs : ∀ i, Summable fun n : ℤ => |Real.exp (-c * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2)| := by
1754    intro i
1755    simp_rw [abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1756    exact summable_shift_gauss hc _
1757  have := (pi_hasSum_real d (fun i n => Real.exp (-c * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2)) hs).summable
1758  refine this.congr fun m => ?_
1759  rw [← Real.exp_sum, wsq, Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
1760  congr 1
1761  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => by ring
1762
1763theorem summable_Wfun_wsq {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hs : 0 ≤ s) (y : Fin d → ℝ) :
1764    Summable fun m : Lattice d => Wfun s (π^2 * wsq y m) := by
1765  obtain ⟨B, hB0, hB⟩ := W_deriv_bound s hs 0
1766  refine Summable.of_norm_bounded_eventually (g := fun m : Lattice d => B * Real.exp (-(π^2 / 2 * wsq y m)))
1767    ((summable_gauss_wsq y (by positivity)).mul_left B) ?_
1768  filter_upwards [eventually_one_le_wsq y] with m hm
1769  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (Wfun_nonneg _ _)]
1770  have := hB (π^2 * wsq y m) hm
1771  rw [iteratedDeriv_zero, abs_of_nonneg (Wfun_nonneg _ _)] at this
1772  refine this.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
1773  congr 2; ring
1774
1775
1776theorem theta_images_wsq {d : ℕ} (y : Fin d → ℝ) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) :
1777    ∑' k : Lattice d, Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k (boxPt y) =
1778      Real.sqrt (π / u) ^ d * ∑' m : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(π^2 * wsq y m / u)) := by
1779  rw [theta_images y hu]
1780  congr 2
1781  funext m
1782  unfold wsq
1783  rw [← Finset.sum_div, ← mul_div_assoc]
1784
1785theorem boxPt_ne_zero_of_wsq {d : ℕ} {y : Fin d → ℝ} (hy : ∀ m : Lattice d, 0 < wsq y m) :
1786    boxPt y ≠ 0 := by
1787  intro h
1788  have : ∀ i, ∃ n : ℤ, (n:ℝ) = y i := by
1789    intro i
1790    have hi : ((y i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = 0 := congrFun h i
1791    rw [AddCircle.coe_eq_zero_iff] at hi
1792    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hi
1793    exact ⟨n, by simpa using hn⟩
1794  choose m hm using this
1795  have := hy m
1796  have h0 : wsq y m = 0 := by
1797    unfold wsq
1798    exact Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i _ => by rw [hm i]; simp
1799  linarith
1800
1801theorem int_Ioc_psi {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (y : Fin d → ℝ)
1802    (hy : ∀ m : Lattice d, 0 < wsq y m) :
1803    ∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s)/2 - 1) * psiFn (boxPt y) t =
1804      π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * ∑' m : Lattice d, Wfun s (π^2 * wsq y m) - 1 / (((d:ℝ) - s)/2) := by
1805  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
1806  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
1807  set F : Lattice d → ℝ → ℝ := fun m t => t ^ (-s/2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(π^2 * wsq y m) / t) with hF
1808  have hF_int : ∀ m, Integrable (F m) (volume.restrict (Ioc (0:ℝ) 1)) := fun m =>
1809    integrableOn_W_integrand s hs0 (by have := hy m; positivity)
1810  have hF_sum : Summable fun m => ∫ t, ‖F m t‖ ∂(volume.restrict (Ioc (0:ℝ) 1)) := by
1811    refine (summable_Wfun_wsq hs0 y).congr fun m => ?_
1812    unfold Wfun
1813    refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc fun t ht => ?_
1814    simp only [hF]
1815    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by have := ht.1; positivity)]
1816  have hsw := integral_tsum_of_summable_integral_norm hF_int hF_sum
1817  have hW : ∀ m, ∫ t, F m t ∂(volume.restrict (Ioc (0:ℝ) 1)) = Wfun s (π^2 * wsq y m) := fun m => rfl
1818  have hx0 : boxPt y ≠ 0 := boxPt_ne_zero_of_wsq hy
1819  have hpsi_int : IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (boxPt y) t) (Ioc 0 1) :=
1820    (integrableOn_psi hsd (boxPt y) hx0).mono_set Ioc_subset_Ioi_self
1821  have hpow_int : IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1)) (Ioc 0 1) := by
1822    have := (intervalIntegral.intervalIntegrable_rpow' (a := 0) (b := 1) (r := a - 1) (by linarith))
1823    exact (intervalIntegrable_iff_integrableOn_Ioc_of_le zero_le_one).mp this
1824  have hpt : ∀ t ∈ Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (boxPt y) t + t ^ (a - 1) =
1825      π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * ∑' m, F m t := by
1826    intro t ht
1827    have ht0 := ht.1
1828    have h1 := psi_eq (boxPt y) ht0
1829    rw [theta_images_wsq y ht0] at h1
1830    have h3 : 1 + psiFn (boxPt y) t =
1831        Real.sqrt (π / t) ^ d * ∑' m : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(π^2 * wsq y m / t)) :=
1832      h1.symm
1833    have hc0 : t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt π ^ d * t ^ (-(d:ℝ)/2)) = π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * t ^ (-s/2 - 1) := by
1834      have e1 : Real.sqrt π ^ d = π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) := by
1835        rw [Real.sqrt_eq_rpow, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul Real.pi_pos.le]
1836        congr 1; ring
1837      rw [e1, ← mul_assoc, mul_comm (t ^ (a - 1)), mul_assoc, ← Real.rpow_add ht0]
1838      congr 2
1839      rw [ha_def]; ring
1840    calc t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (boxPt y) t + t ^ (a - 1) = t ^ (a - 1) * (1 + psiFn (boxPt y) t) := by ring
1841      _ = t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt (π / t) ^ d * ∑' m : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(π^2 * wsq y m / t))) := by rw [h3]
1842      _ = (t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt π ^ d * t ^ (-(d:ℝ)/2))) * ∑' m : Lattice d, Real.exp (-(π^2 * wsq y m / t)) := by
1843          rw [sqrt_div_pow_eq d ht0]; ring
1844      _ = π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * ∑' m, F m t := by
1845          rw [hc0, mul_assoc, ← tsum_mul_left]
1846          congr 1
1847          refine tsum_congr fun m => ?_
1848          simp only [hF, neg_div]
1849  -- integrate
1850  have hint1 : Integrable (fun t => t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (boxPt y) t + t ^ (a - 1)) (volume.restrict (Ioc (0:ℝ) 1)) :=
1851    hpsi_int.add hpow_int
1852  have hI1 : ∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, (t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn (boxPt y) t + t ^ (a - 1)) =
1853      π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * ∑' m, Wfun s (π^2 * wsq y m) := by
1854    rw [setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc (fun t ht => hpt t ht), integral_const_mul, ← hsw]
1855    rfl
1856  rw [integral_add hpsi_int hpow_int] at hI1
1857  have hI2 : ∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, t ^ (a - 1) = 1 / a := by
1858    rw [← intervalIntegral.integral_of_le zero_le_one, integral_rpow (Or.inl (by linarith))]
1859    simp [Real.zero_rpow ha.ne']
1860  linarith
1861
1862
1863/-! ### The large-time part `F2` -/
1864
1865/-- Fourier coefficients of the large-time part. -/
1866def coefF2 (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (k : Lattice d) : ℝ :=
1867  if k = 0 then 0 else
1868    subA d s * ∫ t in Ioi (1:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)
1869
1870theorem exp_le_large {t m : ℝ} (ht : 1 ≤ t) (hm : 1 ≤ m) :
1871    Real.exp (-t * m) ≤ Real.exp (-m / 2) * Real.exp (-t / 2) := by
1872  rw [← Real.exp_add]
1873  apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
1874  nlinarith [mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.mpr ht) (sub_nonneg.mpr hm)]
1875
1876theorem integrable_gamma_half {a : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) :
1877    IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t / 2)) (Ioi 1) := by
1878  have h := integrableOn_rpow_mul_exp_neg_mul_rpow (p := 1) (s := a - 1) (b := 1/2)
1879    (by linarith) one_pos (by norm_num)
1880  refine (h.mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one)).congr_fun (fun t _ => ?_) measurableSet_Ioi
1881  simp only [Real.rpow_one]
1882  congr 2; ring
1883
1884theorem coefF2_bound {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) :
1885    ∃ C : ℝ, 0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ k : Lattice d, |coefF2 d s k| ≤ C * Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) := by
1886  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
1887  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
1888  set c3 := ∫ t in Ioi (1:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t / 2) with hc3
1889  have hc3_0 : 0 ≤ c3 := setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioi fun t ht => by
1890    have : 0 < t := lt_trans zero_lt_one ht
1891    positivity
1892  refine ⟨|subA d s| * c3, by positivity, fun k => ?_⟩
1893  unfold coefF2
1894  by_cases hk : k = 0
1895  · simp [hk]; positivity
1896  · simp only [hk, ite_false]
1897    have hm := one_le_enorm k hk
1898    have hm2 : 1 ≤ Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 := by nlinarith
1899    have hint : IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) (Ioi 1) := by
1900      refine Integrable.mono' ((integrable_gamma_half ha).const_mul (Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2))) ?_ ?_
1901      · refine Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
1902        fun_prop
1903      · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
1904        have ht1 : 1 ≤ t := le_of_lt ht
1905        have ht0 : 0 < t := lt_trans zero_lt_one ht
1906        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
1907        have := exp_le_large ht1 hm2
1908        calc t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)
1909            ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * Real.exp (-t / 2)) := by gcongr
1910          _ = _ := by ring
1911    rw [abs_mul]
1912    have h2 : |∫ t in Ioi (1:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)| ≤
1913        Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * c3 := by
1914      rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs, hc3, ← integral_const_mul]
1915      refine norm_integral_le_of_norm_le ((integrable_gamma_half ha).const_mul _) ?_
1916      refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
1917      have ht1 : 1 ≤ t := le_of_lt ht
1918      have ht0 : 0 < t := lt_trans zero_lt_one ht
1919      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
1920      have := exp_le_large ht1 hm2
1921      calc t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)
1922          ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * Real.exp (-t / 2)) := by gcongr
1923        _ = _ := by ring
1924    calc |subA d s| * |∫ t in Ioi (1:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)|
1925        ≤ |subA d s| * (Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * c3) := by gcongr
1926      _ = _ := by ring
1927
1928
1929theorem F2_eq {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) (x : Torus d) :
1930    subA d s * ∫ t in Ioi (1:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn x t =
1931      ∑' k : Lattice d, coefF2 d s k * Torus.cosMode k x := by
1932  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
1933  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
1934  set G : Lattice d → ℝ → ℝ := fun k t =>
1935    t ^ (a - 1) * (if k = 0 then 0 else Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * Torus.cosMode k x) with hG
1936  set c3 := ∫ t in Ioi (1:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t / 2) with hc3
1937  have hc3_0 : 0 ≤ c3 := setIntegral_nonneg measurableSet_Ioi fun t ht => by
1938    have : 0 < t := lt_trans zero_lt_one ht
1939    positivity
1940  have hbd : ∀ k : Lattice d, k ≠ 0 → ∀ t ∈ Ioi (1:ℝ),
1941      ‖G k t‖ ≤ Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * (t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t / 2)) := by
1942    intro k hk t ht
1943    have ht1 : 1 ≤ t := le_of_lt ht
1944    have ht0 : 0 < t := lt_trans zero_lt_one ht
1945    have hm := one_le_enorm k hk
1946    have hm2 : 1 ≤ Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 := by nlinarith
1947    simp only [hG, hk, ite_false]
1948    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht0 _),
1949      abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
1950    have := exp_le_large ht1 hm2
1951    have hc := abs_cosMode_le k x
1952    calc t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * |Torus.cosMode k x|)
1953        ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 1) := by gcongr
1954      _ ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * Real.exp (-t / 2)) := by
1955          rw [mul_one]; gcongr
1956      _ = _ := by ring
1957  have hGint : ∀ k, Integrable (G k) (volume.restrict (Ioi (1:ℝ))) := by
1958    intro k
1959    by_cases hk : k = 0
1960    · have : G k = fun _ => 0 := by funext t; simp [hG, hk]
1961      rw [this]; exact integrable_zero _ _ _
1962    · refine Integrable.mono' ((integrable_gamma_half ha).const_mul (Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2))) ?_ ?_
1963      · refine Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
1964        simp only [hG, hk, ite_false]
1965        fun_prop
1966      · exact (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => hbd k hk t ht)
1967  have hGsum : Summable fun k => ∫ t, ‖G k t‖ ∂(volume.restrict (Ioi (1:ℝ))) := by
1968    refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => integral_nonneg fun t => norm_nonneg _) (fun k => ?_)
1969      ((summable_gauss_lattice d (1/2) (by norm_num)).mul_left c3)
1970    by_cases hk : k = 0
1971    · have : ∀ t, ‖G k t‖ = 0 := fun t => by simp [hG, hk]
1972      simp only [this, integral_zero]
1973      exact mul_nonneg hc3_0 (Real.exp_pos _).le
1974    · calc ∫ t, ‖G k t‖ ∂(volume.restrict (Ioi (1:ℝ)))
1975          ≤ ∫ t in Ioi (1:ℝ), Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * (t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t / 2)) :=
1976            setIntegral_mono_on (hGint k).norm ((integrable_gamma_half ha).const_mul _)
1977              measurableSet_Ioi (fun t ht => hbd k hk t ht)
1978        _ = _ := by
1979            rw [integral_const_mul]
1980            have : -(1/2:ℝ) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 = -Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2 := by ring
1981            rw [this]; ring
1982  have hsw := integral_tsum_of_summable_integral_norm hGint hGsum
1983  have hpt : ∀ t : ℝ, ∑' k, G k t = t ^ (a - 1) * psiFn x t := by
1984    intro t
1985    simp only [hG, psiFn]
1986    rw [tsum_mul_left]
1987  have hk : ∀ k, subA d s * ∫ t, G k t ∂(volume.restrict (Ioi (1:ℝ))) = coefF2 d s k * Torus.cosMode k x := by
1988    intro k
1989    by_cases hk0 : k = 0
1990    · have : G k = fun _ => 0 := by funext t; simp [hG, hk0]
1991      rw [this]; simp [coefF2, hk0]
1992    · simp only [coefF2, hk0, ite_false]
1993      have : ∀ t, G k t = (t ^ (a - 1) * Real.exp (-t * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * Torus.cosMode k x := by
1994        intro t; simp only [hG, hk0, ite_false]; ring
1995      simp_rw [this]
1996      rw [integral_mul_const]; ring
1997  simp_rw [hpt] at hsw
1998  rw [← hsw, ← tsum_mul_left]
1999  exact tsum_congr hk

2002theorem norm_iteratedFDeriv_c_cos (c : ℝ) (i : ℕ) (t : ℝ) :
2003    ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (fun s => c * Real.cos s) t‖ ≤ |c| := by
2004  have h1 : (fun s => c * Real.cos s) = c • Real.cos := by funext s; simp
2005  rw [h1, iteratedFDeriv_const_smul_apply Real.contDiff_cos.contDiffAt, norm_smul,
2006      norm_iteratedFDeriv_eq_norm_iteratedDeriv, Real.norm_eq_abs, Real.norm_eq_abs]
2007  calc |c| * |iteratedDeriv i Real.cos t| ≤ |c| * 1 := by
2008        gcongr; exact Real.abs_iteratedDeriv_cos_le_one i t
2009    _ = |c| := mul_one _
2010
2011theorem enorm_pow_gauss_bound (n : ℕ) : ∃ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M ∧ ∀ e : ℝ, 0 ≤ e →
2012    e ^ n * Real.exp (-e ^ 2 / 2) ≤ M * Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) := by
2013  refine ⟨1 + (n.factorial : ℝ) / (1/4) ^ n, by positivity, fun e he => ?_⟩
2014  have h1 : e ^ n ≤ 1 + (e ^ 2) ^ n := by
2015    rcases le_total e 1 with h | h
2016    · have : e ^ n ≤ 1 := pow_le_one₀ he h
2017      have : 0 ≤ (e ^ 2) ^ n := by positivity
2018      linarith
2019    · have : e ^ n ≤ (e ^ 2) ^ n := by
2020        rw [← pow_mul]; exact pow_le_pow_right₀ h (by omega)
2021      linarith
2022  have h2 := pow_exp_bound (1/4) (by norm_num) n (e ^ 2) (by positivity)
2023  have h3 : Real.exp (-e ^ 2 / 2) = Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) := by
2024    rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
2025  have h4 : Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) ≤ 1 := by
2026    rw [Real.exp_le_one_iff]; nlinarith [sq_nonneg e]
2027  have h5 : Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) = Real.exp (-((1/4) * e ^ 2)) := by congr 1; ring
2028  rw [h3]
2029  have hp := Real.exp_pos (-(1/4) * e ^ 2)
2030  calc e ^ n * (Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2))
2031      = (e ^ n * Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2)) * Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) := by ring
2032    _ ≤ ((1 + (e ^ 2) ^ n) * Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2)) * Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) := by gcongr
2033    _ = ((Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) + (e ^ 2) ^ n * Real.exp (-((1/4) * e ^ 2)))) * Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) := by
2034        rw [h5]; ring
2035    _ ≤ (1 + (n.factorial : ℝ) / (1/4) ^ n) * Real.exp (-(1/4) * e ^ 2) := by
2036        gcongr
2037
2038theorem contDiff_F2 {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) :
2039    ContDiff ℝ ∞' (fun x : Euc d => ∑' k : Lattice d, coefF2 d s k * Torus.cosMode k (Torus.mk x)) := by
2040  obtain ⟨C, hC0, hC⟩ := coefF2_bound hsd
2041  have hterm : ∀ k : Lattice d, (fun x : Euc d => coefF2 d s k * Torus.cosMode k (Torus.mk x)) =
2042      (fun u : ℝ => coefF2 d s k * Real.cos u) ∘ Torus.freqFunctional k := by
2043    intro k; funext x
2044    simp [Torus.cosMode_mk, Torus.freqFunctional_apply]
2045  set v : ℕ → Lattice d → ℝ := fun n k => C * Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * (2 * π * Lattice.enorm k) ^ n
2046    with hv
2047  refine contDiff_tsum (v := v) (fun k => ?_) (fun n _ => ?_) (fun n k x _ => ?_)
2048  · rw [hterm k]
2049    exact ((contDiff_const.mul Real.contDiff_cos).comp (Torus.freqFunctional k).contDiff).of_le le_top
2050  · obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := enorm_pow_gauss_bound n
2051    refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => ?_) (fun k => ?_)
2052      ((summable_gauss_lattice d (1/4) (by norm_num)).mul_left (C * (2 * π) ^ n * M))
2053    · have he : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := norm_nonneg _
2054      simp only [hv]; positivity
2055    · have he : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := norm_nonneg _
2056      have := hM _ he
2057      simp only [hv]
2058      calc C * Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * (2 * π * Lattice.enorm k) ^ n
2059          = C * (2 * π) ^ n * (Lattice.enorm k ^ n * Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2)) := by
2060            rw [mul_pow]; ring
2061        _ ≤ C * (2 * π) ^ n * (M * Real.exp (-(1/4) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) := by gcongr
2062        _ = _ := by ring
2063  · rw [hterm k]
2064    have hcd : ContDiff ℝ ⊤ (fun u : ℝ => coefF2 d s k * Real.cos u) := contDiff_const.mul Real.contDiff_cos
2065    refine (Torus.norm_iteratedFDeriv_comp_functional (fun u : ℝ => coefF2 d s k * Real.cos u)
2066      hcd |coefF2 d s k| (fun i t => norm_iteratedFDeriv_c_cos _ i t) _ n x).trans ?_
2067    have h1 : ‖Torus.freqFunctional k‖ ^ n ≤ (2 * π * Lattice.enorm k) ^ n :=
2068      pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) (Torus.norm_freqFunctional_le k) n
2069    have h2 := hC k
2070    show _ ≤ C * Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 / 2) * (2 * π * Lattice.enorm k) ^ n
2071    exact mul_le_mul h2 h1 (by positivity) (by positivity)

2075theorem integral_Ici_one_inv (g : ℝ → ℝ) :
2076    ∫ v in Ici (1:ℝ), g v = ∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, (t ^ 2)⁻¹ * g t⁻¹ := by
2077  have himg : (fun t : ℝ => t⁻¹) '' Ioc 0 1 = Ici 1 := by
2078    ext v
2079    simp only [mem_image, mem_Ioc, mem_Ici]
2080    constructor
2081    · rintro ⟨t, ⟨ht0, ht1⟩, rfl⟩
2082      exact (one_le_inv₀ ht0).mpr ht1
2083    · intro hv
2084      have hv0 : 0 < v := lt_of_lt_of_le zero_lt_one hv
2085      exact ⟨v⁻¹, ⟨inv_pos.mpr hv0, inv_le_one_of_one_le₀ hv⟩, inv_inv v⟩
2086  have h := integral_image_eq_integral_abs_deriv_smul (f := fun t : ℝ => t⁻¹)
2087    (f' := fun t => -(t ^ 2)⁻¹) (s := Ioc 0 1) measurableSet_Ioc
2088    (fun x hx => (hasDerivAt_inv hx.1.ne').hasDerivWithinAt) inv_injective.injOn g
2089  rw [himg] at h
2090  rw [h]
2091  refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc fun t ht => ?_
2092  simp [abs_neg, abs_of_pos (inv_pos.mpr (pow_pos ht.1 2))]

2094theorem Wfun_eq_Ici (s w : ℝ) :
2095    Wfun s w = ∫ v in Ici (1:ℝ), v ^ (s/2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(w * v)) := by
2096  rw [integral_Ici_one_inv]
2097  unfold Wfun
2098  refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc fun t ht => ?_
2099  have ht0 := ht.1
2100  rw [Real.inv_rpow ht0.le, ← Real.rpow_neg ht0.le, ← mul_assoc]
2101  congr 1
2102  · rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_neg ht0.le, ← Real.rpow_add ht0]
2103    congr 1; push_cast; ring
2104  · congr 1; rw [div_eq_mul_inv]; ring

2107/-! ### `H_p(w) = ∫_0^1 v^p e^{-wv} dv`, entire in `w` -/
2108
2109def Hp (p w : ℝ) : ℝ := ∫ v in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, v ^ p * Real.exp (-(w * v))
2110
2111def Hmeas (p : ℝ) : Measure ℝ :=
2112  (volume.restrict (Ioc (0:ℝ) 1)).withDensity (fun v => ENNReal.ofReal (v ^ p))

2114theorem integrableOn_pow_Ioc {p : ℝ} (hp : -1 < p) : IntegrableOn (fun v : ℝ => v ^ p) (Ioc 0 1) := by
2115  have := (intervalIntegral.intervalIntegrable_rpow' (a := 0) (b := 1) (r := p) hp)
2116  exact (intervalIntegrable_iff_integrableOn_Ioc_of_le zero_le_one).mp this

2118theorem integrableOn_Hp_integrand {p : ℝ} (hp : -1 < p) (w : ℝ) :
2119    IntegrableOn (fun v : ℝ => v ^ p * Real.exp (-(w * v))) (Ioc 0 1) := by
2120  refine Integrable.mono' ((integrableOn_pow_Ioc hp).mul_const (Real.exp |w|)) ?_ ?_
2121  · refine Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
2122    fun_prop
2123  · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioc).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun v hv => ?_)
2124    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hv.1 _), abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
2125    have : |w * v| ≤ |w| := by
2126      rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos hv.1]
2127      nlinarith [abs_nonneg w, hv.2, hv.1]
2128    have h2 : Real.exp (-(w * v)) ≤ Real.exp |w| :=
2129      Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by linarith [neg_le_abs (w * v)])
2130    have := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hv.1 p
2131    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 this.le

2133theorem Hp_eq_mgf (p w : ℝ) :
2134    Hp p w = ProbabilityTheory.mgf (fun v : ℝ => -v) (Hmeas p) w := by
2135  unfold Hp ProbabilityTheory.mgf Hmeas
2136  rw [integral_withDensity_eq_integral_toReal_smul (by fun_prop) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top)]
2137  refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc fun t ht => ?_
2138  rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht.1 _).le, smul_eq_mul]
2139  congr 2
2140  ring

2142theorem univ_subset_integrableExpSet_H {p : ℝ} (hp : -1 < p) :
2143    (univ : Set ℝ) ⊆ interior (ProbabilityTheory.integrableExpSet (fun v : ℝ => -v) (Hmeas p)) := by
2144  refine isOpen_univ.subset_interior_iff.mpr fun θ _ => ?_
2145  show Integrable (fun v : ℝ => Real.exp (θ * (-v))) (Hmeas p)
2146  unfold Hmeas
2147  rw [integrable_withDensity_iff_integrable_smul' (by fun_prop)
2148    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun _ => ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top)]

2150  refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioc).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
2151  simp only
2152  rw [ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht.1 _).le, smul_eq_mul]
2153  congr 2; ring

2155theorem contDiff_Hp {p : ℝ} (hp : -1 < p) : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (Hp p) := by
2156  have h1 : Hp p = ProbabilityTheory.mgf (fun v : ℝ => -v) (Hmeas p) := funext (Hp_eq_mgf p)
2157  rw [h1]

2159    (univ_subset_integrableExpSet_H hp)
2160  rw [← contDiffOn_univ]
2161  exact (this.contDiffOn uniqueDiffOn_univ).of_le (by exact_mod_cast le_top)

2164theorem integrableOn_gamma_w {a w : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hw : 0 < w) :

2168  refine h.congr_fun (fun t _ => ?_) measurableSet_Ioi

2172theorem Wfun_pos_eq {s w : ℝ} (hs : 0 < s) (hw : 0 < w) :
2173    Wfun s w = Real.Gamma (s/2) * w ^ (-(s/2)) - Hp (s/2 - 1) w := by
2174  have ha : 0 < s/2 := by linarith
2175  have hint := integrableOn_gamma_w ha hw
2176  have h1 : ∫ v in Ioi (0:ℝ), v ^ (s/2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(w * v)) =
2177      Real.Gamma (s/2) * w ^ (-(s/2)) := by
2178    rw [Real.integral_rpow_mul_exp_neg_mul_Ioi ha hw, one_div, Real.inv_rpow hw.le,
2179      ← Real.rpow_neg hw.le, mul_comm]
2180  have hsplit : ∫ v in Ioi (0:ℝ), v ^ (s/2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(w * v)) =
2181      (∫ v in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, v ^ (s/2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(w * v))) +
2182      ∫ v in Ioi (1:ℝ), v ^ (s/2 - 1) * Real.exp (-(w * v)) := by
2183    rw [← Ioc_union_Ioi_eq_Ioi zero_le_one]
2184    refine setIntegral_union (Ioc_disjoint_Ioi le_rfl) measurableSet_Ioi ?_ ?_
2185    · exact hint.mono_set (Ioc_subset_Ioi_self)
2186    · exact hint.mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one)
2187  rw [Wfun_eq_Ici, integral_Ici_eq_integral_Ioi, Hp]
2188  linarith

2191theorem hasDerivAt_Wfun_zero {w : ℝ} (hw : 0 < w) :
2192    HasDerivAt (Wfun 0) (-(Real.exp (-w) / w)) w := by
2193  have hd : DifferentiableAt ℝ (Wfun 0) w :=
2194    ((contDiffOn_Wfun 0 le_rfl).differentiableOn (by simp)).differentiableAt (Ioi_mem_nhds hw)
2195  have h := hd.hasDerivAt
2196  have e1 : deriv (Wfun 0) w = -(Real.exp (-w) / w) := by
2197    rw [← iteratedDeriv_one, iteratedDeriv_Wfun 0 le_rfl 1 hw]
2198    have e2 : ∀ t ∈ Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, t ^ (-(0:ℝ)/2 - 1) * ((-1/t) ^ 1 * Real.exp (-w / t)) =
2199        -((t ^ 2)⁻¹ * Real.exp (-(w * t⁻¹))) := by
2200      intro t ht
2201      have ht0 := ht.1
2202      have : t ^ (-(0:ℝ)/2 - 1) = t⁻¹ := by
2203        rw [show -(0:ℝ)/2 - 1 = -1 by norm_num, Real.rpow_neg_one]
2204      rw [this]
2205      have : -w / t = -(w * t⁻¹) := by rw [div_eq_mul_inv]; ring
2206      rw [this]
2207      field_simp
2208    rw [setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc e2, integral_neg,
2209      ← integral_Ici_one_inv (fun v => Real.exp (-(w * v))), integral_Ici_eq_integral_Ioi]
2210    have := integral_exp_mul_Ioi (a := -w) (by linarith) 1
2211    simp only [neg_mul] at this
2212    rw [this]
2213    simp only [mul_one]
2214    rw [neg_div_neg_eq]
2215  rw [e1] at h
2216  exact h

2219theorem Hp_zero_pos {w : ℝ} (hw : 0 < w) : Hp 0 w = (1 - Real.exp (-w)) / w := by
2220  unfold Hp
2221  simp only [Real.rpow_zero, one_mul]
2222  rw [← intervalIntegral.integral_of_le zero_le_one]
2223  have : ∀ v : ℝ, Real.exp (-(w * v)) = Real.exp ((-w) * v) := fun v => by rw [neg_mul]
2224  simp_rw [this]
2225  rw [intervalIntegral.integral_comp_mul_left (fun x => Real.exp x) (by linarith : -w ≠ 0)]
2226  simp only [integral_exp, smul_eq_mul, mul_one, mul_zero, Real.exp_zero]
2227  field_simp
2228  ring

2230def Phi (w : ℝ) : ℝ := ∫ u in (0:ℝ)..w, Hp 0 u
2231
2232theorem hasDerivAt_Phi (w : ℝ) : HasDerivAt Phi (Hp 0 w) w :=
2233  ((contDiff_Hp (p := 0) (by norm_num)).continuous.integral_hasStrictDerivAt 0 w).hasDerivAt
2234
2235theorem contDiff_Phi : ContDiff ℝ ∞' Phi := by
2236  rw [contDiff_infty_iff_deriv]
2237  refine ⟨fun w => (hasDerivAt_Phi w).differentiableAt, ?_⟩
2238  have : deriv Phi = Hp 0 := funext fun w => (hasDerivAt_Phi w).deriv
2239  rw [this]
2240  exact contDiff_Hp (by norm_num)

2242theorem Wfun_zero_eq : ∃ S : ℝ → ℝ, ContDiff ℝ ∞' S ∧ ∀ w, 0 < w → Wfun 0 w = -Real.log w + S w := by
2243  set D : ℝ → ℝ := fun w => Wfun 0 w + Real.log w - Phi w with hD
2244  have hder : ∀ w ∈ Ioi (0:ℝ), HasDerivAt D 0 w := by
2245    intro w hw
2246    have hw' : 0 < w := hw
2247    have h1 := ((hasDerivAt_Wfun_zero hw').add (Real.hasDerivAt_log hw'.ne')).sub (hasDerivAt_Phi w)
2248    convert h1 using 1
2249    rw [Hp_zero_pos hw']
2250    field_simp
2251    ring
2252  have hconst : ∀ w ∈ Ioi (0:ℝ), D w = D 1 := by
2253    intro w hw
2254    refine isOpen_Ioi.is_const_of_deriv_eq_zero (isPreconnected_Ioi) ?_ ?_ hw (by simp : (1:ℝ) ∈ Ioi 0)
2255    · exact fun x hx => (hder x hx).differentiableAt.differentiableWithinAt
2256    · exact fun x hx => (hder x hx).deriv
2257  refine ⟨fun w => Phi w + D 1, contDiff_Phi.add contDiff_const, fun w hw => ?_⟩
2258  have := hconst w hw
2259  simp only [hD] at this
2260  linarith

2265def thetaCut (w : ℝ) : ℝ := Real.smoothTransition (4 * w - 1)
2266
2267theorem contDiff_thetaCut : ContDiff ℝ ∞' thetaCut :=
2268  (Real.smoothTransition.contDiff (n := ⊤)).comp ((contDiff_const.mul contDiff_id).sub contDiff_const)
2269
2270theorem thetaCut_eq_one {w : ℝ} (hw : 1/2 ≤ w) : thetaCut w = 1 :=
2271  Real.smoothTransition.one_of_one_le (by linarith)
2272
2273theorem thetaCut_eq_zero {w : ℝ} (hw : w ≤ 1/4) : thetaCut w = 0 :=
2274  Real.smoothTransition.zero_of_nonpos (by linarith)
2275
2276def Wt (s w : ℝ) : ℝ := thetaCut w * Wfun s w
2277
2278theorem contDiff_Wt (s : ℝ) (hs : 0 ≤ s) : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (Wt s) := by
2279  rw [contDiff_iff_contDiffAt]
2280  intro w
2281  by_cases hw : 0 < w
2282  · exact contDiff_thetaCut.contDiffAt.mul ((contDiffOn_Wfun s hs).contDiffAt (Ioi_mem_nhds hw))
2283  · have : Wt s =ᶠ[𝓝 w] fun _ => 0 := by
2284      filter_upwards [Iio_mem_nhds (show w < 1/4 by linarith)] with v hv
2285      simp [Wt, thetaCut_eq_zero (le_of_lt hv)]
2286    exact contDiffAt_const.congr_of_eventuallyEq this
2287
2288theorem Wt_eventuallyEq {s w : ℝ} (hw : 1/2 < w) : Wt s =ᶠ[𝓝 w] Wfun s := by
2289  filter_upwards [Ioi_mem_nhds hw] with v hv
2290  simp [Wt, thetaCut_eq_one hv.le]
2291
2292theorem Wt_deriv_bound (s : ℝ) (hs : 0 ≤ s) (i : ℕ) :
2293    ∃ B : ℝ, 0 ≤ B ∧ ∀ w : ℝ, 1 ≤ w → ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (Wt s) w‖ ≤ B * Real.exp (-w / 2) := by
2294  obtain ⟨B, hB0, hB⟩ := W_deriv_bound s hs i
2295  refine ⟨B, hB0, fun w hw => ?_⟩
2296  have h1 : iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (Wt s) w = iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (Wfun s) w :=
2297    ((Wt_eventuallyEq (s := s) (by linarith)).iteratedFDeriv ℝ i).self_of_nhds
2298  rw [h1, norm_iteratedFDeriv_eq_norm_iteratedDeriv, Real.norm_eq_abs]
2299  exact hB w hw
2300
2301
2302/-! ### The quadratic `q_m` and its derivative bounds -/
2303
2304def qm {d : ℕ} (m : Lattice d) (x : Euc d) : ℝ := π ^ 2 * ‖x - Lattice.toEuc m‖ ^ 2
2305
2306theorem norm_iteratedFDeriv_sub_const_le {d : ℕ} (c : Euc d) (i : ℕ) (x : Euc d) :
2307    ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (fun y : Euc d => y - c) x‖ ≤ 1 + ‖x - c‖ := by
2308  cases i with
2309  | zero =>
2310    rw [norm_iteratedFDeriv_zero]; linarith
2311  | succ j =>
2312    rw [← norm_iteratedFDeriv_fderiv]
2313    have : fderiv ℝ (fun y : Euc d => y - c) = fun _ => ContinuousLinearMap.id ℝ (Euc d) := by
2314      funext y; rw [fderiv_sub_const, fderiv_fun_id]
2315    rw [this]
2316    cases j with
2317    | zero =>
2318      rw [norm_iteratedFDeriv_zero]
2319      have := ContinuousLinearMap.norm_id_le (𝕜 := ℝ) (E := Euc d)
2320      linarith [norm_nonneg (x - c)]
2321    | succ j =>
2322      rw [iteratedFDeriv_succ_const]
2323      simp only [Pi.zero_apply, norm_zero]
2324      positivity
2325
2326theorem norm_iteratedFDeriv_qm_le {d : ℕ} (m : Lattice d) (n : ℕ) (x : Euc d) :
2327    ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ n (qm m) x‖ ≤ π ^ 2 * 2 ^ n * (1 + ‖x - Lattice.toEuc m‖) ^ 2 := by
2328  set B : Euc d →L[ℝ] Euc d →L[ℝ] ℝ := (π ^ 2) • (innerSL ℝ : Euc d →L[ℝ] Euc d →L[ℝ] ℝ) with hB
2329  have hq : qm m = fun y => B (y - Lattice.toEuc m) (y - Lattice.toEuc m) := by
2330    funext y
2331    simp only [qm, B, ContinuousLinearMap.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul]
2332    rw [innerSL_apply_apply, real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq]
2333  have hf : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (fun y : Euc d => y - Lattice.toEuc m) := contDiff_id.sub contDiff_const
2334  rw [hq]
2335  have h1 := B.norm_iteratedFDeriv_le_of_bilinear hf hf x (n := n) (by exact_mod_cast le_top)
2336  have hBn : ‖B‖ ≤ π ^ 2 := by
2337    refine ContinuousLinearMap.opNorm_le_bound _ (by positivity) fun u => ?_
2338    refine ContinuousLinearMap.opNorm_le_bound _ (by positivity) fun v => ?_
2339    rw [hB]
2340    simp only [ContinuousLinearMap.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, norm_mul, Real.norm_eq_abs]
2341    rw [innerSL_apply_apply, abs_of_nonneg (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ π^2), mul_assoc]
2342    gcongr
2343    exact abs_real_inner_le_norm u v
2344  set R := 1 + ‖x - Lattice.toEuc m‖ with hR
2345  have hR0 : 0 ≤ R := by positivity
2346  have hsum : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), (n.choose i : ℝ) *
2347        ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (fun y : Euc d => y - Lattice.toEuc m) x‖ *
2348        ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ (n - i) (fun y : Euc d => y - Lattice.toEuc m) x‖ ≤ 2 ^ n * R ^ 2 := by
2349    calc _ ≤ ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), (n.choose i : ℝ) * (R * R) := by
2350          refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => ?_
2351          rw [mul_assoc]
2352          gcongr
2353          · exact norm_iteratedFDeriv_sub_const_le _ _ _
2354          · exact norm_iteratedFDeriv_sub_const_le _ _ _
2355      _ = 2 ^ n * R ^ 2 := by
2356          rw [← Finset.sum_mul, ← Nat.cast_sum, Nat.sum_range_choose]
2357          push_cast; ring
2358  calc _ ≤ ‖B‖ * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), (n.choose i : ℝ) *
2359        ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (fun y : Euc d => y - Lattice.toEuc m) x‖ *
2360        ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ (n - i) (fun y : Euc d => y - Lattice.toEuc m) x‖ := h1
2361    _ ≤ π ^ 2 * (2 ^ n * R ^ 2) := by gcongr
2362    _ = _ := by ring
2363

2365/-! ### The cutoff `χ` -/
2366
2367def bump (d : ℕ) : ContDiffBump (0 : Euc d) := ⟨1/16, 1/8, by norm_num, by norm_num⟩
2368
2369def chi (d : ℕ) : Euc d → ℝ := fun x => bump d x
2370
2371theorem contDiff_chi {d : ℕ} : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (chi d) := (bump d).contDiff
2372
2373theorem chi_one {d : ℕ} {x : Euc d} (hx : ‖x‖ ≤ 1/16) : chi d x = 1 :=
2374  (bump d).one_of_mem_closedBall (by simpa [bump] using hx)
2375
2376theorem chi_zero {d : ℕ} {x : Euc d} (hx : 1/8 ≤ ‖x‖) : chi d x = 0 :=
2377  (bump d).zero_of_le_dist (by simpa [bump] using hx)
2378
2379theorem iteratedFDeriv_chi_mul_eq_zero {d : ℕ} (G : Euc d → ℝ) {x : Euc d} (hx : 1/8 < ‖x‖) (i : ℕ) :
2380    iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (fun y => chi d y * G y) x = 0 := by
2381  have h : (fun y => chi d y * G y) =ᶠ[𝓝 x] fun _ => (0:ℝ) := by
2382    have : {y : Euc d | 1/8 < ‖y‖} ∈ 𝓝 x :=
2383      (isOpen_lt continuous_const continuous_norm).mem_nhds hx
2384    filter_upwards [this] with y hy
2385    simp [chi_zero (le_of_lt hy)]
2386  rw [(h.iteratedFDeriv ℝ i).self_of_nhds]
2387  simp
2388
2389theorem chi_deriv_bound {d : ℕ} (i : ℕ) : ∃ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M ∧ ∀ x, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (chi d) x‖ ≤ M := by
2390  have : BddAbove (range fun x => ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (chi d) x‖) := by
2391    apply ((contDiff_chi (d := d)).continuous_iteratedFDeriv
2392      (mod_cast le_top)).norm.bddAbove_range_of_hasCompactSupport
2393    apply HasCompactSupport.comp_left _ norm_zero
2394    exact ((bump d).hasCompactSupport).iteratedFDeriv i
2395  obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ := this
2396  exact ⟨max M 0, le_max_right _ _, fun x => (hM (mem_range_self x)).trans (le_max_left _ _)⟩
2397
2398/-! ### Derivative bounds for the image terms -/
2399
2400theorem image_comp_bound {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hs : 0 ≤ s) (n : ℕ) :
2401    ∃ K : ℝ, 0 ≤ K ∧ ∀ m : Lattice d, m ≠ 0 → ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ ≤ 1/4 → ∀ j ≤ n,
2402      ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (fun y => Wt s (qm m y)) x‖ ≤
2403        K * (Lattice.enorm m ^ (2 * n) * Real.exp (-2 * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2)) := by
2404  choose B hB0 hB using Wt_deriv_bound s hs
2405  set BS := ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), B i with hBS
2406  have hBS0 : 0 ≤ BS := Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => hB0 i
2407  have hπ : (9:ℝ) ≤ π ^ 2 := by nlinarith [Real.pi_gt_three]
2408  refine ⟨(n.factorial : ℝ) * BS * (18 * π ^ 2) ^ n, by positivity, fun m hm x hx j hj => ?_⟩
2409  set e := Lattice.enorm m with he
2410  have he1 : 1 ≤ e := one_le_enorm m hm
2411  set w := qm m x with hw
2412  set R := 1 + ‖x - Lattice.toEuc m‖ with hR
2413  have hdist1 : e - 1/4 ≤ ‖x - Lattice.toEuc m‖ := by
2414    have := norm_sub_norm_le (Lattice.toEuc m) x
2415    rw [norm_sub_rev] at this
2416    have : e = ‖Lattice.toEuc m‖ := rfl
2417    linarith
2418  have hdist2 : ‖x - Lattice.toEuc m‖ ≤ e + 1/4 := by
2419    have := norm_sub_le x (Lattice.toEuc m)
2420    have : e = ‖Lattice.toEuc m‖ := rfl
2421    linarith
2422  have hw4 : 4 * e ^ 2 ≤ w := by
2423    have h1 : 3/4 * e ≤ ‖x - Lattice.toEuc m‖ := by linarith
2424    have h2 : (3/4 * e) ^ 2 ≤ ‖x - Lattice.toEuc m‖ ^ 2 := by gcongr
2425    simp only [hw, qm]
2426    nlinarith
2427  have hw1 : 1 ≤ w := by nlinarith
2428  have hR1 : 1 ≤ R := by simp only [hR]; linarith [norm_nonneg (x - Lattice.toEuc m)]
2429  have hR3 : R ≤ 3 * e := by simp only [hR]; linarith
2430  -- Faa di Bruno
2431  have hC : ∀ i, i ≤ j → ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (Wt s) (qm m x)‖ ≤ BS * Real.exp (-w / 2) := by
2432    intro i hi
2433    refine (hB i w hw1).trans ?_
2434    gcongr
2435    exact Finset.single_le_sum (f := B) (fun k _ => hB0 k) (Finset.mem_range.mpr (by omega))
2436  have hD : ∀ i, 1 ≤ i → i ≤ j → ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (qm m) x‖ ≤ (2 * π ^ 2 * R ^ 2) ^ i := by
2437    intro i hi _
2438    have h1 : 1 ≤ π ^ 2 * R ^ 2 := by nlinarith
2439    have h2 : π ^ 2 * 2 ^ i * R ^ 2 ≤ (2 * π ^ 2 * R ^ 2) ^ i := by
2440      rw [show 2 * π ^ 2 * R ^ 2 = 2 * (π ^ 2 * R ^ 2) by ring, mul_pow]
2441      calc π ^ 2 * 2 ^ i * R ^ 2 = 2 ^ i * (π ^ 2 * R ^ 2) := by ring
2442        _ ≤ 2 ^ i * (π ^ 2 * R ^ 2) ^ i := by gcongr; exact le_self_pow₀ h1 (by omega)
2443    exact (norm_iteratedFDeriv_qm_le m i x).trans h2
2444  have hqm : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (qm m) := by
2445    unfold qm
2446    exact contDiff_const.mul ((contDiff_norm_sq ℝ).comp (contDiff_id.sub contDiff_const))
2447  have hFB := norm_iteratedFDeriv_comp_le (contDiff_Wt s hs) hqm (n := j) (by exact_mod_cast le_top) x hC hD
2448  refine (le_of_eq_of_le rfl hFB).trans ?_
2449  have hexp : Real.exp (-w / 2) ≤ Real.exp (-2 * e ^ 2) := by
2450    rw [Real.exp_le_exp]; linarith
2451  have hpow : (2 * π ^ 2 * R ^ 2) ^ j ≤ (18 * π ^ 2) ^ n * e ^ (2 * n) := by
2452    have hb : 1 ≤ 18 * π ^ 2 * e ^ 2 := by nlinarith
2453    have hR9 : R ^ 2 ≤ 9 * e ^ 2 := by nlinarith
2454    have hbase : 2 * π ^ 2 * R ^ 2 ≤ 18 * π ^ 2 * e ^ 2 := by
2455      calc 2 * π ^ 2 * R ^ 2 ≤ 2 * π ^ 2 * (9 * e ^ 2) := by gcongr
2456        _ = 18 * π ^ 2 * e ^ 2 := by ring
2457    calc (2 * π ^ 2 * R ^ 2) ^ j ≤ (18 * π ^ 2 * e ^ 2) ^ j :=
2458          pow_le_pow_left₀ (by positivity) hbase j
2459      _ ≤ (18 * π ^ 2 * e ^ 2) ^ n := pow_le_pow_right₀ hb hj
2460      _ = (18 * π ^ 2) ^ n * e ^ (2 * n) := by rw [mul_pow, pow_mul]
2461  have hfact : (j.factorial : ℝ) ≤ n.factorial := by exact_mod_cast Nat.factorial_le hj
2462  calc (j.factorial : ℝ) * (BS * Real.exp (-w / 2)) * (2 * π ^ 2 * R ^ 2) ^ j
2463      ≤ (n.factorial : ℝ) * (BS * Real.exp (-2 * e ^ 2)) * ((18 * π ^ 2) ^ n * e ^ (2 * n)) := by
2464        gcongr
2465    _ = _ := by ring
2466
2467
2468theorem image_term_bound {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hs : 0 ≤ s) (n : ℕ) :
2469    ∃ K : ℝ, 0 ≤ K ∧ ∀ m : Lattice d, m ≠ 0 → ∀ x : Euc d,
2470      ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ n (fun y => chi d y * Wt s (qm m y)) x‖ ≤
2471        K * (Lattice.enorm m ^ (2 * n) * Real.exp (-2 * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2)) := by
2472  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := image_comp_bound (d := d) hs n
2473  choose M hM0 hM using fun i => chi_deriv_bound (d := d) i
2474  set MS := ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), M i with hMS
2475  have hMS0 : 0 ≤ MS := Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => hM0 i
2476  refine ⟨2 ^ n * MS * K, by positivity, fun m hm x => ?_⟩
2477  set E := Lattice.enorm m ^ (2 * n) * Real.exp (-2 * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2) with hE
2478  have hE0 : 0 ≤ E := by
2479    have : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm m := norm_nonneg _
2480    positivity
2481  by_cases hx : 1/8 < ‖x‖
2482  · rw [iteratedFDeriv_chi_mul_eq_zero _ hx, norm_zero]
2483    positivity
2484  · push Not at hx
2485    have hx4 : ‖x‖ ≤ 1/4 := by linarith
2486    have hqm : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (qm m) := by
2487      unfold qm
2488      exact contDiff_const.mul ((contDiff_norm_sq ℝ).comp (contDiff_id.sub contDiff_const))
2489    have hG : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (fun y => Wt s (qm m y)) := (contDiff_Wt s hs).comp hqm
2490    refine (norm_iteratedFDeriv_mul_le contDiff_chi hG x (n := n) (by exact_mod_cast le_top)).trans ?_
2491    calc ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), (n.choose i : ℝ) * ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ i (chi d) x‖ *
2492          ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ (n - i) (fun y => Wt s (qm m y)) x‖
2493        ≤ ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), (n.choose i : ℝ) * MS * (K * E) := by
2494          refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun i hi => ?_
2495          have hin : i ≤ n := Nat.lt_succ_iff.mp (Finset.mem_range.mp hi)
2496          gcongr
2497          · exact (hM i x).trans (Finset.single_le_sum (f := M) (fun k _ => hM0 k) hi)
2498          · exact hK m hm x hx4 (n - i) (Nat.sub_le n i)
2499      _ = 2 ^ n * MS * K * E := by
2500          rw [← Finset.sum_mul, ← Finset.sum_mul, ← Nat.cast_sum, Nat.sum_range_choose]
2501          push_cast; ring
2502
2503/-- the image terms (with the cutoff), indexed by all of `ℤ^d` (the `m = 0` term is `0`) -/
2504def Tm (d : ℕ) (s : ℝ) (m : Lattice d) (y : Euc d) : ℝ :=
2505  if m = 0 then 0 else chi d y * Wt s (qm m y)
2506
2507theorem contDiff_images {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hs : 0 ≤ s) :
2508    ContDiff ℝ ∞' (fun y : Euc d => ∑' m : Lattice d, Tm d s m y) := by
2509  choose K hK0 hK using fun n => image_term_bound (d := d) hs n
2510  choose M hM0 hM using fun n => enorm_pow_gauss_bound (2 * n)
2511  set v : ℕ → Lattice d → ℝ := fun n m =>
2512    K n * (Lattice.enorm m ^ (2 * n) * Real.exp (-2 * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2)) with hv
2513  have hqm : ∀ m : Lattice d, ContDiff ℝ ∞' (qm m) := by
2514    intro m
2515    unfold qm
2516    exact contDiff_const.mul ((contDiff_norm_sq ℝ).comp (contDiff_id.sub contDiff_const))
2517  refine contDiff_tsum (v := v) (fun m => ?_) (fun n _ => ?_) (fun n m x _ => ?_)
2518  · by_cases hm : m = 0
2519    · have : Tm d s m = fun _ => 0 := by funext y; simp [Tm, hm]
2520      rw [this]; exact contDiff_const
2521    · have : Tm d s m = fun y => chi d y * Wt s (qm m y) := by funext y; simp [Tm, hm]
2522      rw [this]; exact contDiff_chi.mul ((contDiff_Wt s hs).comp (hqm m))
2523  · refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun m => ?_) (fun m => ?_)
2524      ((summable_gauss_lattice d (1/4) (by norm_num)).mul_left (K n * M n))
2525    · have : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm m := norm_nonneg _
2526      simp only [hv]
2527      have := hK0 n
2528      positivity
2529    · have he : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm m := norm_nonneg _
2530      have h1 := hM n _ he
2531      have h2 : Real.exp (-2 * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2) ≤ Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm m ^ 2 / 2) := by
2532        rw [Real.exp_le_exp]; nlinarith [sq_nonneg (Lattice.enorm m)]
2533      simp only [hv]
2534      calc K n * (Lattice.enorm m ^ (2 * n) * Real.exp (-2 * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2))
2535          ≤ K n * (Lattice.enorm m ^ (2 * n) * Real.exp (-Lattice.enorm m ^ 2 / 2)) := by
2536            have := hK0 n
2537            gcongr
2538        _ ≤ K n * (M n * Real.exp (-(1/4) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2)) := by
2539            have := hK0 n
2540            gcongr
2541        _ = _ := by ring
2542  · by_cases hm : m = 0
2543    · have : Tm d s m = fun _ => 0 := by funext y; simp [Tm, hm]
2544      rw [this]
2545      simp only [hv]
2546      rw [iteratedFDeriv_fun_zero]
2547      simp only [Pi.zero_apply, norm_zero]
2548      have : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm m := norm_nonneg _
2549      have := hK0 n
2550      positivity
2551    · have : Tm d s m = fun y => chi d y * Wt s (qm m y) := by funext y; simp [Tm, hm]
2552      rw [this]
2553      exact hK n m hm x

2556/-! ### Periodisation of a function supported near `0` -/
2557
2558theorem toEuc_add {d : ℕ} (a b : Lattice d) :
2559    Lattice.toEuc (a + b) = Lattice.toEuc a + Lattice.toEuc b := by
2560  ext i; simp [Lattice.toEuc]
2561
2562theorem rep_mk_eq {d : ℕ} (y : Euc d) :
2563    ∃ m0 : Lattice d, Torus.rep (Torus.mk y) = y + Lattice.toEuc m0 := by
2564  have h := Torus.mk_rep (Torus.mk y)
2565  have : ∀ i, ∃ n : ℤ, (n : ℝ) = Torus.rep (Torus.mk y) i - y i := by
2566    intro i
2567    have hi := congrFun h i
2568    simp only [Torus.mk] at hi
2569    have : ((Torus.rep (Torus.mk y) i - y i : ℝ) : UnitAddCircle) = 0 := by
2570      rw [AddCircle.coe_sub, hi, sub_self]
2571    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := (AddCircle.coe_eq_zero_iff (1:ℝ)).mp this
2572    exact ⟨n, by simpa using hn⟩
2573  choose m0 hm0 using this
2574  refine ⟨m0, ?_⟩
2575  ext i
2576  simp [Lattice.toEuc, hm0]
2577
2578theorem finite_near {d : ℕ} (y0 : Euc d) :
2579    {n : Lattice d | ‖y0 + Lattice.toEuc n‖ < 1/4}.Finite := by
2580  have h := Filter.eventually_cofinite.mp (eventually_one_le_wsq (d := d) (fun i => -y0 i))
2581  refine h.subset fun n hn => ?_
2582  simp only [Set.mem_setOf_eq] at hn ⊢
2583  intro h1
2584  have hw : wsq (fun i => -y0 i) n = ‖y0 + Lattice.toEuc n‖ ^ 2 := by
2585    rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_sq_eq]
2586    unfold wsq
2587    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
2588    simp [Lattice.toEuc, Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs]
2589    ring
2590  rw [hw] at h1
2591  have : ‖y0 + Lattice.toEuc n‖ ^ 2 < 1/16 := by
2592    have h0 := norm_nonneg (y0 + Lattice.toEuc n)
2593    nlinarith
2594  have hπ : π ^ 2 < 16 := by nlinarith [Real.pi_lt_four, Real.pi_pos]
2595  nlinarith [sq_nonneg ‖y0 + Lattice.toEuc n‖]
2596
2597/-- periodisation `P f (y) = ∑_n f(y + n)` -/
2598def perz {d : ℕ} (f : Euc d → ℝ) (y : Euc d) : ℝ := ∑' n : Lattice d, f (y + Lattice.toEuc n)
2599
2600theorem contDiff_perz {d : ℕ} {f : Euc d → ℝ} (hf : ContDiff ℝ ∞' f)
2601    (hsupp : ∀ y, 1/8 ≤ ‖y‖ → f y = 0) : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (perz f) := by
2602  rw [contDiff_iff_contDiffAt]
2603  intro y0
2604  set S := (finite_near y0).toFinset with hS
2605  have heq : perz f =ᶠ[𝓝 y0] fun y => ∑ n ∈ S, f (y + Lattice.toEuc n) := by
2606    filter_upwards [Metric.ball_mem_nhds y0 (by norm_num : (0:ℝ) < 1/8)] with y hy
2607    unfold perz
2608    refine tsum_eq_sum fun n hn => hsupp _ ?_
2609    simp only [hS, Set.Finite.mem_toFinset, Set.mem_setOf_eq, not_lt] at hn
2610    have h1 : dist y y0 < 1/8 := hy
2611    rw [dist_eq_norm] at h1
2612    have : y0 + Lattice.toEuc n = (y + Lattice.toEuc n) - (y - y0) := by abel
2613    have h2 := norm_sub_le (y + Lattice.toEuc n) (y - y0)
2614    rw [← this] at h2
2615    linarith
2616  refine ContDiffAt.congr_of_eventuallyEq ?_ heq
2617  refine ContDiff.contDiffAt ?_
2618  exact ContDiff.sum fun n _ => hf.comp (contDiff_id.add contDiff_const)
2619
2620theorem perz_eq {d : ℕ} {f : Euc d → ℝ} (hsupp : ∀ y, 1/8 ≤ ‖y‖ → f y = 0) {x : Euc d}
2621    (hx : ‖x‖ < 1/2) : perz f x = f x := by
2622  unfold perz
2623  rw [tsum_eq_single 0]
2624  · have : Lattice.toEuc (0 : Lattice d) = 0 := by ext i; simp [Lattice.toEuc]
2625    rw [this, add_zero]
2626  · intro n hn
2627    refine hsupp _ ?_
2628    have h1 := one_le_enorm n hn
2629    have h2 := norm_sub_norm_le (Lattice.toEuc n) (-x)
2630    have : Lattice.toEuc n - -x = x + Lattice.toEuc n := by abel
2631    rw [this, norm_neg] at h2
2632    have : Lattice.enorm n = ‖Lattice.toEuc n‖ := rfl
2633    linarith
2634
2635theorem periodize {d : ℕ} {f : Euc d → ℝ} (hf : ContDiff ℝ ∞' f)
2636    (hsupp : ∀ y, 1/8 ≤ ‖y‖ → f y = 0) :
2637    ∃ r : Torus d → ℝ, Torus.IsSmooth r ∧ ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < 1/2 → r (Torus.mk x) = f x := by
2638  refine ⟨fun z => perz f (Torus.rep z), ?_, ?_⟩
2639  · have : Torus.lift (fun z => perz f (Torus.rep z)) = perz f := by
2640      funext y
2641      obtain ⟨m0, hm0⟩ := rep_mk_eq y
2642      simp only [Torus.lift, Function.comp]
2643      rw [hm0]
2644      unfold perz
2645      rw [← (Equiv.addLeft m0).tsum_eq (fun n => f (y + Lattice.toEuc n))]
2646      refine tsum_congr fun n => ?_
2647      simp only [Equiv.coe_addLeft, toEuc_add, add_assoc]
2648    unfold Torus.IsSmooth Torus.IsCk
2649    rw [this]
2650    exact contDiff_perz hf hsupp
2651  · intro x hx
2652    obtain ⟨m0, hm0⟩ := rep_mk_eq x
2653    show perz f (Torus.rep (Torus.mk x)) = f x
2654    rw [hm0]
2655    have : perz f (x + Lattice.toEuc m0) = perz f x := by
2656      unfold perz
2657      rw [← (Equiv.addLeft m0).tsum_eq (fun n => f (x + Lattice.toEuc n))]
2658      refine tsum_congr fun n => ?_
2659      simp only [Equiv.coe_addLeft, toEuc_add, add_assoc]
2660    rw [this, perz_eq hsupp hx]
2661

2663/-! ### Assembly of the local decomposition -/
2664
2665theorem wsq_eq_qm {d : ℕ} (y : Euc d) (m : Lattice d) :
2666    π ^ 2 * wsq (fun i => y i) m = qm m y := by
2667  unfold qm wsq
2668  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_sq_eq]
2669  congr 1
2670  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
2671  simp [Lattice.toEuc, Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs]
2672  ring
2673
2674theorem decomp1 {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (y : Euc d)
2675    (hy : ∀ m : Lattice d, 0 < wsq (fun i => y i) m) :
2676    periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk y) =
2677      subA d s * (π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * ∑' m : Lattice d, Wfun s (π^2 * wsq (fun i => y i) m)
2678        - 1 / (((d:ℝ) - s)/2)) +
2679      ∑' k : Lattice d, coefF2 d s k * Torus.cosMode k (Torus.mk y) := by
2680  have hmk : Torus.mk y = boxPt (fun i => y i) := rfl
2681  have hx0 : Torus.mk y ≠ 0 := by rw [hmk]; exact boxPt_ne_zero_of_wsq hy
2682  rw [periodicRiesz_eq_integral hd hs0 hsd _ hx0]
2683  have hint := integrableOn_psi hsd (Torus.mk y) hx0
2684  have hsplit : ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (Torus.mk y) t =
2685      (∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (Torus.mk y) t) +
2686      ∫ t in Ioi (1:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiFn (Torus.mk y) t := by
2687    rw [← Ioc_union_Ioi_eq_Ioi zero_le_one]
2688    exact setIntegral_union (Ioc_disjoint_Ioi le_rfl) measurableSet_Ioi
2689      (hint.mono_set Ioc_subset_Ioi_self) (hint.mono_set (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one))
2690  rw [hsplit, mul_add, F2_eq hsd, hmk, int_Ioc_psi hs0 hsd _ hy]
2691
2692theorem qm_ge {d : ℕ} {m : Lattice d} (hm : m ≠ 0) {y : Euc d} (hy : ‖y‖ ≤ 1/16) :
2693    1/2 ≤ qm m y := by
2694  have h1 := one_le_enorm m hm
2695  have h2 := norm_sub_norm_le (Lattice.toEuc m) y
2696  rw [norm_sub_rev] at h2
2697  have : Lattice.enorm m = ‖Lattice.toEuc m‖ := rfl
2698  have h3 : 15/16 ≤ ‖y - Lattice.toEuc m‖ := by linarith
2699  have hπ : (9:ℝ) ≤ π ^ 2 := by nlinarith [Real.pi_gt_three]
2700  unfold qm
2701  nlinarith [mul_le_mul h3 h3 (by norm_num) (norm_nonneg _)]
2702
2703theorem decomp2 {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) {y : Euc d} (hy : ‖y‖ ≤ 1/16) :
2704    ∑' m : Lattice d, Wfun s (π^2 * wsq (fun i => y i) m) =
2705      Wfun s (π ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ 2) + ∑' m : Lattice d, Tm d s m y := by
2706  rw [(summable_Wfun_wsq hs0 (fun i => y i)).tsum_eq_add_tsum_ite 0]
2707  congr 1
2708  · rw [wsq_eq_qm]
2709    have : Lattice.toEuc (0 : Lattice d) = 0 := by ext i; simp [Lattice.toEuc]
2710    simp only [qm, this, sub_zero]
2711  · refine tsum_congr fun m => ?_
2712    by_cases hm : m = 0
2713    · simp [Tm, hm]
2714    · simp only [Tm, hm, if_false]
2715      rw [chi_one hy, one_mul, wsq_eq_qm, Wt, thetaCut_eq_one (qm_ge hm hy), one_mul]
2716
2717
2718theorem wsq_pos_small {d : ℕ} {x : Euc d} (hx0 : x ≠ 0) (hx : ‖x‖ ≤ 1/16) (m : Lattice d) :
2719    0 < wsq (fun i => x i) m := by
2720  have hπ : 0 < π ^ 2 := by positivity
2721  have h := wsq_eq_qm x m
2722  by_cases hm : m = 0
2723  · have h0 : Lattice.toEuc m = 0 := by ext i; simp [Lattice.toEuc, hm]
2724    have : 0 < qm m x := by
2725      unfold qm; rw [h0, sub_zero]
2726      have := norm_pos_iff.mpr hx0
2727      positivity
2728    rw [← h] at this
2729    exact pos_of_mul_pos_right this hπ.le
2730  · have := qm_ge hm hx
2731    rw [← h] at this
2732    exact pos_of_mul_pos_right (by linarith) hπ.le
2733
2734theorem local_decomp {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
2735    (S : ℝ → ℝ) (hS : ContDiff ℝ ∞' S) (c : ℝ) (sing : ℝ → ℝ)
2736    (hsing : ∀ y : Euc d, y ≠ 0 → ‖y‖ ≤ 1/16 →
2737      subA d s * π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * Wfun s (π ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ 2) = sing ‖y‖ + S (π ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ 2) + c) :
2738    ∃ (r : Torus d → ℝ) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ ∧ Torus.IsSmooth r ∧
2739      ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ → x ≠ 0 → periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk x) = sing ‖x‖ + r (Torus.mk x) := by
2740  set K0 := subA d s * π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) with hK0
2741  set F2e : Euc d → ℝ := fun y => ∑' k : Lattice d, coefF2 d s k * Torus.cosMode k (Torus.mk y)
2742    with hF2e
2743  set f : Euc d → ℝ := fun y =>
2744    chi d y * (S (π ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ 2) + c - subA d s * (1 / (((d:ℝ) - s)/2)) + F2e y) +
2745      K0 * ∑' m : Lattice d, Tm d s m y with hf
2746  have hfC : ContDiff ℝ ∞' f := by
2747    have h1 : ContDiff ℝ ∞' (fun y : Euc d => S (π ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ 2)) :=
2748      hS.comp (contDiff_const.mul (contDiff_norm_sq ℝ))
2749    have h2 : ContDiff ℝ ∞' F2e := contDiff_F2 hsd
2750    exact (contDiff_chi.mul (((h1.add contDiff_const).sub contDiff_const).add h2)).add
2751      (contDiff_const.mul (contDiff_images hs0))
2752  have hfs : ∀ y, 1/8 ≤ ‖y‖ → f y = 0 := by
2753    intro y hy
2754    have hc := chi_zero (d := d) hy
2755    have hT : ∀ m, Tm d s m y = 0 := fun m => by simp [Tm, hc]
2756    simp only [hf, hc, hT, tsum_zero, zero_mul, mul_zero, add_zero]
2757  obtain ⟨r, hr, hrf⟩ := periodize hfC hfs
2758  refine ⟨r, 1/16, by norm_num, hr, fun x hx hx0 => ?_⟩
2759  have hx' : ‖x‖ ≤ 1/16 := hx.le
2760  rw [hrf x (by linarith), decomp1 hd hs0 hsd x (wsq_pos_small hx0 hx'), decomp2 hs0 hx']
2761  have hsx := hsing x hx0 hx'
2762  simp only [hf, hF2e, chi_one hx', one_mul]
2763  rw [hK0] at hsx ⊢
2764  linear_combination hsx
2765

2767theorem subA_const_pos {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hs : 0 < s) (hsd : s < d) :
2768    subA d s * π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * Real.Gamma (s/2) * π ^ (-s) = 1 := by
2769  have hG1 : 0 < Real.Gamma (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2) := Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by linarith)
2770  have hG2 : 0 < Real.Gamma (s / 2) := Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by linarith)
2771  have h2 : (2:ℝ) ^ ((d:ℝ) - s) * 2 ^ (s - d) = 1 := by
2772    rw [← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num)]; simp
2773  have hπ : π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * π ^ (s - d) * π ^ (-s) = 1 := by
2774    rw [← Real.rpow_add Real.pi_pos, ← Real.rpow_add Real.pi_pos, ← Real.rpow_add Real.pi_pos]
2775    ring_nf; simp
2776  unfold subA rieszConst
2777  rw [if_neg (ne_of_gt hs), Real.mul_rpow (by norm_num) Real.pi_pos.le]
2778  field_simp
2779  linear_combination (π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * π ^ (s - d) * π ^ (-s)) * h2 + hπ
2780
2781theorem subA_const_zero {d : ℕ} (hd : 0 < d) :
2782    subA d 0 * π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) = 1/2 := by
2783  have hG : 0 < Real.Gamma ((d:ℝ) / 2) := Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by positivity)
2784  have h2 : (2:ℝ) ^ ((d:ℝ) - 1) * 2 ^ ((0:ℝ) - d) = 1/2 := by
2785    rw [← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num)]; ring_nf; rw [Real.rpow_neg_one]; norm_num
2786  have hπ : π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * π ^ ((0:ℝ) - d) * π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) = 1 := by
2787    rw [← Real.rpow_add Real.pi_pos, ← Real.rpow_add Real.pi_pos]
2788    ring_nf; simp
2789  unfold subA rieszConst
2790  rw [if_pos rfl, Real.mul_rpow (by norm_num) Real.pi_pos.le, sub_zero]
2791  field_simp
2792  linear_combination 2 * (π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) * π ^ ((0:ℝ) - d) * π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2)) * h2 + hπ

2795theorem periodicRiesz_local_singularity' {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 < s) (hsd : s < d) :
2796    ∃ (r : Torus d → ℝ) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ ∧ Torus.IsSmooth r ∧
2797      ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ → x ≠ 0 →
2798        periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk x) = ‖x‖ ^ (-s) + r (Torus.mk x) := by
2799  set K0 := subA d s * π ^ ((d:ℝ)/2) with hK0
2800  refine local_decomp hd hs0.le hsd (fun w => -(K0 * Hp (s/2 - 1) w))
2801    (contDiff_const.mul (contDiff_Hp (by linarith))).neg 0 (fun r => r ^ (-s)) ?_
2802  intro y hy0 _
2803  have hy : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hy0
2804  have hw : 0 < π ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ 2 := by positivity
2805  rw [Wfun_pos_eq hs0 hw]
2806  have hpow : (π ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ 2) ^ (-(s/2)) = π ^ (-s) * ‖y‖ ^ (-s) := by
2807    rw [Real.mul_rpow (by positivity) (by positivity), ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_natCast,
2808      ← Real.rpow_mul Real.pi_pos.le, ← Real.rpow_mul hy.le]
2809    congr 2 <;> push_cast <;> ring
2810  rw [hpow]
2811  have hc := subA_const_pos hs0 hsd
2812  rw [← hK0] at hc
2813  linear_combination ‖y‖ ^ (-s) * hc

2815theorem periodicLog_local_singularity' {d : ℕ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) :
2816    ∃ (r : Torus d → ℝ) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ ∧ Torus.IsSmooth r ∧
2817      ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ → x ≠ 0 →
2818        periodicRiesz d 0 (Torus.mk x) = -Real.log ‖x‖ + r (Torus.mk x) := by
2819  have hd0 : (0:ℝ) < d := by exact_mod_cast (show 0 < d by omega)
2820  obtain ⟨S0, hS0, hW⟩ := Wfun_zero_eq
2821  refine local_decomp hd le_rfl hd0 (fun w => (1/2) * S0 w) (contDiff_const.mul hS0)
2822    (-Real.log π) (fun r => -Real.log r) ?_
2823  intro y hy0 _
2824  have hy : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hy0
2825  have hw : 0 < π ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ 2 := by positivity
2826  rw [hW _ hw, subA_const_zero (by omega),
2827    Real.log_mul (by positivity) (by positivity), Real.log_pow, Real.log_pow]
2828  push_cast
2829  ring

2833theorem theta_deriv_eq (u y : ℝ) (hu : 0 < u) :
2834    ∑' n : ℤ, 2 * π * I * n * Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * y - u * (n:ℂ)^2) =
2835      ((Real.sqrt (π / u) * (2 * π ^ 2 / u) *
2836        ∑' n : ℤ, ((n:ℝ) - y) * Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y)^2 / u)) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
2837  have hπ : (π:ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast Real.pi_ne_zero
2838  have hu' : (u:ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hu.ne'
2839  set τ : ℂ := I * u / π with hτ
2840  have hτ0 : τ ≠ 0 := by rw [hτ]; simp [hπ, hu', Complex.I_ne_zero]
2841  have h1 : (∑' n : ℤ, 2 * π * I * n * Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * y - u * (n:ℂ)^2)) =
2842      jacobiTheta₂' (y:ℂ) τ := by
2843    unfold jacobiTheta₂' jacobiTheta₂'_term jacobiTheta₂_term
2844    refine tsum_congr fun n => ?_
2845    congr 2
2846    rw [hτ]
2847    field_simp
2848    ring_nf
2849    simp [Complex.I_sq]
2850    ring
2851  have him : 0 < (-1 / τ).im := by
2852    rw [hτ]
2853    have : (-1 / (I * (u:ℂ) / π)) = ((π / u : ℝ) : ℂ) * I := by
2854      field_simp
2855      rw [Complex.ofReal_div]
2856      field_simp
2857      ring_nf
2858      simp [Complex.I_sq]
2859    rw [this]
2860    simp only [Complex.mul_im, Complex.ofReal_re, Complex.ofReal_im, Complex.I_re, Complex.I_im]
2861    simp only [mul_one, zero_mul, add_zero]
2862    positivity
2863  have hsum := ((hasSum_jacobiTheta₂'_term ((y:ℂ) / τ) him).sub
2864    ((hasSum_jacobiTheta₂_term ((y:ℂ) / τ) him).mul_left (2 * π * I * y))).tsum_eq
2865  rw [h1, jacobiTheta₂'_functional_equation]
2866  rw [← hsum, ← tsum_mul_left]
2867  have h2 : (-I * τ : ℂ) = ((u / π : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
2868    rw [hτ]; push_cast; field_simp; simp [Complex.I_sq]
2869  have h3 : (((u / π : ℝ) : ℂ)) ^ (1/2 : ℂ) = ((Real.sqrt (u / π) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
2870    rw [show (1/2:ℂ) = ((1/2:ℝ):ℂ) by push_cast; rfl, ← Complex.ofReal_cpow (by positivity),
2871      Real.sqrt_eq_rpow]
2872  rw [h2, h3]
2873  have hs : Real.sqrt (u / π) ≠ 0 := (Real.sqrt_pos.mpr (by positivity)).ne'
2874  have hs' : (1:ℂ) / ((Real.sqrt (u / π) : ℝ) : ℂ) = ((Real.sqrt (π / u) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
2875    have : Real.sqrt (π / u) = 1 / Real.sqrt (u / π) := by
2876      rw [one_div, ← Real.sqrt_inv, inv_div]
2877    rw [this]; push_cast; rfl
2878  rw [hs', Complex.ofReal_mul, Complex.ofReal_tsum, ← tsum_mul_left]
2879  refine tsum_congr fun n => ?_
2880  unfold jacobiTheta₂'_term jacobiTheta₂_term
2881  push_cast
2882  have e1 : Complex.exp (-π * I * (y:ℂ) ^ 2 / τ) * Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * ((y:ℂ) / τ) + π * I * (n:ℂ) ^ 2 * (-1 / τ))
2883      = Complex.exp (-(π ^ 2 * ((n:ℂ) - y) ^ 2 / u)) := by
2884    rw [← Complex.exp_add]
2885    congr 1
2886    rw [hτ]
2887    field_simp
2888    ring_nf
2889  have e2 : (1:ℂ) / τ = -(I * π / u) := by
2890    rw [hτ]; field_simp; ring_nf; simp [Complex.I_sq]
2891  calc ((Real.sqrt (π / u) : ℝ) : ℂ) * Complex.exp (-π * I * (y:ℂ) ^ 2 / τ) / τ *
2892        (2 * π * I * n * Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * ((y:ℂ) / τ) + π * I * (n:ℂ) ^ 2 * (-1 / τ)) -
2893          2 * π * I * y * Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * ((y:ℂ) / τ) + π * I * (n:ℂ) ^ 2 * (-1 / τ)))
2894      = ((Real.sqrt (π / u) : ℝ) : ℂ) * (2 * π * I * ((n:ℂ) - y)) * ((1:ℂ) / τ) *
2895        (Complex.exp (-π * I * (y:ℂ) ^ 2 / τ) * Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * ((y:ℂ) / τ) + π * I * (n:ℂ) ^ 2 * (-1 / τ))) := by
2896        field_simp
2897    _ = _ := by
2898        rw [e1, e2]
2899        field_simp
2900        ring_nf
2901        simp [Complex.I_sq]
2902        ring

2905theorem prod_cexp_eq {d : ℕ} (k : Lattice d) (y : Fin d → ℝ) (u : ℝ) :
2906    ∏ i, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * ((k i : ℤ) : ℂ) * (y i : ℝ) - u * ((k i : ℤ) : ℂ)^2) =
2907      ((Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ) * Torus.mode k (boxPt y) := by
2908  rw [mode_eq_prod]
2909  have h2 : ∏ i, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * ((k i : ℤ) : ℂ) * (y i : ℝ) - u * ((k i : ℤ) : ℂ)^2) =
2910      (∏ i, Complex.exp (-((u:ℂ) * ((k i : ℤ):ℂ)^2))) *
2911        ∏ i, Complex.exp (2 * π * I * (k i : ℤ) * (y i : ℝ)) := by
2912    rw [← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
2913    refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
2914    rw [← Complex.exp_add]
2915    congr 1
2916    ring
2917  rw [h2, ← Complex.exp_sum, Complex.ofReal_exp, enorm_sq]
2918  congr 2
2919  push_cast
2920  rw [neg_mul, Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]

2922theorem abs_mul_exp_le {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) (v : ℝ) :
2923    |v| * Real.exp (-c * v ^ 2) ≤ (1 + 2 / c) * Real.exp (-(c / 2) * v ^ 2) := by
2924  have h1 : |v| ≤ 1 + v ^ 2 := by nlinarith [sq_abs v, sq_nonneg (|v| - 1/2), abs_nonneg v]
2925  have h2 := pow_exp_bound (c / 2) (by positivity) 1 (v ^ 2) (by positivity)
2926  simp only [pow_one, Nat.factorial_one, Nat.cast_one] at h2
2927  have h3 : Real.exp (-c * v ^ 2) = Real.exp (-(c / 2) * v ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(c / 2 * v ^ 2)) := by
2928    rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
2929  have h4 : Real.exp (-(c / 2 * v ^ 2)) ≤ 1 := by
2930    rw [Real.exp_le_one_iff]; have : 0 ≤ c / 2 * v ^ 2 := by positivity
2931    linarith
2932  rw [h3]
2933  have hE := Real.exp_pos (-(c / 2) * v ^ 2)
2934  have hE2 := Real.exp_pos (-(c / 2 * v ^ 2))
2935  calc |v| * (Real.exp (-(c / 2) * v ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(c / 2 * v ^ 2)))
2936      ≤ (1 + v ^ 2) * (Real.exp (-(c / 2) * v ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(c / 2 * v ^ 2))) := by gcongr
2937    _ = Real.exp (-(c / 2) * v ^ 2) * (Real.exp (-(c / 2 * v ^ 2)) + v ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(c / 2 * v ^ 2))) := by
2938        ring
2939    _ ≤ Real.exp (-(c / 2) * v ^ 2) * (1 + 1 / (c / 2)) := by gcongr
2940    _ = _ := by rw [one_div_div]; ring

2942/-- the `j`-th component of the gradient of the heat semigroup part -/
2943def psiG {d : ℕ} (j : Fin d) (x : Torus d) (u : ℝ) : ℝ :=
2944  ∑' k : Lattice d, -(Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (2 * π * ((k j : ℤ) : ℝ)) * Torus.sinMode k x)

2946theorem summable_lin_shift_gauss {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) (y : ℝ) :
2947    Summable fun n : ℤ => |((n:ℝ) - y) * Real.exp (-c * ((n:ℝ) - y) ^ 2)| := by
2948  refine ((summable_shift_gauss (c := c/2) (by positivity) y).mul_left (1 + 2 / c)).of_nonneg_of_le
2949    (fun _ => abs_nonneg _) fun n => ?_
2950  rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
2951  have := abs_mul_exp_le hc ((n:ℝ) - y)
2952  convert this using 3

2954theorem psiG_images {d : ℕ} (j : Fin d) (y : Fin d → ℝ) {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) :
2955    psiG j (boxPt y) u = Real.sqrt (π / u) ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2 / u) *
2956      ∑' m : Lattice d, (((m j : ℤ) : ℝ) - y j) * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * wsq y m / u)) := by
2957  set f : Fin d → ℤ → ℂ := fun i n => (if i = j then 2 * π * I * n else 1) *
2958    Complex.exp (2 * π * I * n * (y i) - u * (n:ℂ)^2) with hf
2959  have hnorm : ∀ i (m : ℤ), ‖Complex.exp (2 * π * I * m * (y i) - u * (m:ℂ)^2)‖ = Real.exp (-u * (m:ℝ)^2) := by
2960    intro i m
2961    rw [Complex.norm_exp]
2962    congr 1
2963    rw [show ((m:ℂ)) = ((m:ℝ):ℂ) by simp]
2964    simp [pow_two, Complex.mul_re]
2965  have hfn : ∀ i, Summable fun m => ‖f i m‖ := by
2966    intro i
2967    by_cases hij : i = j
2968    · have h1 : Summable fun m : ℤ => |(m:ℝ) - 0| * Real.exp (-u * ((m:ℝ) - 0)^2) := by
2969        refine (summable_lin_shift_gauss hu 0).congr fun m => ?_
2970        rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
2971      refine (h1.mul_left (2 * π)).congr fun m => ?_
2972      simp only [hf, hij, if_true, norm_mul, hnorm]
2973      simp [Complex.norm_I, abs_of_pos Real.pi_pos]
2974      ring
2975    · refine (summable_exp_sq u hu).congr fun m => ?_
2976      simp only [hf, hij, if_false, one_mul, hnorm]
2977  obtain ⟨hsum, hhas⟩ := pi_hasSum d f hfn
2978  have hterm : ∀ k : Lattice d,
2979      -(Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (2 * π * ((k j : ℤ) : ℝ)) * Torus.sinMode k (boxPt y)) =
2980      (∏ i, f i (k i)).re := by
2981    intro k
2982    have h1 : ∏ i, f i (k i) = (2 * π * I * ((k j : ℤ) : ℂ)) *
2983        (((Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) : ℝ) : ℂ) * Torus.mode k (boxPt y)) := by
2984      simp only [hf]
2985      rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib, prod_cexp_eq]
2986      congr 1
2987      rw [Finset.prod_ite_eq' Finset.univ j (fun i => 2 * π * I * ((k i : ℤ) : ℂ))]
2988      simp
2989    rw [h1]
2990    simp only [Torus.sinMode, Complex.mul_re, Complex.mul_im, Complex.ofReal_re, Complex.ofReal_im,
2991      Complex.I_re, Complex.I_im]
2992    push_cast
2993    simp only [Complex.ofReal_re, Complex.ofReal_im, Complex.intCast_re, Complex.intCast_im,
2994      Complex.re_ofNat, Complex.im_ofNat]
2995    ring
2996  set T : Fin d → ℝ := fun i => if i = j then
2997      Real.sqrt (π / u) * (2 * π ^ 2 / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, ((n:ℝ) - y i) * Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2 / u))
2998    else Real.sqrt (π / u) * ∑' n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2 / u)) with hT
2999  have hfac : ∀ i, ∑' n, f i n = ((T i : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
3000    intro i
3001    by_cases hij : i = j
3002    · simp only [hf, hT, hij, if_true]
3003      rw [← theta_deriv_eq u (y j) hu]
3004    · simp only [hf, hT, hij, if_false, one_mul]
3005      rw [theta_eq u (y i) hu]
3006  have hpsi : psiG j (boxPt y) u = ∏ i, T i := by
3007    unfold psiG
3008    simp_rw [hterm]
3009    rw [← Complex.re_tsum hsum.of_norm, hhas.tsum_eq]
3010    simp_rw [hfac]
3011    rw [← Complex.ofReal_prod, Complex.ofReal_re]
3012  -- product of real sums
3013  set g : Fin d → ℤ → ℝ := fun i n => Real.sqrt (π / u) *
3014    ((if i = j then (2 * π ^ 2 / u) * ((n:ℝ) - y i) else 1) * Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2 / u))) with hg
3015  have hgs : ∀ i, Summable fun m => |g i m| := by
3016    intro i
3017    have hc : 0 < π ^ 2 / u := by positivity
3018    have e : ∀ n : ℤ, Real.exp (-(π^2 * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2 / u)) = Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / u) * ((n:ℝ) - y i)^2) := by
3019      intro n; congr 1; ring
3020    by_cases hij : i = j
3021    · refine ((summable_lin_shift_gauss hc (y i)).mul_left (Real.sqrt (π / u) * (2 * π ^ 2 / u))).congr fun n => ?_
3022      simp only [hg, hij, if_true, e, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (Real.sqrt_nonneg _),
3023        abs_of_pos (by positivity : (0:ℝ) < 2 * π ^ 2 / u)]
3024      ring
3025    · refine ((summable_shift_gauss hc (y i)).mul_left (Real.sqrt (π / u))).congr fun n => ?_
3026      simp only [hg, hij, if_false, one_mul, e]
3027      rw [abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg (Real.sqrt_nonneg _), abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
3028  have hpr := (pi_hasSum_real d g hgs).tsum_eq
3029  have hTg : ∀ i, T i = ∑' n, g i n := by
3030    intro i
3031    by_cases hij : i = j
3032    · simp only [hT, hg, hij, if_true]
3033      rw [← tsum_mul_left]
3034      exact tsum_congr fun n => by ring
3035    · simp only [hT, hg, hij, if_false, one_mul]
3036      rw [tsum_mul_left]
3037  rw [hpsi]
3038  simp_rw [hTg]
3039  rw [← hpr, ← tsum_mul_left]
3040  refine tsum_congr fun m => ?_
3041  simp only [hg]
3042  rw [Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin,
3043    Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_ite_eq' Finset.univ j, ← Real.exp_sum]
3044  simp only [Finset.mem_univ, if_true]
3045  have : ∑ i, -(π ^ 2 * (((m i : ℤ) : ℝ) - y i) ^ 2 / u) = -(π ^ 2 * wsq y m / u) := by
3046    unfold wsq
3047    rw [Finset.sum_neg_distrib, ← Finset.sum_div, Finset.mul_sum]
3048  rw [this]
3049  ring

3052theorem pow_gauss_le {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) (n : ℕ) : ∃ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M ∧ ∀ e : ℝ, 0 ≤ e →
3053    e ^ n * Real.exp (-c * e ^ 2) ≤ M * Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) := by
3054  refine ⟨1 + (n.factorial : ℝ) / (c/2) ^ n, by positivity, fun e he => ?_⟩
3055  have h1 : e ^ n ≤ 1 + (e ^ 2) ^ n := by
3056    rcases le_total e 1 with h | h
3057    · have : e ^ n ≤ 1 := pow_le_one₀ he h
3058      have : 0 ≤ (e ^ 2) ^ n := by positivity
3059      linarith
3060    · have : e ^ n ≤ (e ^ 2) ^ n := by
3061        rw [← pow_mul]; exact pow_le_pow_right₀ h (by omega)
3062      linarith
3063  have h2 := pow_exp_bound (c/2) (by positivity) n (e ^ 2) (by positivity)
3064  have h3 : Real.exp (-c * e ^ 2) = Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) := by
3065    rw [← Real.exp_add]; congr 1; ring
3066  have h4 : Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) ≤ 1 := by
3067    rw [Real.exp_le_one_iff]; have : 0 ≤ c/2 * e^2 := by positivity
3068    linarith
3069  have h5 : Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) = Real.exp (-((c/2) * e ^ 2)) := by congr 1; ring
3070  rw [h3]
3071  have hp := Real.exp_pos (-(c/2) * e ^ 2)
3072  calc e ^ n * (Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) * Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2))
3073      = (e ^ n * Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2)) * Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) := by ring
3074    _ ≤ ((1 + (e ^ 2) ^ n) * Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2)) * Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) := by gcongr
3075    _ = ((Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) + (e ^ 2) ^ n * Real.exp (-((c/2) * e ^ 2)))) *
3076          Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) := by rw [h5]; ring
3077    _ ≤ (1 + (n.factorial : ℝ) / (c/2) ^ n) * Real.exp (-(c/2) * e ^ 2) := by
3078        gcongr

3080theorem summable_pow_gauss_lattice (d : ℕ) {c : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) (n : ℕ) :
3081    Summable fun k : Lattice d => Lattice.enorm k ^ n * Real.exp (-c * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
3082  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := pow_gauss_le hc n
3083  refine Summable.of_nonneg_of_le (fun k => ?_) (fun k => hM _ (norm_nonneg _))
3084    ((summable_gauss_lattice d (c/2) (by positivity)).mul_left M)
3085  have : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := norm_nonneg _
3086  positivity

3095theorem heatRieszGrad_apply {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (x : Torus d)

3110    have h3 : ‖Lattice.toEuc k‖ = Lattice.enorm k := rfl

3115          Lattice.enorm k := by
