3220theorem heatRieszGrad_eq_integral {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) {ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 < ε) (x : Torus d)
3221    (j : Fin d) :
3222    heatRieszGrad d s ε x j = rieszConst d s * (2 * π) ^ (s - d) / Real.Gamma (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2) *
3223      ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiG j x (t + ε) := by
3224  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
3225  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
3226  have hG : 0 < Real.Gamma a := Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos ha
3227  set A := rieszConst d s * (2 * π) ^ (s - d) / Real.Gamma a with hA
3228  have hterm : ∀ k : Lattice d,
3229      -(rieszCoeff d s k * Real.exp (-ε * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * 2 * π * Torus.sinMode k x) *
3230        ((k j : ℤ) : ℝ) = A * ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), gG j a ε x k t := by
3231    intro k
3232    by_cases hk : k = 0
3233    · rw [hk, gG_zero]; simp [rieszCoeff]
3234    · have h := single_k_integral_c hsd ε (-(2 * π * ((k j : ℤ) : ℝ) * Torus.sinMode k x)) k hk
3235      have : (fun t => gG j a ε x k t) = fun t => t ^ (a - 1) *
3236          (Real.exp (-((t + ε) * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) * -(2 * π * ((k j : ℤ) : ℝ) * Torus.sinMode k x)) := by
3237        funext t; simp only [gG, neg_mul]; ring
3238      rw [this, h]
3239      have hm : 0 < Lattice.enorm k := lt_of_lt_of_le one_pos (one_le_enorm k hk)
3240      have : rieszCoeff d s k = rieszConst d s * ((2 * π) ^ (s - d) * Lattice.enorm k ^ (s - d)) := by
3241        unfold rieszCoeff
3242        simp only [hk, ite_false]
3243        rw [Real.mul_rpow (by positivity) hm.le]
3244      rw [this, hA]
3245      field_simp
3246      rfl
3247  have hsw : ∑' k : Lattice d, ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), gG j a ε x k t =
3248      ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), ∑' k : Lattice d, gG j a ε x k t :=
3249    integral_tsum_of_summable_integral_norm (gG_integrable j ha ε x) (gG_norm_summable j ha hε x)
3250  have hpt : ∀ t : ℝ, ∑' k : Lattice d, gG j a ε x k t = t ^ (a - 1) * psiG j x (t + ε) := by
3251    intro t
3252    unfold gG psiG
3253    rw [tsum_mul_left]
3254  rw [heatRieszGrad_apply hsd hε x j]
3255  simp_rw [hterm]
3256  rw [tsum_mul_left, hsw]
3257  congr 1
3258  exact integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t => hpt t)

3261theorem psiG_term_bound {d : ℕ} (j : Fin d) (x : Torus d) (k : Lattice d) (u : ℝ) :
3262    ‖-(Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (2 * π * ((k j : ℤ) : ℝ)) * Torus.sinMode k x)‖ ≤
3263      2 * π * Lattice.enorm k * Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
3264  rw [norm_neg, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_mul, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _),
3265    abs_of_pos (by positivity : (0:ℝ) < 2 * π)]
3266  have h1 := abs_coord_le_enorm k j
3267  have h2 : |Torus.sinMode k x| ≤ 1 := (Complex.abs_im_le_norm _).trans (norm_mode k x).le
3268  have he : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := norm_nonneg _
3269  calc Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (2 * π * |((k j : ℤ) : ℝ)|) * |Torus.sinMode k x|
3270      ≤ Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (2 * π * Lattice.enorm k) * 1 := by gcongr
3271    _ = _ := by ring

3273theorem psiG_continuousOn {d : ℕ} (j : Fin d) (x : Torus d) : ContinuousOn (psiG j x) (Ioi 0) := by
3274  have key : ∀ u0 : ℝ, 0 < u0 → ContinuousOn (psiG j x) (Ici u0) := by
3275    intro u0 hu0
3276    unfold psiG
3277    refine continuousOn_tsum (u := fun k : Lattice d => 2 * π * (Lattice.enorm k ^ 1 *
3278      Real.exp (-u0 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)))
3279      (fun k => ?_) ((summable_pow_gauss_lattice d hu0 1).mul_left (2 * π)) ?_
3280    · exact (by fun_prop : Continuous fun u : ℝ => -(Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) *
3281          (2 * π * ((k j : ℤ) : ℝ)) * Torus.sinMode k x)).continuousOn
3282    · intro k u hu
3283      have hu' : u0 ≤ u := hu
3284      refine (psiG_term_bound j x k u).trans ?_
3285      have he : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := norm_nonneg _
3286      rw [pow_one, ← mul_assoc]
3287      gcongr
3288  intro u hu
3289  have hu' : (0:ℝ) < u := hu
3290  have := (key (u/2) (by positivity)).continuousAt (Ici_mem_nhds (show u / 2 < u by linarith))
3291  exact this.continuousWithinAt

3293theorem psiG_large_bound {d : ℕ} (j : Fin d) :
3294    ∃ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M ∧ ∀ (x : Torus d) (u : ℝ), 1 ≤ u → |psiG j x u| ≤ M * Real.exp (-u) := by
3295  set G := ∑' k : Lattice d, 2 * π * Real.exp 1 * (Lattice.enorm k ^ 1 * Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2))
3296    with hG
3297  have hGs : Summable fun k : Lattice d => 2 * π * Real.exp 1 *
3298      (Lattice.enorm k ^ 1 * Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) :=
3299    (summable_pow_gauss_lattice d one_pos 1).mul_left _
3300  have hG0 : 0 ≤ G := tsum_nonneg fun k => by
3301    have : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := norm_nonneg _
3302    positivity
3303  refine ⟨G, hG0, fun x u hu => ?_⟩
3304  have hb : ∀ k : Lattice d,
3305      ‖-(Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) * (2 * π * ((k j : ℤ) : ℝ)) * Torus.sinMode k x)‖ ≤
3306        Real.exp (-u) * (2 * π * Real.exp 1 * (Lattice.enorm k ^ 1 * Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2))) := by
3307    intro k
3308    refine (psiG_term_bound j x k u).trans ?_
3309    have he : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm k := norm_nonneg _
3310    by_cases hk : k = 0
3311    · have h0 : Lattice.enorm k = 0 := by
3312        rw [hk]; simp [Lattice.enorm, Lattice.toEuc]; rfl
3313      rw [h0]; simp
3314    · have hm := one_le_enorm k hk
3315      have : Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) ≤ Real.exp (-u) * Real.exp 1 * Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2) := by
3316        rw [← Real.exp_add, ← Real.exp_add]
3317        apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
3318        nlinarith [mul_nonneg (sub_nonneg.mpr hu) (sub_nonneg.mpr (show (1:ℝ) ≤ Lattice.enorm k ^ 2 by nlinarith))]
3319      calc 2 * π * Lattice.enorm k * Real.exp (-u * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)
3320          ≤ 2 * π * Lattice.enorm k * (Real.exp (-u) * Real.exp 1 * Real.exp (-1 * Lattice.enorm k ^ 2)) := by
3321            gcongr
3322        _ = _ := by ring
3323  have h2 := tsum_of_norm_bounded (hGs.mul_left (Real.exp (-u))).hasSum hb
3324  rw [tsum_mul_left, ← hG] at h2
3325  rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs, mul_comm]
3326  exact h2

3329theorem wsq_ge_enorm {d : ℕ} (x : Euc d) (hx : ∀ i, |x i| ≤ 1/2) (m : Lattice d) :
3330    Lattice.enorm m ^ 2 / 4 ≤ wsq (fun i => x i) m := by
3331  rw [enorm_sq, Finset.sum_div]
3332  unfold wsq
3333  exact Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => sq_sub_ge (x i) (hx i) (m i)

3335theorem psiG_small_bound {d : ℕ} (j : Fin d) :
3336    ∃ K : ℝ, 0 ≤ K ∧ ∀ x : Euc d, (∀ i, |x i| ≤ 1/2) → ∀ u : ℝ, 0 < u → u ≤ 1 →
3337      |psiG j (Torus.mk x) u| ≤ Real.sqrt (π / u) ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2 / u) *
3338        (‖x‖ * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 / u)) + K * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * u)))) := by
3339  set c : Lattice d → ℝ := fun m => (Lattice.enorm m + 1) * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / 8) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2)
3340    with hc
3341  have hcs : Summable c := by
3342    have h1 := (summable_pow_gauss_lattice d (c := π ^ 2 / 8) (by positivity) 1).add
3343      (summable_gauss_lattice d (π ^ 2 / 8) (by positivity))
3344    refine h1.congr fun m => ?_
3345    simp only [hc, pow_one]; ring
3346  have hc0 : ∀ m, 0 ≤ c m := fun m => by
3347    have : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm m := norm_nonneg _
3348    simp only [hc]; positivity
3349  refine ⟨∑' m, c m, tsum_nonneg hc0, fun x hx u hu hu1 => ?_⟩
3350  have hmk : Torus.mk x = boxPt (fun i => x i) := rfl
3351  rw [hmk, psiG_images j _ hu]
3352  set a : Lattice d → ℝ := fun m => (((m j : ℤ) : ℝ) - x j) * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * wsq (fun i => x i) m / u))
3353    with ha
3354  have hcoord : ∀ m : Lattice d, |((m j : ℤ) : ℝ) - x j| ≤ Lattice.enorm m + 1 := by
3355    intro m
3356    have h1 := abs_coord_le_enorm m j
3357    have h2 := hx j
3358    calc |((m j : ℤ) : ℝ) - x j| ≤ |((m j : ℤ) : ℝ)| + |x j| := abs_sub _ _
3359      _ ≤ _ := by linarith
3360  have hπ8 : (0:ℝ) < π ^ 2 := by positivity
3361  have habs : ∀ m, |a m| ≤ c m := by
3362    intro m
3363    simp only [ha, hc]
3364    rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
3365    have hw := wsq_ge_enorm x hx m
3366    have hw0 := wsq_nonneg (fun i => x i) m
3367    have hexp : Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * wsq (fun i => x i) m / u)) ≤
3368        Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / 8) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2) := by
3369      rw [Real.exp_le_exp]
3370      have h1 : π ^ 2 * wsq (fun i => x i) m ≤ π ^ 2 * wsq (fun i => x i) m / u := by
3371        rw [le_div_iff₀ hu]
3372        have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hu1 (mul_nonneg hπ8.le hw0)
3373        linarith
3374      nlinarith
3375    have he : 0 ≤ Lattice.enorm m := norm_nonneg _
3376    exact mul_le_mul (hcoord m) hexp (Real.exp_pos _).le (by positivity)
3377  have hsa : Summable a := Summable.of_norm_bounded hcs (fun m => by rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact habs m)
3378  rw [hsa.tsum_eq_add_tsum_ite 0]
3379  have h0 : |a 0| ≤ ‖x‖ * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 / u)) := by
3380    simp only [ha]
3381    have e : π ^ 2 * wsq (fun i => x i) 0 = π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 := by
3382      rw [wsq_eq_qm]
3383      have : Lattice.toEuc (0 : Lattice d) = 0 := by ext i; simp [Lattice.toEuc]
3384      simp only [qm, this, sub_zero]
3385    rw [e, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
3386    gcongr
3387    simp only [Pi.zero_apply, Int.cast_zero, zero_sub, abs_neg]
3388    have := PiLp.norm_apply_le x j
3389    rwa [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
3390  have hrest : |∑' m, (if m = 0 then 0 else a m)| ≤ (∑' m, c m) * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * u))) := by
3391    have hb : ∀ m : Lattice d, ‖(if m = 0 then 0 else a m)‖ ≤ Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * u))) * c m := by
3392      intro m
3393      by_cases hm : m = 0
3394      · simp only [hm, if_true, norm_zero]
3395        exact mul_nonneg (Real.exp_pos _).le (hc0 _)
3396      · simp only [hm, if_false, Real.norm_eq_abs, ha, hc]
3397        rw [abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.exp_pos _)]
3398        have he := one_le_enorm m hm
3399        have hw := wsq_ge_enorm x hx m
3400        have hexp : Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * wsq (fun i => x i) m / u)) ≤
3401            Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * u))) * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / 8) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2) := by
3402          rw [← Real.exp_add, Real.exp_le_exp]
3403          have h1 : 1 / 4 ≤ wsq (fun i => x i) m := by nlinarith
3404          have h2 : wsq (fun i => x i) m / (2 * u) ≥ 1 / (8 * u) := by
3405            rw [ge_iff_le, div_le_div_iff₀ (by positivity) (by positivity)]; nlinarith
3406          have h3 : wsq (fun i => x i) m / (2 * u) ≥ Lattice.enorm m ^ 2 / 8 := by
3407            rw [ge_iff_le, le_div_iff₀ (by positivity)]; nlinarith
3408          have e1 : π ^ 2 * wsq (fun i => x i) m / u =
3409              π ^ 2 * (wsq (fun i => x i) m / (2 * u)) + π ^ 2 * (wsq (fun i => x i) m / (2 * u)) := by
3410            field_simp; ring
3411          have e2 : π ^ 2 / (8 * u) = π ^ 2 * (1 / (8 * u)) := by ring
3412          rw [e1, e2]
3413          nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 hπ8.le, mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h3 hπ8.le]
3414        calc |((m j : ℤ) : ℝ) - x j| * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * wsq (fun i => x i) m / u))
3415            ≤ (Lattice.enorm m + 1) * (Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * u))) *
3416                Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / 8) * Lattice.enorm m ^ 2)) := by
3417              gcongr
3418              exact hcoord m
3419          _ = _ := by ring
3420    have h2 := tsum_of_norm_bounded (hcs.mul_left (Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * u))))).hasSum hb
3421    rw [tsum_mul_left] at h2
3422    rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs, mul_comm]
3423    exact h2
3424  rw [abs_mul]
3425  have hpre : 0 ≤ Real.sqrt (π / u) ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2 / u) := by positivity
3426  rw [abs_of_nonneg hpre]
3427  gcongr
3428  calc |a 0 + ∑' m, (if m = 0 then 0 else a m)| ≤ |a 0| + |∑' m, (if m = 0 then 0 else a m)| :=
3429        abs_add_le _ _
3430    _ ≤ _ := by linarith

3433theorem psiG_bound {d : ℕ} (j : Fin d) (x : Euc d) (hx : ∀ i, |x i| ≤ 1/2) (hx0 : x ≠ 0) :
3434    ∃ M : ℝ, 0 ≤ M ∧ ∀ u : ℝ, 0 < u → |psiG j (Torus.mk x) u| ≤ M * Real.exp (-u) := by
3435  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := psiG_small_bound (d := d) j
3436  obtain ⟨M1, hM10, hM1⟩ := psiG_large_bound (d := d) j
3437  have hxn : 0 < ‖x‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hx0
3438  set c0 := min (π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2) (π ^ 2 / 8) with hc0
3439  have hc0p : 0 < c0 := lt_min (by positivity) (by positivity)
3440  set M0 := π ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2) * (‖x‖ + K) * (((d + 1).factorial : ℝ) / c0 ^ (d + 1)) with hM0
3441  have hM00 : 0 ≤ M0 := by positivity
3442  refine ⟨M0 * Real.exp 1 + M1, by positivity, fun u hu => ?_⟩
3443  rcases le_total u 1 with h1 | h1
3444  · refine (hK x hx u hu h1).trans ?_
3445    set w := 1 / u with hw
3446    have hw0 : 0 < w := by positivity
3447    have hsq : Real.sqrt (π / u) ≤ π * w := by
3448      have h2 : 1 ≤ π / u := by rw [le_div_iff₀ hu]; linarith [Real.pi_gt_three]
3449      have h3 : Real.sqrt (π / u) ≤ π / u := Real.sqrt_le_iff.mpr ⟨by linarith, by nlinarith⟩
3450      calc Real.sqrt (π / u) ≤ π / u := h3
3451        _ = π * w := by rw [hw]; ring
3452    have he1 : Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 / u)) ≤ Real.exp (-(c0 * w)) := by
3453      rw [Real.exp_le_exp, hw]
3454      have : c0 ≤ π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 := min_le_left _ _
3455      have : c0 * (1 / u) ≤ π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 * (1 / u) := by gcongr
3456      have e : π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 / u = π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 * (1 / u) := by ring
3457      linarith
3458    have he2 : Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * u))) ≤ Real.exp (-(c0 * w)) := by
3459      rw [Real.exp_le_exp, hw]
3460      have : c0 ≤ π ^ 2 / 8 := min_le_right _ _
3461      have : c0 * (1 / u) ≤ π ^ 2 / 8 * (1 / u) := by gcongr
3462      have e : π ^ 2 / (8 * u) = π ^ 2 / 8 * (1 / u) := by field_simp
3463      linarith
3464    have hpe := pow_exp_bound c0 hc0p (d + 1) w hw0.le
3465    have hfin : Real.sqrt (π / u) ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2 / u) *
3466        (‖x‖ * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 / u)) + K * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * u)))) ≤ M0 := by
3467      calc Real.sqrt (π / u) ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2 / u) *
3468            (‖x‖ * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 / u)) + K * Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * u))))
3469          ≤ (π * w) ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2 * w) * (‖x‖ * Real.exp (-(c0 * w)) + K * Real.exp (-(c0 * w))) := by
3470            have e : 2 * π ^ 2 / u = 2 * π ^ 2 * w := by rw [hw]; ring
3471            rw [e]
3472            gcongr
3473        _ = π ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2) * (‖x‖ + K) * (w ^ (d + 1) * Real.exp (-(c0 * w))) := by ring
3474        _ ≤ M0 := by rw [hM0]; gcongr
3475    have : 1 ≤ Real.exp 1 * Real.exp (-u) := by
3476      rw [← Real.exp_add]; apply Real.one_le_exp; linarith
3477    nlinarith [Real.exp_pos (-u), Real.exp_pos 1]
3478  · refine (hM1 _ u h1).trans ?_
3479    have : 0 ≤ M0 * Real.exp 1 * Real.exp (-u) := by positivity
3480    nlinarith [Real.exp_pos (-u)]

3483/-- the candidate gradient component `A ∫_0^∞ t^{a-1} ∂_j Ψ(t, x) dt` -/
3484def gradComp {d : ℕ} (s : ℝ) (j : Fin d) (x : Torus d) : ℝ :=
3485  subA d s * ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiG j x t

3487theorem heatGrad_comp_tendsto {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) (x : Euc d) (hx : ∀ i, |x i| ≤ 1/2)
3488    (hx0 : x ≠ 0) (j : Fin d) :
3489    Tendsto (fun ε => heatRieszGrad d s ε (Torus.mk x) j) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 (gradComp s j (Torus.mk x))) := by
3490  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
3491  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
3492  obtain ⟨M, hM0, hM⟩ := psiG_bound j x hx hx0
3493  have hcont := psiG_continuousOn j (Torus.mk x)
3494  have hmeas : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 ≤ ε → AEStronglyMeasurable (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (a - 1) * psiG j (Torus.mk x) (t + ε))
3495      (volume.restrict (Ioi 0)) := by
3496    intro ε hε
3497    refine ContinuousOn.aestronglyMeasurable ?_ measurableSet_Ioi
3498    refine ContinuousOn.mul (continuousOn_id.rpow_const fun t ht => Or.inl (ne_of_gt ht)) ?_
3499    refine hcont.comp (continuousOn_id.add continuousOn_const) fun t ht => ?_
3500    simp only [mem_Ioi] at ht ⊢
3501    linarith
3502  have hlim : Tendsto (fun ε => ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiG j (Torus.mk x) (t + ε)) (𝓝[>] 0)
3503      (𝓝 (∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiG j (Torus.mk x) t)) := by
3504    refine tendsto_integral_filter_of_dominated_convergence
3505      (fun t => M * (Real.exp (-t) * t ^ (a - 1))) ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
3506    · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε using hmeas ε (le_of_lt hε)
3507    · filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
3508      refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
3509      have hε' : 0 < ε := hε
3510      have ht' : 0 < t := ht
3511      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' _)]
3512      have h1 := hM (t + ε) (by linarith)
3513      have h2 : Real.exp (-(t + ε)) ≤ Real.exp (-t) := Real.exp_le_exp.mpr (by linarith)
3514      have h3 := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht' (a - 1)
3515      calc t ^ (a - 1) * |psiG j (Torus.mk x) (t + ε)| ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (M * Real.exp (-t)) := by
3516            gcongr
3517            exact h1.trans (by gcongr)
3518        _ = _ := by ring
3519    · exact (Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha).const_mul M
3520    · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
3521      have ht' : 0 < t := ht
3522      refine Tendsto.const_mul _ ?_
3523      have h1 : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => t + ε) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 t) := by
3524        have : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => t + ε) (𝓝 0) (𝓝 (t + 0)) :=
3525          (continuous_const.add continuous_id).tendsto 0
3526        rw [add_zero] at this
3527        exact this.mono_left nhdsWithin_le_nhds
3528      have h2 : ContinuousAt (psiG j (Torus.mk x)) t :=
3529        (hcont t ht').continuousAt (Ioi_mem_nhds ht')
3530      exact h2.tendsto.comp h1
3531  refine (hlim.const_mul (subA d s)).congr' ?_
3532  filter_upwards [self_mem_nhdsWithin] with ε hε
3533  rw [heatRieszGrad_eq_integral hsd hε]
3534  rfl

3536theorem periodicRieszGrad_eq {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hsd : s < d) (x : Euc d) (hx : ∀ i, |x i| ≤ 1/2)
3537    (hx0 : x ≠ 0) :
3538    periodicRieszGrad d s (Torus.mk x) = WithLp.toLp 2 (fun j => gradComp s j (Torus.mk x)) := by
3539  unfold periodicRieszGrad
3540  apply Tendsto.limUnder_eq
3541  have h : Tendsto (fun ε => WithLp.ofLp (heatRieszGrad d s ε (Torus.mk x))) (𝓝[>] 0)
3542      (𝓝 (fun j => gradComp s j (Torus.mk x))) :=
3543    tendsto_pi_nhds.mpr fun j => heatGrad_comp_tendsto hsd x hx hx0 j
3544  exact ((PiLp.continuous_toLp 2 _).tendsto _).comp h

3547theorem integrableOn_pow_exp_Ioc {N θ : ℝ} (hN : 0 < N) (hθ : 0 < θ) :
3548    IntegrableOn (fun t : ℝ => t ^ (-N) * Real.exp (-θ / t)) (Ioc 0 1) := by
3549  refine Measure.integrableOn_of_bounded (M := (N ^ N * Real.exp (-N)) * θ ^ (-N)) (by simp) ?_ ?_
3550  · refine Measurable.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
3551    fun_prop
3552  · refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioc).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun t ht => ?_)
3553    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by have := ht.1; positivity)]
3554    exact t_pow_exp_bound hN hθ ht.1

3556theorem int_Ioc_pow_exp_le {N θ : ℝ} (hN : 1 < N) (hθ : 0 < θ) :
3557    ∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, t ^ (-N) * Real.exp (-θ / t) ≤ Real.Gamma (N - 1) * θ ^ (-(N - 1)) := by
3558  have hb : 0 < N - 1 := by linarith
3559  have e1 : ∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, t ^ (-N) * Real.exp (-θ / t) =
3560      ∫ v in Ici (1:ℝ), v ^ ((N - 1) - 1) * Real.exp (-(θ * v)) := by
3561    rw [integral_Ici_one_inv]
3562    refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioc fun t ht => ?_
3563    have ht0 := ht.1
3564    rw [Real.inv_rpow ht0.le, ← Real.rpow_neg ht0.le, ← mul_assoc]
3565    congr 1
3566    · rw [← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_neg ht0.le, ← Real.rpow_add ht0]
3567      congr 1; push_cast; ring
3568    · congr 1; rw [div_eq_mul_inv]; ring
3569  rw [e1]
3570  have hint := integrableOn_gamma_w hb hθ
3571  have h2 : ∫ v in Ici (1:ℝ), v ^ ((N - 1) - 1) * Real.exp (-(θ * v)) ≤
3572      ∫ v in Ioi (0:ℝ), v ^ ((N - 1) - 1) * Real.exp (-(θ * v)) := by
3573    rw [integral_Ici_eq_integral_Ioi]
3574    refine setIntegral_mono_set hint ?_ (Filter.Eventually.of_forall (Ioi_subset_Ioi zero_le_one))
3575    refine (ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun v hv => ?_)
3576    have : (0:ℝ) < v := hv
3577    positivity
3578  refine h2.trans (le_of_eq ?_)
3579  rw [Real.integral_rpow_mul_exp_neg_mul_Ioi hb hθ, one_div, Real.inv_rpow hθ.le,
3580    ← Real.rpow_neg hθ.le, mul_comm]
3581

3583theorem subA_nonneg {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) : 0 ≤ subA d s := by
3584  unfold subA
3585  have := rieszConst_pos hd hs0 hsd
3586  have := Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (show 0 < ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 by linarith)
3587  have : 0 < (2 * π) ^ (s - d) := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by positivity) _
3588  positivity

3590theorem gradComp_bound {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (j : Fin d) :
3591    ∃ C1 C2 : ℝ, 0 ≤ C1 ∧ 0 ≤ C2 ∧ ∀ x : Euc d, (∀ i, |x i| ≤ 1/2) → x ≠ 0 →
3592      |gradComp s j (Torus.mk x)| ≤ C1 * ‖x‖ ^ (-s - 1) + C2 := by
3593  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := psiG_small_bound (d := d) j
3594  obtain ⟨M1, hM10, hM1⟩ := psiG_large_bound (d := d) j
3595  set a : ℝ := ((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 with ha_def
3596  have ha : 0 < a := by rw [ha_def]; linarith
3597  set N : ℝ := s / 2 + 2 with hN
3598  have hN1 : 1 < N := by rw [hN]; linarith
3599  set P : ℝ := 2 * π ^ 2 * Real.sqrt π ^ d with hP
3600  have hP0 : 0 ≤ P := by positivity
3601  set A := subA d s with hA
3602  have hA0 : 0 ≤ A := subA_nonneg hd hs0 hsd
3603  set θ2 : ℝ := π ^ 2 / 8 with hθ2
3604  have hθ2p : 0 < θ2 := by positivity
3605  have hG1 : 0 ≤ Real.Gamma (N - 1) := (Real.Gamma_pos_of_pos (by linarith)).le
3606  refine ⟨A * (P * Real.Gamma (N - 1) * π ^ (-(s + 2))),
3607    A * (P * K * (Real.Gamma (N - 1) * θ2 ^ (-(N - 1))) + M1 * Real.Gamma a),
3608    by positivity, by positivity, fun x hx hx0 => ?_⟩
3609  have hxn : 0 < ‖x‖ := norm_pos_iff.mpr hx0
3610  set θ1 : ℝ := π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 with hθ1
3611  have hθ1p : 0 < θ1 := by positivity
3612  set b1 : ℝ → ℝ := fun t => P * ‖x‖ * (t ^ (-N) * Real.exp (-θ1 / t)) +
3613    P * K * (t ^ (-N) * Real.exp (-θ2 / t)) with hb1
3614  have hb1i : IntegrableOn b1 (Ioc 0 1) :=
3615    ((integrableOn_pow_exp_Ioc (by linarith) hθ1p).const_mul _).add
3616      ((integrableOn_pow_exp_Ioc (by linarith) hθ2p).const_mul _)
3617  set B : ℝ → ℝ := fun t => (Ioc (0:ℝ) 1).indicator b1 t + M1 * (Real.exp (-t) * t ^ (a - 1)) with hB
3618  have hBi : IntegrableOn B (Ioi 0) :=
3619    (hb1i.integrable_indicator measurableSet_Ioc).integrableOn.add
3620      ((Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha).const_mul M1)
3621  have hpt : ∀ t ∈ Ioi (0:ℝ), ‖t ^ (a - 1) * psiG j (Torus.mk x) t‖ ≤ B t := by
3622    intro t ht
3623    have ht0 : 0 < t := ht
3624    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_mul, abs_of_pos (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht0 _)]
3625    rcases le_or_gt t 1 with h1 | h1
3626    · have hmem : t ∈ Ioc (0:ℝ) 1 := ⟨ht0, h1⟩
3627      simp only [hB, Set.indicator_of_mem hmem]
3628      have h2 := hK x hx t ht0 h1
3629      have hpow : t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt (π / t) ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2 / t)) = P * t ^ (-N) := by
3630        rw [sqrt_div_pow_eq d ht0, hP, div_eq_mul_inv (2 * π ^ 2), ← Real.rpow_neg_one t]
3631        have : t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt π ^ d * t ^ (-(d:ℝ)/2) * (2 * π ^ 2 * t ^ (-1:ℝ))) =
3632            2 * π ^ 2 * Real.sqrt π ^ d * (t ^ (a - 1) * t ^ (-(d:ℝ)/2) * t ^ (-1:ℝ)) := by ring
3633        rw [this, ← Real.rpow_add ht0, ← Real.rpow_add ht0]
3634        congr 2
3635        rw [hN, ha_def]; ring
3636      have e1 : Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 / t)) = Real.exp (-θ1 / t) := by rw [hθ1, neg_div]
3637      have e2 : Real.exp (-(π ^ 2 / (8 * t))) = Real.exp (-θ2 / t) := by
3638        rw [hθ2]; congr 1; field_simp
3639      rw [e1, e2] at h2
3640      have hp := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht0 (a - 1)
3641      calc t ^ (a - 1) * |psiG j (Torus.mk x) t|
3642          ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt (π / t) ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2 / t) *
3643              (‖x‖ * Real.exp (-θ1 / t) + K * Real.exp (-θ2 / t))) := by gcongr
3644        _ = (t ^ (a - 1) * (Real.sqrt (π / t) ^ d * (2 * π ^ 2 / t))) *
3645              (‖x‖ * Real.exp (-θ1 / t) + K * Real.exp (-θ2 / t)) := by ring
3646        _ = b1 t := by rw [hpow, hb1]; ring
3647        _ ≤ b1 t + M1 * (Real.exp (-t) * t ^ (a - 1)) := by
3648            have : 0 ≤ M1 * (Real.exp (-t) * t ^ (a - 1)) := by positivity
3649            linarith
3650    · have hnmem : t ∉ Ioc (0:ℝ) 1 := fun h => by linarith [h.2]
3651      simp only [hB, Set.indicator_of_notMem hnmem, zero_add]
3652      have h2 := hM1 (Torus.mk x) t h1.le
3653      have hp := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos ht0 (a - 1)
3654      calc t ^ (a - 1) * |psiG j (Torus.mk x) t| ≤ t ^ (a - 1) * (M1 * Real.exp (-t)) := by gcongr
3655        _ = _ := by ring
3656  have hint : |∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (a - 1) * psiG j (Torus.mk x) t| ≤ ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), B t := by
3657    rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]
3658    exact norm_integral_le_of_norm_le hBi
3659      ((ae_restrict_iff' measurableSet_Ioi).mpr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall hpt))
3660  have hBint : ∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), B t = (∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, b1 t) + M1 * Real.Gamma a := by
3661    simp only [hB]
3662    rw [integral_add (hb1i.integrable_indicator measurableSet_Ioc).integrableOn
3663      ((Real.GammaIntegral_convergent ha).const_mul M1), integral_const_mul,
3664      ← Real.Gamma_eq_integral ha, setIntegral_indicator measurableSet_Ioc,
3665      Set.inter_eq_right.mpr Ioc_subset_Ioi_self]
3666  have hb1int : ∫ t in Ioc (0:ℝ) 1, b1 t ≤
3667      P * ‖x‖ * (Real.Gamma (N - 1) * θ1 ^ (-(N - 1))) + P * K * (Real.Gamma (N - 1) * θ2 ^ (-(N - 1))) := by
3668    simp only [hb1]
3669    rw [integral_add ((integrableOn_pow_exp_Ioc (by linarith) hθ1p).const_mul _)
3670      ((integrableOn_pow_exp_Ioc (by linarith) hθ2p).const_mul _), integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul]
3671    gcongr
3672    · exact int_Ioc_pow_exp_le hN1 hθ1p
3673    · exact int_Ioc_pow_exp_le hN1 hθ2p
3674  have hθ1pow : ‖x‖ * θ1 ^ (-(N - 1)) = π ^ (-(s + 2)) * ‖x‖ ^ (-s - 1) := by
3675    rw [hθ1, Real.mul_rpow (by positivity) (by positivity), ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_natCast,
3676      ← Real.rpow_mul Real.pi_pos.le, ← Real.rpow_mul hxn.le]
3677    have e3 : ‖x‖ * (π ^ ((2:ℕ) * -(N - 1) : ℝ) * ‖x‖ ^ ((2:ℕ) * -(N - 1) : ℝ)) =
3678        π ^ ((2:ℕ) * -(N - 1) : ℝ) * (‖x‖ ^ (1:ℝ) * ‖x‖ ^ ((2:ℕ) * -(N - 1) : ℝ)) := by
3679      rw [Real.rpow_one]; ring
3680    rw [e3, ← Real.rpow_add hxn]
3681    congr 2 <;> push_cast <;> rw [hN] <;> ring
3682  unfold gradComp
3683  rw [← hA, abs_mul, abs_of_nonneg hA0]
3684  calc A * |∫ t in Ioi (0:ℝ), t ^ (((d:ℝ) - s) / 2 - 1) * psiG j (Torus.mk x) t|
3685      ≤ A * ((P * ‖x‖ * (Real.Gamma (N - 1) * θ1 ^ (-(N - 1))) +
3686          P * K * (Real.Gamma (N - 1) * θ2 ^ (-(N - 1)))) + M1 * Real.Gamma a) := by
3687        gcongr
3688        exact hint.trans (hBint ▸ by linarith)
3689    _ = A * (P * Real.Gamma (N - 1) * (‖x‖ * θ1 ^ (-(N - 1)))) +
3690          A * (P * K * (Real.Gamma (N - 1) * θ2 ^ (-(N - 1))) + M1 * Real.Gamma a) := by ring
3691    _ = _ := by rw [hθ1pow]; ring
3692

3694theorem norm_toLp_le_sum {d : ℕ} (v : Fin d → ℝ) :
3695    ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 v : Euc d)‖ ≤ ∑ j, |v j| := by
3696  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
3697  rw [Real.sqrt_le_left (Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => abs_nonneg _)]
3698  have := Finset.sum_sq_le_sq_sum_of_nonneg (s := Finset.univ) (f := fun j => |v j|)
3699    (fun j _ => abs_nonneg _)
3700  simpa [Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs] using this

3702theorem periodicRiesz_grad_bound' {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ} (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
3703    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ x : Euc d, x ≠ 0 → (∀ i, |x i| ≤ 1 / 2) →
3704      ‖periodicRieszGrad d s (Torus.mk x)‖ ≤ C * (‖x‖ ^ (-s - 1) + 1) := by
3705  choose C1 C2 hC1 hC2 hC using fun j => gradComp_bound hd hs0 hsd j
3706  refine ⟨∑ j, (C1 j + C2 j), fun x hx0 hx => ?_⟩
3707  rw [periodicRieszGrad_eq hsd x hx hx0]
3708  refine (norm_toLp_le_sum _).trans ?_
3709  have hr : 0 ≤ ‖x‖ ^ (-s - 1) := Real.rpow_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) _
3710  rw [Finset.sum_mul]
3711  refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun j _ => (hC j x hx hx0).trans ?_
3712  have := hC1 j
3713  have := hC2 j
3714  nlinarith
3715

3727theorem integral_periodicRiesz (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
3728    Integrable (periodicRiesz d s) ∧ ∫ x, periodicRiesz d s x = 0 := by
3729  refine ⟨integrable_periodicRiesz hd hs0 hsd, ?_⟩
3730  have h := integral_periodicRiesz_mode hd hs0 hsd 0
3731  simp only [neg_zero, Torus.mode_zero, one_mul] at h
3732  rw [integral_complex_ofReal] at h
3733  have h0 : rieszCoeff d s 0 = 0 := by simp [rieszCoeff]
3734  rw [h0] at h
3735  exact_mod_cast h

3738/-- `g` is smooth away from `0` and its Fourier coefficients are `ĝ` (KERN-01). -/
3739theorem periodicRiesz_fourierCoeff (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (k : Lattice d) :
3740    Torus.funFourierCoeff (fun x => (periodicRiesz d s x : ℂ)) k = rieszCoeff d s k := by
3741  have h := integral_periodicRiesz_mode hd hs0 hsd k
3742  unfold Torus.funFourierCoeff UnitAddTorus.mFourierCoeff
3743  rw [Torus.volume_eq_pi_haar] at h
3744  simp only [smul_eq_mul]
3745  exact h

3748/-- Local normalisation `H:eq:intro-kernel` (lines 181-186): `g(x) = |x|^{-s} + O(1)` for
3749`0 < s < d`, with the regular part smooth near the origin (KERN-01). -/
3750theorem periodicRiesz_local_singularity (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 < s) (hsd : s < d) :
3751    ∃ (r : Torus d → ℝ) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ ∧ Torus.IsSmooth r ∧
3752      ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ → x ≠ 0 →
3753        periodicRiesz d s (Torus.mk x) = ‖x‖ ^ (-s) + r (Torus.mk x) :=
3754  periodicRiesz_local_singularity' hd hs0 hsd
3755
3756/-- Local normalisation in the logarithmic case: `g(x) = -log|x| + O(1)` (KERN-01). -/
3757theorem periodicLog_local_singularity (hd : 2 ≤ d) :
3758    ∃ (r : Torus d → ℝ) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ ∧ Torus.IsSmooth r ∧
3759      ∀ x : Euc d, ‖x‖ < δ → x ≠ 0 →
3760        periodicRiesz d 0 (Torus.mk x) = -Real.log ‖x‖ + r (Torus.mk x) :=
3761  periodicLog_local_singularity' hd
3762
3763/-- Global bounds `|∇^{⊗n} g(x)| ≤ C_n (|x|^{-s-n} + 1)` away from `0`, for the
3764representative in `(-1/2, 1/2]^d` (KERN-01; cf. Lemma C.2 for the mollified version). -/
3765theorem periodicRiesz_grad_bound (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) :
3766    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ x : Euc d, x ≠ 0 → (∀ i, |x i| ≤ 1 / 2) →
3767      ‖periodicRieszGrad d s (Torus.mk x)‖ ≤ C * (‖x‖ ^ (-s - 1) + 1) :=
3768  periodicRiesz_grad_bound' hd hs0 hsd

3785theorem lintegral_conv_le (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) (F : Torus d → ℝ≥0∞) (x : Torus d) :
3786    ∫⁻ y, F (x - y) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ y, F y := by

3793theorem convMeasure_bounded' (g : Torus d → ℝ) (hg : Integrable g) (ρ : Torus d → ℝ)
3794    (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) :
3795    ∃ C, ∀ x, |convMeasure g (withDensityReal ρ) x| ≤ C := by
3796  refine ⟨(ENNReal.ofReal Λ * ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal ‖g y‖).toReal, fun x => ?_⟩
3797  have h1 := norm_integral_le_lintegral_norm (μ := withDensityReal ρ) (fun y => g (x - y))
3798  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at h1
3799  refine h1.trans (ENNReal.toReal_mono ?_ (lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ hΛ (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal ‖g z‖) x))
3800  refine ENNReal.mul_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top ?_
3801  have := hg.hasFiniteIntegral
3802  unfold HasFiniteIntegral at this
3803  simp only [← ofReal_norm] at this
3804  exact this.ne

3806theorem norm_toLp_le_card_mul (h : 0 < d) (y : Fin d → ℝ) : ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d)‖ ≤ d * ‖y‖ := by
3807  rw [EuclideanSpace.norm_eq]
3808  have h1 : ∀ i, ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) i‖ ^ 2 ≤ ‖y‖ ^ 2 := fun i => by
3809    have := norm_le_pi_norm y i
3810    exact pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) this 2
3811  have h2 : ∑ i, ‖(WithLp.toLp 2 y : Euc d) i‖ ^ 2 ≤ (d : ℝ) * ‖y‖ ^ 2 := by
3812    calc _ ≤ ∑ _i : Fin d, ‖y‖ ^ 2 := Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => h1 i
3813      _ = _ := by simp
3814  have hd1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ d := by exact_mod_cast h
3815  calc Real.sqrt _ ≤ Real.sqrt ((d : ℝ) ^ 2 * ‖y‖ ^ 2) := by
3816        apply Real.sqrt_le_sqrt
3817        nlinarith [sq_nonneg ‖y‖, mul_nonneg (sq_nonneg ‖y‖) (sub_nonneg.2 hd1)]
3818    _ = d * ‖y‖ := by
3819        rw [← mul_pow, Real.sqrt_sq (by positivity)]

3826theorem lintegral_exp_abs_log_lt_top (hd : 2 ≤ d) {a : ℝ} (ha0 : 0 < a) (had : a < d) :
3827    ∫⁻ x : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 x|)) < ∞ := by
3828  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
3829  have hdR : (2 : ℝ) ≤ d := by exact_mod_cast hd
3830  obtain ⟨r, δ, hδ, hr, hloc⟩ := periodicLog_local_singularity (d := d) hd
3831  obtain ⟨R, hR⟩ := (isCompact_closedBall (0 : Euc d) 1).exists_bound_of_continuousOn
3832    (f := Torus.lift r) (show ContDiff ℝ ((⊤ : ℕ∞) : WithTop ℕ∞) (Torus.lift r) from hr).continuous.continuousOn
3833  obtain ⟨C1, C2, hC1, hC2, hbd⟩ := periodicRiesz_box_bound (d := d) (s := 0) hd le_rfl
3834    (by exact_mod_cast hd0)
3835  set δ' : ℝ := min δ 1 with hδ'
3836  have hδ'0 : 0 < δ' := lt_min hδ one_pos
3837  set θ : ℝ := δ' / d with hθ
3838  have hθ0 : 0 < θ := by positivity
3839  set M : ℝ := C1 * θ ^ (-((0 + (d:ℝ)) / 2)) + C2 with hM
3840  set B : ℝ := Real.exp (a * M) + Real.exp (a * R) * 1 with hB
3841  have hI : IntegrableOn (fun y : Fin d → ℝ => Real.exp (a * M) + Real.exp (a * R) * ‖y‖ ^ (-a)) (Box d) :=
3842    (integrableOn_const (measure_Box_ne_top d)).add
3843      ((integrableOn_Box_rpow hd0 had).const_mul _)
3844  have hne : ∀ᵐ y ∂(volume.restrict (Box d)), y ≠ 0 := ae_restrict_of_ae (ae_ne_pi hd0)
3845  rw [lintegral_torus_box (fun x => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 x|)))]
3846  calc ∫⁻ y in Box d, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 (boxPt y)|))
3847      ≤ ∫⁻ y in Box d, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * M) + Real.exp (a * R) * ‖y‖ ^ (-a)) := by
3848        refine lintegral_mono_ae ?_
3849        filter_upwards [hne, ae_restrict_mem (measurableSet_Box d)] with y hy hyB
3850        refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
3851        have hypos : 0 < ‖y‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hy
3852        have hrp : 0 ≤ Real.exp (a * R) * ‖y‖ ^ (-a) :=
3853          mul_nonneg (Real.exp_pos _).le (Real.rpow_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) _)
3854        by_cases hcase : θ ≤ ‖y‖
3855        · -- away from the origin
3856          have h1 := hbd y hyB hy
3857          have h2 : ‖y‖ ^ (-((0 + (d:ℝ)) / 2)) ≤ θ ^ (-((0 + (d:ℝ)) / 2)) :=
3858            Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hθ0 hcase (by have : (0:ℝ) ≤ (0 + (d:ℝ))/2 := by positivity
3859                                                      linarith)
3860          have h3 : |periodicRiesz d 0 (boxPt y)| ≤ M := by
3861            refine h1.trans ?_
3862            rw [hM]
3863            have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h2 hC1
3864            linarith
3865          have h4 : Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 (boxPt y)|) ≤ Real.exp (a * M) :=
3866            Real.exp_le_exp.2 (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h3 ha0.le)
3867          linarith
3868        · -- near the origin
3869          replace hcase := not_le.1 hcase
3870          set x : Euc d := WithLp.toLp 2 y with hx
3871          have hxn : ‖x‖ < δ' := by
3872            have := norm_toLp_le_card_mul hd0 y
3873            have hdpos : (0:ℝ) < d := by positivity
3874            calc ‖x‖ ≤ d * ‖y‖ := this
3875              _ < d * θ := by gcongr
3876              _ = δ' := by rw [hθ]; field_simp
3877          have hx0 : x ≠ 0 := by
3878            intro h0
3879            apply hy
3880            ext i
3881            have := congrArg (fun z : Euc d => z i) h0
3882            simpa [hx] using this
3883          have hxpos : 0 < ‖x‖ := norm_pos_iff.2 hx0
3884          have hxδ : ‖x‖ < δ := lt_of_lt_of_le hxn (min_le_left _ _)
3885          have hx1 : ‖x‖ < 1 := lt_of_lt_of_le hxn (min_le_right _ _)
3886          have hg : periodicRiesz d 0 (boxPt y) = -Real.log ‖x‖ + r (Torus.mk x) := by
3887            have := hloc x hxδ hx0
3888            exact this
3889          have hrR : |r (Torus.mk x)| ≤ R := by
3890            have := hR x (by simpa using hx1.le)
3891            simpa [Torus.lift, Real.norm_eq_abs] using this
3892          have hlog : Real.log ‖x‖ < 0 := Real.log_neg hxpos hx1
3893          have habs : |periodicRiesz d 0 (boxPt y)| ≤ -Real.log ‖x‖ + R := by
3894            rw [hg]
3895            have := abs_le.1 hrR
3896            rw [abs_le]; constructor <;> linarith [this.1, this.2]
3897          have h4 : Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 (boxPt y)|) ≤
3898              Real.exp (a * R) * ‖x‖ ^ (-a) := by
3899            calc _ ≤ Real.exp (a * (-Real.log ‖x‖ + R)) :=
3900                  Real.exp_le_exp.2 (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left habs ha0.le)
3901              _ = Real.exp (a * R) * ‖x‖ ^ (-a) := by
3902                  rw [Real.rpow_def_of_pos hxpos, ← Real.exp_add]
3903                  congr 1; ring
3904          have h5 : ‖x‖ ^ (-a) ≤ ‖y‖ ^ (-a) :=
3905            Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_nonpos hypos (norm_le_norm_toLp y) (by linarith)
3906          have h6 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h5 (Real.exp_pos (a * R)).le
3907          have := Real.exp_pos (a * M)
3908          linarith
3909    _ = ∫⁻ y in Box d, ‖Real.exp (a * M) + Real.exp (a * R) * ‖y‖ ^ (-a)‖ₑ := by
3910        refine lintegral_congr fun y => ?_
3911        rw [Real.enorm_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
3912    _ < ∞ := hI.2
3913
3914end KernelAux2
3915
3916/-- `g * μ` is bounded and continuous for a bounded density `μ` (`0 ≤ s < d`). -/
3917theorem convMeasure_bounded (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (ρ : Torus d → ℝ)
3918    (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) :
3919    ∃ C, ∀ x, |convMeasure (periodicRiesz d s) (withDensityReal ρ) x| ≤ C :=
3920  KernelAux2.convMeasure_bounded' _ (integrable_periodicRiesz hd hs0 hsd) ρ Λ hΛ
3921
3922/-- Positive definiteness: `∬ g(x-y) dν(x) dν(y) ≥ 0` for finite signed measures with
3923zero mass having finite energy (expressed through Fourier coefficients). -/
3924theorem energy_nonneg_fourier (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d) (c : Lattice d → ℝ) :
3925    0 ≤ ∑' k : Lattice d, rieszCoeff d s k * c k ^ 2 := by
3926  refine tsum_nonneg fun k => ?_
3927  by_cases hk : k = 0
3928  · simp [rieszCoeff, hk]
3929  · exact mul_nonneg (rieszCoeff_pos hd hs0 hsd k hk).le (sq_nonneg _)
3930
3931/-- Lemma 4.24 `M:lem:periodic-log-row` (source lines 5572-5578): for `μ` with a bounded
3932density on `𝕋^d` and `a ∈ (0,d)`, the canonically centered periodic logarithmic kernel
3933satisfies `sup_x ∫ e^{a|g_μ(x,y)|} dμ(y) < ∞`.  Here the canonically centered kernel is
3934`g_μ(x,y) = g(x-y) - g*μ(x) - g*μ(y) + ∬ g dμ dμ`. -/
3935def centeredKernel {d : ℕ} (g : Torus d → ℝ) (μ : Measure (Torus d)) (x y : Torus d) : ℝ :=
3936  g (x - y) - convMeasure g μ x - convMeasure g μ y + energyPairing g μ μ
3937
3938theorem periodic_log_row (hd : 2 ≤ d) (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ)
3939    (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ) (a : ℝ) (ha0 : 0 < a) (had : a < d) :
3940    ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ x, ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal
3941      (Real.exp (a * |centeredKernel (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) x y|))
3942      ∂(withDensityReal ρ) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C := by
3943  have hd0 : 0 < d := by omega
3944  obtain ⟨C0, hC0⟩ := convMeasure_bounded (s := 0) hd le_rfl (by exact_mod_cast hd0) ρ hρ Λ hΛ
3945  set I : ℝ≥0∞ := ∫⁻ z : Torus d, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 z|)) with hI
3946  have hIt : I ≠ ∞ := (KernelAux2.lintegral_exp_abs_log_lt_top hd ha0 had).ne
3947  set E : ℝ := energyPairing (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) (withDensityReal ρ) with hE
3948  set K : ℝ := 2 * C0 + |E| with hK
3949  refine ⟨(ENNReal.ofReal Λ * (ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * K)) * I)).toReal, fun x => ?_⟩
3950  rw [ENNReal.ofReal_toReal (ENNReal.mul_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
3951    (ENNReal.mul_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top hIt))]
3952  have hpt : ∀ y, ENNReal.ofReal
3953      (Real.exp (a * |centeredKernel (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) x y|)) ≤
3954      ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * K)) *
3955        ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 (x - y)|)) := by
3956    intro y
3957    rw [← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (Real.exp_pos _).le]
3958    refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
3959    rw [← Real.exp_add]
3960    refine Real.exp_le_exp.2 ?_
3961    have h1 : |centeredKernel (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) x y| ≤
3962        |periodicRiesz d 0 (x - y)| + K := by
3963      unfold centeredKernel
3964      have hx := hC0 x
3965      have hy := hC0 y
3966      rw [← hE]
3967      calc _ ≤ |periodicRiesz d 0 (x - y) - convMeasure (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) x
3968              - convMeasure (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) y| + |E| := abs_add_le _ _
3969        _ ≤ |periodicRiesz d 0 (x - y) - convMeasure (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) x|
3970              + |convMeasure (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) y| + |E| := by
3971            gcongr; exact abs_sub _ _
3972        _ ≤ |periodicRiesz d 0 (x - y)| + |convMeasure (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) x|
3973              + |convMeasure (periodicRiesz d 0) (withDensityReal ρ) y| + |E| := by
3974            gcongr; exact abs_sub _ _
3975        _ ≤ _ := by rw [hK]; linarith
3976    nlinarith
3977  calc _ ≤ ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * K)) *
3978        ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 (x - y)|)) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) :=
3979        lintegral_mono hpt
3980    _ = ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * K)) *
3981        ∫⁻ y, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 (x - y)|)) ∂(withDensityReal ρ) :=
3982        lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
3983    _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * K)) * (ENNReal.ofReal Λ * I) := by
3984        gcongr
3985        exact KernelAux2.lintegral_conv_le ρ Λ hΛ (fun z => ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (a * |periodicRiesz d 0 z|))) x
3986    _ = _ := by ring
3987
3988
3989end KernelFacts2

3991/-! ### The response inverse (work package KERN-02) -/

3993section Response

3995variable {d : ℕ} {s : ℝ}

3997/-- General form of `exists_unique_response`: any integrable kernel with real nonnegative
3998Fourier coefficients. -/
3999theorem exists_unique_response_of_kernel {g : Torus d → ℝ} (hgi : Integrable g)
4000    (ĝ : Lattice d → ℝ) (hĝ : ∀ n, 0 ≤ ĝ n)
4001    (hcoef : ∀ n, Torus.funFourierCoeff (fun x => (g x : ℂ)) n = (ĝ n : ℂ))
4002    (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
4003    (β : ℝ) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : MemLp ψ 2 (withDensityReal ρ)) :
4004    ∃ h, IsResponse g (withDensityReal ρ) β ψ h ∧
4005      ∀ h', IsResponse g (withDensityReal ρ) β ψ h' → h' =ᵐ[withDensityReal ρ] h :=
4006  ResponseAux.exists_unique_respEq_general hgi ĝ hĝ
4007    (ResponseAux.hcoef_of_funFourierCoeff hcoef) hρ hΛ hβ hψ
4008
4009/-- General form of `susceptibilityCov_bounds`. -/
4010theorem susceptibilityCov_bounds_of_kernel {g : Torus d → ℝ} (hgi : Integrable g)
4011    (ĝ : Lattice d → ℝ) (hĝ : ∀ n, 0 ≤ ĝ n)
4012    (hcoef : ∀ n, Torus.funFourierCoeff (fun x => (g x : ℂ)) n = (ĝ n : ℂ))
4013    (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
4014    (β : ℝ) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) :
4015    ∃ c C : ℝ, 0 < c ∧ ∀ φ : Torus d → ℝ, MemLp φ 2 (withDensityReal ρ) →
4016      c * l2Inner (withDensityReal ρ) (projMean (withDensityReal ρ) φ)
4017          (projMean (withDensityReal ρ) φ) ≤
4018        susceptibilityCov g (withDensityReal ρ) β φ φ ∧
4019      susceptibilityCov g (withDensityReal ρ) β φ φ ≤
4020        C * l2Inner (withDensityReal ρ) (projMean (withDensityReal ρ) φ)
4021          (projMean (withDensityReal ρ) φ) := by
4022  obtain ⟨c, hc, H⟩ := ResponseAux.susc_bounds_general hgi ĝ hĝ
4023    (ResponseAux.hcoef_of_funFourierCoeff hcoef) hρ hΛ hβ
4024  refine ⟨c, 1, hc, fun φ hφ => ?_⟩
4025  have hex := exists_unique_response_of_kernel hgi ĝ hĝ hcoef ρ hρ Λ hΛ β hβ φ hφ
4026  have hex' : ∃ h, IsResponse g (withDensityReal ρ) β φ h :=
4027    let ⟨h, hh, _⟩ := hex; ⟨h, hh⟩
4028  have hr : IsResponse g (withDensityReal ρ) β φ (responseInverse g (withDensityReal ρ) β φ) := by
4029    have e : responseInverse g (withDensityReal ρ) β φ = hex'.choose := by
4030      unfold responseInverse
4031      simp only [hex', dite_true]
4032    rw [e]
4033    exact hex'.choose_spec
4034  obtain ⟨h1, h2⟩ := H φ _ hφ hr
4035  refine ⟨h1, ?_⟩
4036  rw [one_mul]
4037  exact h2
4038
4039/-- `K_μ` is a bounded nonnegative self-adjoint operator on `L²_0(μ)` for a bounded density
4040`μ` and `0 ≤ s < d` (positivity of `ĝ`); hence for every `β ≥ 0` and `ψ ∈ L²(μ)` the
4041response equation has a unique solution (KERN-02). -/
4042theorem exists_unique_response (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
4043    (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
4044    (β : ℝ) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) (ψ : Torus d → ℝ) (hψ : MemLp ψ 2 (withDensityReal ρ)) :
4045    ∃ h, IsResponse (periodicRiesz d s) (withDensityReal ρ) β ψ h ∧
4046      ∀ h', IsResponse (periodicRiesz d s) (withDensityReal ρ) β ψ h' →
4047        h' =ᵐ[withDensityReal ρ] h := by
4048  have h0 : ∀ n : Lattice d, 0 ≤ rieszCoeff d s n := fun n => by
4049    by_cases hn : n = 0
4050    · simp [rieszCoeff, hn]
4051    · exact (rieszCoeff_pos hd hs0 hsd n hn).le
4052  exact exists_unique_response_of_kernel (integral_periodicRiesz hd hs0 hsd).1 _ h0
4053    (periodicRiesz_fourierCoeff hd hs0 hsd) ρ hρ Λ hΛ β hβ ψ hψ
4054
4055/-- The susceptibility form is symmetric and nonnegative, bounded above and below by
4056multiples of `‖P_μ φ‖²_{L²(μ)}` (used in Proposition 2.4; KERN-02). -/
4057theorem susceptibilityCov_bounds (hd : 2 ≤ d) (hs0 : 0 ≤ s) (hsd : s < d)
4058    (ρ : Torus d → ℝ) (hρ : IsProbDensity ρ) (Λ : ℝ) (hΛ : ∀ x, ρ x ≤ Λ)
4059    (β : ℝ) (hβ : 0 ≤ β) :
4060    ∃ c C : ℝ, 0 < c ∧ ∀ φ : Torus d → ℝ, MemLp φ 2 (withDensityReal ρ) →
4061      c * l2Inner (withDensityReal ρ) (projMean (withDensityReal ρ) φ)
4062          (projMean (withDensityReal ρ) φ) ≤
4063        susceptibilityCov (periodicRiesz d s) (withDensityReal ρ) β φ φ ∧
4064      susceptibilityCov (periodicRiesz d s) (withDensityReal ρ) β φ φ ≤
4065        C * l2Inner (withDensityReal ρ) (projMean (withDensityReal ρ) φ)
4066          (projMean (withDensityReal ρ) φ) := by
4067  have h0 : ∀ n : Lattice d, 0 ≤ rieszCoeff d s n := fun n => by
4068    by_cases hn : n = 0
4069    · simp [rieszCoeff, hn]
4070    · exact (rieszCoeff_pos hd hs0 hsd n hn).le
4071  exact susceptibilityCov_bounds_of_kernel (integral_periodicRiesz hd hs0 hsd).1 _ h0
4072    (periodicRiesz_fourierCoeff hd hs0 hsd) ρ hρ Λ hΛ β hβ
4073
4074end Response
